# Fuel (Nutrition) — Feature Documentation

> The `/fuel` tab — meal pacing, supplement stack/protocol, pantry (Kamra), recipes, and a weekly fuel rhythm. **Status: 🟢 Phase-2 done (exit audit `mezo-t16y.4`, 2026-07-05)** — every Fuel surface is backend-backed (Pantry+Import, Recipes, Meal-logging+Scoring, Medication, Stack/Protocol, Water), deterministically composed from real reads (Mai timeline P5, Terv week P4), or an explicit honest ghost; the ONLY remaining theater is 🟣 P8 territory (`mezo-0h6w`): the Replan cascade + Stack-recommendation fixtures (real-mode ghosts) and the proactive daily-briefing PROSE (null until the P8 brain) — the meal-score prose itself is REAL (`MealCoachService`, mezo-mr4n, gated on `mezo.feature.meal-coach.enabled` + companion): `summary`/`tagline`/`improve[]` plus, since mezo-jcpt.1, a per-dimension `note`. The Pantry inventory (`usePantry`/`usePantryActions`, mezo-9xu) is real dual-mode CRUD against `/api/pantry`; **since Fuel P6 (mezo-bka) the `imports` feed + `suggestions` are REAL too** — an OpenFoodFacts lookup-import (`GET /api/pantry-import/lookup` + `POST /api/pantry-import`, `pantry_import` table; the project's FIRST outbound HTTP client, deterministic REST not AI) plus deterministic swap-suggestion heuristics, both riding the same `PantryResponse`. **Since the URL-scrape slice (mezo-8vum) the Kamra also imports by pasting a product URL** — `POST /api/pantry-import/scrape` fetches the page, an LLM extracts a full nutrition + category + NOVA + price draft (the project's **first LLM-backed feature endpoint**, gated on `mezo.feature.pantry-scrape.enabled`), and the user confirms it down the same P6 `POST /api/pantry-import` path; low-confidence (Atwater-inconsistent) drafts land as `manual-review`. The LLM sits behind a **consumer-owned `ScrapeLlm` port** (companion provides the adapter — ADR 0012), so `feature/pantry` never imports `feature/companion`. **Since the photo-import slice (mezo-d8tr) the Kamra also imports by photographing a nutrition label** — `POST /api/pantry-import/photo` (multipart: one label photo + an optional front-of-pack photo) runs a multimodal LLM call over the same consumer-owned-port idiom (`PhotoExtractLlm`, companion's multi-image `CompanionLlm` overload) to extract the draft, deterministic confidence scoring, and the `photo` provenance value on both `pantry_item.source` and `pantry_import.source`; the confirm carries an explicit `origin: 'photo'` marker (no URL) down the same `POST /api/pantry-import` path. **Recipes (`useRecipes`/`useRecipeActions`, mezo-lns)** are real dual-mode CRUD against `/api/recipe` — a `recipe` + `recipe_ingredient` aggregate whose lines reference `pantry_item`, with per-line `name`/`contribution` macros computed at read time. **Meal-logging (`useFuelDay`/`useMealActions`/`useRecipeLogs`, mezo-arb)** is real dual-mode against `/api/meal` + `/api/fuel/day/{date}` — a polymorphic `meal` + `meal_item` aggregate (items reference a `recipe` or a `pantry_item`, each with a per-line macro snapshot); **since mezo-u68c a logged meal carries a NAME** — the log surface (`LogMealSheet` then, `LogFlowPage` since `mezo-d20.4.2`) captures an editable name defaulted from the meal's lines via the shared pure `deriveMealName` helper (`features/fuel/logic/deriveMealName.ts`; recipe line → recipe name, pantry items → joined names, capped + "…"), so `save()` now sends a real `title` (was `null`), and the Mai timeline **de-blanks any title-less meal** by deriving that same display name from its `mealItems` in `buildDayPlan` (`SlotCard` then falls back `mealName || label`) — so meals logged before the fix still show a name, no backfill; the same de-blank rule is factored into the shared `mealDisplayName` helper and **also applied in the `MealScoreSheet` header** (title → derived name → `'Étkezés'`), so a title-less-but-scored pre-fix meal never opens a blank score sheet; **the meal score is REAL since Fuel P7 (mezo-yta, ADR 0006) and 8-dimensional since mezo-7797: a deterministic engine (`MealScoringService`, `feature/nutrition`) scores every meal at write (Macro .22 · Rost .10 · WHO .14 · Zsírminőség .10 · NOVA .18 · Növényi diverzitás .08 · Energia-sűrűség .06 · Context .12 → `meal.score` numeric + the typed `meal.breakdown` jsonb envelope) and computes the recipe `mezoFit.score` at read**, **and TRAINING-AWARE since mezo-ta8p: a logged meal is classified `standard`/`pre_workout`/`post_workout` from the day's workout schedule windows, and the pre/post roles overlay role-specific macro/WHO/NOVA targets (fast carbs = fuel; `standard` = the v0 rubric, zero regression — §9)** — the deterministic engine itself always emits `summary`/`improve[]`/dimension `note` as null (P8-shaped sockets on the wire), and `MealCoachService` (mezo-mr4n) fills them asynchronously post-write when companion is on; pre-P7 rows stay NULL behind the pending sparkle. **Since the AI meal-log slice (mezo-78rn) a meal can also be logged from free text and/or a photo** — `POST /api/meal/ai-draft` runs ONE cheap-tier LLM call that parses the food AND matches it against the owner's pantry+recipe catalog (matched lines take DB macros, unknown lines become FK-less `estimate` lines with LLM macros), returns an editable draft the user confirms down the SAME `POST /api/meal` path (so P7 scoring runs unchanged); it is the project's **second LLM-backed endpoint** (gated on `mezo.feature.meal-ai-log.enabled`), reaching the cheap tier — with a multimodal vision overload — via the same **consumer-owned `MealDraftLlm` port** idiom as scrape (companion provides the adapter — ADR 0012), and `meal.provenance jsonb` records how each meal originated (manual/ai-text/ai-photo). **Since the recipe AI-breakdown slice (mezo-bw3y) the recipe detail explains its fit**: `GET /api/recipe/{id}/breakdown` lazily materializes a **template breakdown** — the deterministic 8-dim template envelope (`MealScoringService.recipeTemplateBreakdown`, hero ≡ envelope by construction; the seven meal-shared dims — Macro · Rost · WHO · Zsírminőség · NOVA · Növényi diverzitás · Energia-sűrűség — plus **Adag-arány**, which replaces the meal-side Context since mezo-7797) enriched by the project's **third LLM-backed endpoint** with Hungarian prose (`summary`/per-dim `detail`/`improve[]`/`fitsFor[]`) over the consumer-owned `RecipeBreakdownLlm` port (ADR 0012, gated on `mezo.feature.recipe-ai-score.enabled`), cached in `recipe.breakdown` jsonb (+ `fits_for`) and invalidated on edit / numeric drift; prose-less degrade (flag/companion off, LLM error) still serves the deterministic envelope. **Since the recipe meal-role slice (mezo-uavr) that template surface is scored under the recipe's OWN declared role** — `recipe.role` (`standard`/`pre_workout`/`post_workout`, default `standard`, picked by the user in the editor, never derived from name/tags) selects the rubric via the exact same `mezo.fuel.scoring.roles` overlay the logged surface uses, now extracted into one shared `MealScoringService.rubricFor(MealRole)` so the two surfaces cannot drift; `standard` is the identity overlay ⇒ every pre-existing recipe keeps byte-for-byte its old numbers. The role **retargets** `macro`/`who`/`nova` (the other five dims, `Adag-arány` included, stay role-independent) and the prose prompt carries it, so the text stops scolding deliberate pre-workout sugar underneath a number that already counts it as fuel. **The logged-meal surface is untouched** — a logged meal still derives its role from the day's training windows (mezo-ta8p), because a pre-workout recipe eaten on a rest day was not a pre-workout meal. The owner's prod pantry is seeded with a **147-item imported catalog** (`seed/pantry-catalog.json` → idempotent `@Profile("demodata")` `PantryCatalogLoader`, mezo-zza); that slice also extended `pantry_item` with preserved nutrition facts (`fiber_g/sugar_g/salt_g/saturated_fat_g`), a **`category` enum** (18 values: vegetables…supplement/other) and additional **`source`** vendors (lidl/nutriversum/herbahaz/nutrifit/decathlon). **Since the meal-slot-templates slice (mezo-7102, PR 1) the day planner also accepts a user-authored override**: a per-day-type (`rest`/`training_am`/`training_pm`) `meal_slot_template` — mixed fixed/relative anchors + percent-of-budget rows — overlays `buildDayPlan`'s `placeWindows`/`splitBudget` (`GET/PUT/DELETE /api/fuel/slot-templates[/{dayType}]`, ungated like the protocol endpoints; edited on the new full-page `/fuel/slots`, §2); absent a template the engine is byte-identical to before. **Since PR 2 of the same slice the editor also carries a Tier-2 qualitative read** — `POST /api/fuel/slot-templates/evaluate` on a **new own tag `FuelSlotPlan`** (the project's **fourth LLM-backed endpoint**, gated `mezo.feature.slot-template-ai.enabled` independently of the ungated CRUD) runs ONE stateless cheap-tier call over the consumer-owned **`SlotPlanLlm`** port (companion adapter `SlotPlanLlmAdapter`, ADR 0012, two-switch-gated on `slot-template-ai` **and** companion) that judges the current draft against the goal balance + training placement and returns a strict-JSON Hungarian `{verdict: ok|adjust, summary, suggestions[]}`; nothing is persisted, the **"Mezo értékelése"** button never blocks saving, and a companion-off/parse-failure degrades to an honest 503 `FUEL_SLOT_TEMPLATE_LLM_UNAVAILABLE` the FE renders as a plain degrade note (§4).

## 1. Summary

Fuel is mezo's nutrition domain: five sub-views under the bottom-nav route `/fuel` covering today's meal/supplement **pacing** (`"Mai"`), the weekly **plan/rhythm** (`"Terv"`), an AI supplement-**protocol** builder (`"Stack"`), a **recipe** library (`"Receptek"`), and a **pantry/shelf** (`"Kamra"`). It exists to turn training + medication context (a generic dose-cycle domain — the owner currently tracks no active medication, §2/§4) into appetite-aware, time-boxed nutrition guidance.

**Status per layer:**
- **FE mock:** ✅ complete — all five views, all sheets return static mock data synchronously; the Fuel hooks in `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts` are mock-only **except** the Pantry, Recipes and Meal-logging hooks (`usePantry`/`usePantryActions`, `useRecipes`/`useRecipeActions`, `useFuelDay`/`useMealActions`/`useRecipeLogs`), which are dual-mode (mock returns the static seed synchronously via `initialData`).
- **FE real:** 🔶 **Pantry + Recipes + Meal-logging + Medication + Stack/Protocol** — `usePantry`/`usePantryActions` (`data/fuel/pantryApi.ts`, `/api/pantry`) — **since S4 (`mezo-qw37.4`) backed by the shared `pantry_catalog` + per-user `pantry_item` split; details, catalog search and the author/OWNER edit gate live in [`pantry.md`](pantry.md)**, `useRecipes`/`useRecipeActions` (`data/fuel/recipeApi.ts`, `/api/recipe`, mezo-lns), `useFuelDay`/`useMealActions`/`useWaterActions`/`useRecipeLogs` (`data/fuel/mealApi.ts`, `/api/fuel/day/{date}` + `/api/meal` + `/api/water-log` — the last is the `+víz` quick-log, mezo-0z5), `useMedication`/`useMedicationActions` (`data/fuel/medicationApi.ts`, `/api/medication`, mezo-d94) and **`useStack`/`useProtocol` + `useStackActions`/`useProtocolActions`** (`data/fuel/fuelApi.ts` + `data/fuel/stackHooks.ts`, `/api/fuel/protocol` + `/api/fuel/intake`, mezo-09g) all branch on `isMockMode()` and hit a real API in real mode (the `useWeight`/`useDualQuery` dual-mode pattern). The **Mai timeline** is **unified in both modes** — **`useFuelTimeline`** (`data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts`, Fuel P5 mezo-9ys; static seed retired mezo-53su): the hand-authored `fuelPlan.today` seed is gone, and **both modes compose** the live day via the pure `buildDayPlan` (`features/fuel/logic/buildDayPlan.ts`) — the **wake/bed anchor from the sleep goal** (`useSleepGoal`, `mezo-dbsr`) + `mealsPerDay` **& `caffeineCutoff` from Fuel settings** (`useFuelSettings`, mezo-53su) + a **dynamic, activity-aware daily budget** (`BMR×NEAT + Σ MET activity + goal balance`, BMR-floored — mezo-1oy5; §5), the day's logged meals + recipe suggestions, the supplement protocol — **since mezo-vx9v Task 9, the living-protocol occurrences projected by `projectStackDay`, not the retired `buildProtocol()`** — + intakes, and today's gym/sport/run blocks. Since **mezo-1oy5 the timeline is a FIXED plan** — windows sit at their wake/training/bed anchors and `now` only paints each unlogged meal window's STATE (never re-flows the schedule), so past-unlogged meals surface as faded, still-loggable `missed` cards. ALL composed hooks are called unconditionally and only the injected `nowHHmm` branches on `isMockMode()` (mock pins `MOCK_NOW_HHMM='13:30'`). **The Terv weekly plan is dual-mode too — `useFuelWeek` moved to `data/fuel/fuelWeekHooks.ts` (Fuel P4, mezo-kpo):** mock serves the `fuelWeek.ts` seeds unchanged; real composes the live week (gym days from `useTrain()`'s derived schedule, volleyball from Train's sport schedule, the medication cycle strip from `useMedication().cycle.week`, weekly stats from the new `GET /api/fuel/week/{start}` rollup) and returns honest-empty for the surfaces with no real source yet (`patterns`/`weeklySupplements` `[]`, `weeklyNote`/`supplementsAdherence` null); gym times are **read-only** here (set in Train — the mesocycle planner + the Gym "Időpontok" chip, `mezo-4t43`). Only `useReplanScenarios` still has no real path (mock-only const spread in `data/fuel/fuelReadHooks.ts`). Its sibling mock-only recommendation-seed reader, `useStackRecommendations`, was **retired** in mezo-vx9v Task 8 — the reworked Stack page has no recommendation-card concept left to feed.
- **Backend:** 🔶 **Pantry + Recipes + Meal-logging + Medication + Stack/Protocol** — `backend/.../feature/pantry` (`pantry_catalog` shared definitions + `pantry_item` per-user state since S4 `mezo-qw37.4` — see [`pantry.md`](pantry.md); `pantry.yml`, mezo-9xu), `backend/.../feature/recipe` (`recipe` + `recipe_ingredient`, `recipe.yml`, mezo-lns) — see [`recipe.md`](recipe.md), `backend/.../feature/meal` (`meal` + `meal_item` + **`water_log`**, `meal.yml`, `/api/fuel/day/{date}` + `/api/meal` + `/api/recipe/{id}/logs` + `POST/DELETE /api/water-log` — `FuelDayService.consumed.water` is now a real per-day Σ, no longer a fake `= targets.water` 100% ring, mezo-arb/mezo-0z5; **since mezo-najo the day/week `targets` kcal + protein come from the ACTIVE GOAL's prescribed recept** — the `GoalPrescriptionJson` segment covering that date's goal-week (week derived from `goal.startDate`), so the MacroHero, the chat snapshot's `[Mai üzemanyag]` and `get_fuel_log` all say the goal-engine number (TDEE + training + deficit) instead of the static config; `mezo.nutrition.*` is the per-field FALLBACK (no active/evaluated goal or no covering segment); since Diet Plan slice 1 (mezo-xwgb) `c`/`f` also come from that segment's prescribed `carbsG`/`fatG` when present (config fallback for a pre-split/no-covering segment); `water` still never comes from the recept — it is always the saved diet-preference row's `waterMl` (or its config ghost) via `DietPreferencesResolver`), `backend/.../feature/medication` (`medication` + `medication_dose`, `medication.yml`, `/api/medication` + `…/{id}/dose`, **derived cycle** via `MedicationCycleService`, mezo-d94) and **`backend/.../feature/fuel`** (the first Fuel-owned package: `protocol` + `protocol_item` + `supplement_intake` tables, `ProtocolService`/`IntakeService`, `FuelController` implementing the new generated `FuelApi`; `fuel.yml`, `/api/fuel/protocol` + `/api/fuel/intake`, mezo-09g — **since mezo-vx9v the protocol is a single living row of occurrences, not a versioned selection snapshot: occurrence CRUD endpoints `POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/fuel/protocol/items[/{id}]` (the old whole-selection `activate` retired), zone placement decided by `PlacementEngine`'s rule table → pantry-timing → `StackPlacementLlm` fallback chain (gated `mezo.feature.stack-placement-llm.enabled`, consumer-owned port ADR 0012) — see §4, ADR [0017](../decisions/0017-living-occurrence-protocol.md)**) are all live. The rest of Fuel backend is ❌ none; `train`/`biometrics`/`auth`/`pantry`/`recipe`/`meal`/`medication`/`fuel` are the backend features so far. **The pantry feature also carries the first LLM-backed pipeline (mezo-8vum):** `WebPageClient` (browser-like fetch — never follows redirects, streaming size cap, SSRF guard) → `HtmlNutritionStripper` (jsoup, a NEW dependency) → `ScrapeExtractionService` (strict-JSON prompt over the consumer-owned `ScrapeLlm` port) → `ScrapeDraftValidator` (deterministic confidence), orchestrated by `PantryScrapeService` behind the gated `PantryScrapeController` — see §4/§5 and ADR 0012. Since Fuel P7 the promised pattern is delivered: **`backend/.../feature/nutrition`** (the scoring core slice — `MealScoringService` + the full `MealBreakdownJson` envelope + `MealScoringProperties`/`NutritionTargetsProperties` configs) computes the deterministic meal score at write and the recipe fit at read; `meal.score numeric` (migration `202607051000_mezo-yta`) denormalizes the envelope's `value` (ADR 0006 §4). Since **mezo-53su** the fuel feature also owns a **`fuel_settings`** per-user singleton (`GET/PUT /api/fuel/settings`, tag `FuelSettings`, gated `mezo.feature.fuel-settings.enabled`, config-ghost 4/"14:00") for the eating cadence + caffeine cutoff, plus the **ungated `CaffeineCutoffPort`/`CaffeineCutoffResolver`** the habit `no_stim_after` metric reads (see §4/§5).

Driving design: **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md)** (Slice C · Fuel: `food_item`/`meal`/`meal_item`/`recipe`/`supplement_intake`/`medication(_dose)`/`nutrition_targets` + a fuel-timeline **view**, wiring these same nine hooks). Fuel is sequenced **after** Train deliberately because Train de-risks the typed-jsonb provenance-envelope pattern (`@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)`) that Fuel's meal `score` will reuse. Roadmap: **[`docs/milestones/roadmap.md`](../milestones/roadmap.md)**.

## 2. User-facing behavior

`/fuel` is one of the **five first-class tabs** of the Design 2.0 shell (Nap · Edzés · Fuel · Mezo · Én — [ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md)). **The Fuel section shell is dissolved and DELETED (F8, `mezo-d20.9.1`):** `FuelSection.tsx`, `pages/tabs.ts`/`FUEL_TABS`, the shared `AppHero` identity header and the `SubNavDropdown` sub-nav are gone. `/fuel` now renders **`FuelMaiPage` directly — the tab's Mozaik hub** — and every former sub-tab is a **full-page sibling** on its own stable route, reached by tapping a hub tile (the Huawei-Health „tile → own page” idiom of **Mozaik 2.0**, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md), which supersedes the Napív iOS-list language of [ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-today-ios-list-language.md)). This is a **view-layer swap only**: the routes, the composed day (`useFuelTimeline`/`buildDayPlan`), every data hook and every contract survived; the settings sheet alone was later promoted to its own page (`mezo-2xzf`) — the redesign is *recompose, not reinvent*. There is no nested shell and no section-level navigation dropdown left in Fuel; the ⚙️ **Fuel-beállítások** action the old dropdown owned re-homed onto the hub as its own band, and the sub-pages own their headers (`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero` on the F3-refaced ones, the older `.pghead-np` header on the ones whose deep design round is still F7.3 pending — §9). Quick logging is the shell's floating coral **`QuickLogFab`** (`frontend/src/app/QuickLogFab.tsx`), present on every screen; its `QuickInputSheet` „Étkezés” tile is **dynamic** — with an open eating window it routes straight into `/fuel/log/uj?w=<tileKey>`, with none it lands on `/fuel/log/uj`'s free-item branch (mezo-7lst) — but it only ever resolves against **today's** window, so the hub's own window lane must still end with a log door itself (below), the only way to log against a past day. Routes are declared in `frontend/src/app/router.tsx`.

| Route | Reached from | View | What the user does |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/fuel` (index) | the Fuel tab | `FuelMaiPage` | **The hub Mozaik** — keret-hero + Logolás hero tile + 6 tiles |
| `/fuel/log` | hub Logolás hero tile | `FuelLogPage` | **Napi logolás** — stacked window blocks (mezo-byo1); **since mezo-1j3z a day stepper** (‹ › + a date pill, max 7 days back) and a `?d=YYYY-MM-DD` deep link put the page on a past day (§2, §9 RESOLVED); **since mezo-bq2t every block CTA NAVIGATES to `/fuel/log/uj`** instead of expanding a composer in place (§2, §9 RESOLVED) |
| `/fuel/log/uj` | a `/fuel/log` block CTA | `FuelLogNewPage` | **The logolás saját oldala** (mezo-bq2t) — the full-screen `MealComposer` on its own route; the context lives in the URL: `?d=YYYY-MM-DD` (a past day, clamped to the same 7-day window — anything outside it or unparsable falls back to today), `?w=<ablak-kulcs>` (the tapped window's `${time}-${label}` key; unknown or absent → ablakon-kívüli logolás with the visible MIKOR segment), `?ai=1` (AI-panel armed on mount); Mentés/Mégse both `navigate` back to `/fuel/log` on the same day (§2) |
| `/fuel/plan` | hub tile „Terv” | `FuelPlanPage` | Weekly rhythm |
| `/fuel/stack` | hub tile „Stack” | `FuelStackPage` | Next-action hub — next dose, progress, compact rhythm and four focused doors |
| `/fuel/stack/protocol` | Stack tile „Teljes protokoll” | `FuelStackProtocolPage` | Read the living protocol, placement and reasons |
| `/fuel/stack/today` | Stack tile „Mai nap” | `FuelStackTodayPage` | Read the complete timed day and intake state |
| `/fuel/stack/meals` | Stack tile „Étkezések” | `FuelStackMealsPage` | Read meal bindings, verdicts and suggestions |
| `/fuel/stack/manage` | Stack tile „Protokoll kezelése” | `FuelStackManagePage` | Management hub with four focused tasks |
| `/fuel/stack/manage/protocol` | management tile „Protokoll” | `FuelStackManageProtocolPage` | Edit occurrences and doses with immediate autosave |
| `/fuel/stack/manage/timing` | management tile „Időzítés” | `FuelStackManageTimingPage` | Move occurrence zones with immediate autosave |
| `/fuel/stack/manage/meals` | management tile „Étkezési kapcsolatok” | `FuelStackManageMealsPage` | Honest read-only explanation of meal relationships |
| `/fuel/stack/manage/add` | management tile „Új elem” | `FuelStackAddPage` | Add a pantry item as a new protocol occurrence |
| `/fuel/recipes` | hub tile „Receptek” | `FuelRecipesPage` | Recipe library |
| `/fuel/recipes/new` | Receptek header `＋ Új` | `RecipeEditorPage` | Create a recipe (create = edit) |
| `/fuel/recipes/:id` | a recipe card | `RecipeDetailPage` | Recipe detail |
| `/fuel/recipes/:id/edit` | detail → Szerkesztés | `RecipeEditorPage` | Edit a recipe |
| `/fuel/recipes/muhely[?recipeId=]` | Receptek header „✨ Műhely” · detail „✨ Iterálás a Műhelyben” | `RecipeWorkshopPage` | Receptműhely — AI recept-builder chat (mezo-92pb) |
| `/fuel/kamra` | hub tile „Kamra” | `FuelKamraPage` | Pantry / shelf |
| `/fuel/kamra/:id` | a Kamra card | `KamraItemDetailPage` | Pantry item detail (full page) |
| `/fuel/gyogyszer` | hub tile „Gyógyszer” | `FuelMedicationPage` | Generic medication cycle + dose log; full lifecycle from the UI since F7.3 (create/edit/stop, §2) |
| `/fuel/naplo` | hub tile „Napló” | `FuelNaploPage` | Week-centric fuel log / AI-score trend (F3.6) |
| `/fuel/settings` | hub band „Fuel-beállítások” | `FuelSettingsPage` | Napi ritmus, makróprofil, célok és finomhangolás önálló Mozaik oldalon (`mezo-2xzf`) |
| `/fuel/slots` | `FuelSettingsPage` → „Étkezési ablakok” | `FuelSlotsPage` | Meal-slot template editor — day-type recommended/custom split (mezo-7102) |

**Every route above is a flat sibling now** — the `fuel` group has no `children` and renders no chrome of its own, so the distinction the old doc drew between „sub-tab” and „full page” dissolved with the shell: `RecipeEditorPage`, `RecipeDetailPage`, `KamraItemDetailPage`, `FuelSettingsPage` and `FuelSlotsPage` are now structurally identical to `FuelPlanPage` or `FuelStackPage`. `new` is still listed before `:id` for clarity (React Router ranks static over dynamic regardless). `/fuel/slots` remains reachable only from `FuelSettingsPage`'s „Étkezési ablakok szerkesztése ›” row. Existing Fuel routes were not renamed; `/fuel/settings` is additive, and the `LegacyPathRedirect` entries in `router.tsx` cover the two tabs that DID move (`/today/*` → `/nap`, `/insights/*` → `/mezo`).

**`/fuel/slots` (`FuelSlotsPage`, mezo-7102 — spec [`2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md)):** the meal-slot-template editor. A sage `SegButton` tablist (`useStickyTab('fuel.slots.dayType','rest')`) switches between the three canonical **day types** (Pihenőnap / Reggeli edzés / Esti edzés, `SlotTemplateDayType`). With no saved template for the selected type the page shows a read-only **recommended** preview — today's real training blocks (when today's own `resolveDayType` matches the selected type) or a synthetic single-block reference day, run through the UNCHANGED `placeWindows`/`splitBudget` — plus a **"Testreszabás"** CTA that forks it into an editable draft (`seedRowsFromRecommendation`, `FuelSlotsPage.tsx:131-145`: each row's `budgetPct` seeds from the recommended plan's kcal share, Σ-normalized to 100, drift absorbed into the largest slot — the `splitBudget`/`splitBudgetPct` dinner-absorbs idiom generalized). Once a template exists (saved or forked), the page is a live editor: per-row **név** (free text) / **típus** chips (the `MealSlot` enum, keeping log filling + `LogFlowPage`'s `initialSlot` + the API contract unchanged) / **szerep** chips (`ROLE_OPTIONS` reuse) / **horgony** (fix `HH:mm`, or a ±15-stepped signed offset against wake/training-start/training-end/bed) / **budget %** + törlés, a **Σ budget%** pill, a live `compileTemplate`+`splitBudgetPct` preview, and the Tier-1 **`validateSlotPlan`** guardrails inline (errors — red, block `Mentés`; warnings — amber, advisory only). **"Ajánlott visszaállítása"** deletes the saved template (`useSlotTemplateActions().deleteTemplate`) and reverts to the recommendation. A portaled `.recipe-save-bar` (Mégse/Mentés) mirrors `RecipeEditorPage`'s save-bar idiom. A cold real-mode load's `useSlotTemplates()` starts pending (`templates=[]`), so the editor re-syncs `rows`/`forked` off the resolved template once the GET settles rather than stranding an empty draft (`FuelSlotsPage.tsx:169-181`). **Six mezo-7102-review polish fixes (mezo-4ghd):** the same real-mode cold-mount race is now ALSO closed at the source — **`Testreszabás` is disabled while `useSlotTemplates().isPending`** (mock mode resolves synchronously, so it's never disabled there) — so a fork can no longer start before a late-arriving saved template lands and get silently overwritten on `Mentés`; **switching the day-type tab now keeps a per-day-type draft** (`{rows, forked}`, stashed only while `forked` — `FuelSlotsPage.tsx`'s render-time day-type-switch block) so an unsaved fork/edit on one day type survives a switch away and restores on switch-back, while a day type only ever viewed read-only still falls through untouched to the existing-template late-arrival sync above; `validateSlotPlan` gained a tier-1 **`label_length`** error (an empty/whitespace-only label, or one over the wire's `maxLength: 40`) and the **`Slot neve`** input hard-caps at 40 chars; **Budget %** commits as a clamped integer (`[1,100]`, parseInt semantics — "12.5" commits 12) and **Eltolás perc** clamps to the wire's `[-720, 720]`; the error/warning lists key off `` `${code}-${i}` `` so a repeated code (e.g. two `gap` or `pre_workout_big` hits) no longer collides; and the slot-row `.zcard`s zero out their inline left/right margin so they align with the page's 16px container padding instead of double-insetting. The Tier-2 LLM **"Mezo értékelése"** button (mezo-7102 PR 2 — `useSlotTemplateEvaluation` → `slotTemplateApi.evaluate`, `POST /api/fuel/slot-templates/evaluate`, the `SlotPlanLlm` port) sits just under the Tier-1 guardrails — disabled while a save-blocking error exists or a call is already in flight — and on tap shows a twinkle **"Mezo értékeli a felosztást…"** while pending, then either a verdict card (sage `"rendben"` / amber `"érdemes igazítani"` chip + the Hungarian `summary` + per-slot `suggestions` rows) or, on a 503/405 degrade, the honest note **"Az AI-értékelés most nem elérhető — a determinisztikus ellenőrzés él."** The read is evaluate-on-demand and self-clearing — any edit to `rows` or a day-type switch invalidates the last result (`rowsAtEval` render-time reset, mirroring `trackedDayType` above) — and it NEVER gates `Mentés`, which stays governed by the Tier-1 `errors` alone (§4).

**„Mai” → the Fuel HUB (`FuelMaiPage`, Design 2.0 F3.1 · `mezo-d20.4.1`, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)):** the **fifth** recomposition of this one page, and the first that is not an *Island* at all. The window-river sky of [ADR 0023](../decisions/0023-fuel-window-river.md) and the keret-hero sky of [ADR 0024](../decisions/0024-fuel-keret-hero.md) are retired as a LAYOUT — `.sky-islands`, `WindowIsland`, `DoneWindowsCapsule`, `EmptyDayIsland`, the `?w=` selection URL state and the standing `.mai-logrow` are all deleted (F8, `mezo-d20.9.1`) — but everything they were built on survived verbatim: `useFuelTimeline`, `buildDayPlan`, `pickHeroWindow`, `deriveDailyBudget`, `buildEnergyBreakdown` and `KeretHero` itself are untouched. Anatomy top→bottom: the app shell's **`AppHeader`** (section label + clay spot · daypart switch · Mezo-messages circle · notification bell with an unread badge and a 3-row peek menu · profile orb → `/me`; mounted once by `AppLayout`, identical on every hub since `mezo-atry` — [today.md](today.md#the-header-is-the-shells-not-the-hubs) — `FuelMaiPage` itself renders none of it); the **`KeretHero`** band (§ below); the **Logolás hero tile** (`FuelLogHeroTile`, mezo-byo1 — the swimlane's successor, § below); the **6-tile mosaic** (Terv · Stack · Receptek · Kamra · Gyógyszer · Napló — `Mosaic`/`Tile` from `shared/ui/mozaik`, domain washes white/sage/coral/gold/lav/sky, one clay icon each; every tile's `line` is honest — a line the composition has no number for simply does not render, e.g. an empty pantry shows no count and a cycle-less medication tile shows nothing); and the **Fuel-beállítások band**. The whole body is wrapped in an **`EntranceGroup`** with per-child `--d` delays (0 / 70 / 160–360 / 400 ms) — a one-shot rise choreography on mount, then stillness, switched off wholesale under reduced motion (`shared/ui/mozaik/motion.tsx`). The **Mezo band** is RETIRED (mezo-04lo, 2026-09-01 — unused in practice): the counter band, its `/fuel/uzenetek` page (`FuelMezoPage`), the `fuelMezoMessages` filter and the route are all deleted; the companion's Fuel-context voice reaches the user via the shell header's Mezo circle (`/nap/uzenetek`) like every other context. Because the hub is an ordinary scrolling page, the whole `sky-flow`/viewport-fill class of layout bug (`mezo-gllr`, §9) is structurally gone.

**The window river is now a HERO TILE + ITS OWN PAGE (mezo-byo1 — the swimlane dissolved).** The horizontal `WindowLane` swimlane proved hard to scan (user verdict in the 2026-08-31 brainstorm) and is DELETED; `logic/fuelSwimlane.ts` survives as the pure VM under BOTH of its successors. On the hub, **`components/FuelLogHeroTile.tsx`** renders ONE live, full-width coral door under the KeretHero: a `now` window leads with `„LOGOLÁS · MOST”` (pulsing dot) + `{label} · {time}` + the honest plan line (`„a tervből: …”` only when the window is not a `ghost`); with no open window it points at the next `future` one; an all-done day flips the wash to sage (`„Minden ablak kész ✓”`); an empty day names the gap (`„nincs mai terv — tervezz és logolj”`). Below: a per-window dot strip (sage=done · coral pulse=now · amber=missed) and the `{done}/{n} ablak kész[ · {m} pótolható]` status line — the pótolható count only when real. Tapping it navigates to **`/fuel/log` (`pages/FuelLogPage.tsx`)**, a routed `MozaikPage` (coral tone, `‹ Fuel` chip, live hero: today's kcal `/ target` + the window counter) whose body stacks every window VERTICALLY as a **`components/WindowBlock.tsx`** — a wide, state-washed card carrying the same clay icon, stamp (`KÉSZ ✓`/`MOST`/`KIMARADT`), best-known name, honest meta, kcal cell (vanishes rather than printing 0) and the three mini macro rings. **Since mezo-bq2t `Logold`/`Pótold`/`✨ AI` NAVIGATE to `/fuel/log/uj` (`pages/FuelLogNewPage.tsx`) instead of expanding the `MealComposer` in place** — the earlier grid-rows 0fr→1fr well, `WindowBlock`'s `open`/`children` props and the `.flog-composer` CSS are all gone. The tapped tile's key (`` `${slot.time}-${slot.label}` ``) and the AI intent travel as `?w=`/`?ai=1` query params, so the new page can re-derive the same tile — `fixedSlot = tile.slotKey` (the mezo-bnsf contract, still enforced by construction; the MIKOR segment still never renders for a window-anchored log) plus the window's `suggestedRecipeId` prefilled on the non-AI path. The day stepper mirrors its result into `/fuel/log`'s own `?d=` as well (`replace`, so stepping never piles up history), which is what makes the browser/PWA back gesture land on the day the user left — a query-less `/fuel/log` history entry would silently re-open on today. Scroll position is NOT preserved on the way back: the route remounts and the app carries no scroll restoration. The full-screen page's header names the window and the day it books to (gold Pótlás tone + a note on a past day), and its save bar sticks to the bottom of the page above the tab bar; the quick-log FAB is hidden on this route (`AppLayout.tsx`), same as on `/mezo/chat`. Mentés/Mégse both `navigate` back to `/fuel/log` on the same day (`replace`, so back doesn't reopen the closed composer), and the page re-derives live from there. The list always ends with the trailing **`Ablakon kívül`** block (dashed, `＋ Logolás`/`✨ AI`, navigating with NO `w` — the mezo-66te standing door, never fabricating a window), and an empty day leads with the `Üres nap` block (`＋ tervezz` → `/fuel/plan`). State is never re-derived from a clock: `slot.state` from `buildDayPlan` stays authoritative (`pending` → `future`), exactly as the lane had it.

**Since mezo-1j3z the page carries a day stepper** — `‹`/`›` chips flank a date pill (`aug 30.`, lowercase weekday, a trailing `„ · ma"` only on today) that steps the composed day back up to **7 days**, and a `?d=YYYY-MM-DD` query param deep-links straight to one (out-of-range or malformed values clamp to today). On a past day the page reflows as **Pótlás, not Napló**: `MozaikPage` tone flips to gold, the eyebrow reads `„Pótlás"`, an honest note explains the day is closed to new plan changes, and the block list runs through the new pure `asPastDayLane(vm)` (`logic/fuelSwimlane.ts`) — a `now`/`future` tile has no meaning on a day that already happened, so both normalize to `missed` and `nowKey` is forced `null`. The save CTA reads `„✓ Pótlás · {dátum}."` instead of the today wording, an all-done past day shows the sage day-closed card, and a past **empty** day hides the `＋ tervezz` CTA (there is nothing to plan retroactively). See §9 (RESOLVED) for what this replaces.

**`components/KeretHero.tsx` survives the redesign essentially intact** — it was already a bare halo-sage band rather than an `Island`, which is exactly why it transplanted onto a Mozaik hub without a rewrite. It sits directly under the shell's `AppHeader` (there is no `AppHero` above it any more). Anatomy top→bottom: a large Geist-200 **count-up of the day's consumed kcal** (2s ease-out; `prefers-reduced-motion` → the instant final value, via a local rAF fork of `shared/ui/CountUp`'s hook), a **segmented day-bar** (`.khero-dayseg`, one segment per done meal window + a gold now-marker), **three signed energy chips** (`Alap`/`Mozgás`/`Cél`, `Cél` explicitly `−`/`+`; each opens its own `EnergyBreakdownSheet` section, and all three **vanish entirely on a `staticEnergy` day** — with no biometric profile the breakdown would be meaningless), and **five progress rings** (Fehérje/Szénhidrát/Zsír/**Rost**/**Víz**; SVG `stroke-dashoffset`, filled by a pure CSS transition one frame after mount, not per-frame JS). The **víz ring is a real `button`** opening `sheets/WaterLogSheet.tsx` (250/400/500 ml chips or a manual ml input; manual overrides the chip). **The hub v3 declutter (fuel iterations §2) removed the `„eddig X / Y · n/m ablak”` of-line** — the frame is told by the chips and the day-bar, and the lane says how many windows are done. The pure VM is unchanged: `logic/keretHero.ts`'s `buildKeretHero({ budget, staticEnergy, consumed, meals, water, slots, nowHHmm })` — ring percents (shared `pct()`), day-bar segments (all sharing `budget.kcal` as the denominator with a ghost remainder, so an overshoot day cannot blow the bar past 100%), the now-marker fraction (placed on the span of the day's own meal windows, absent when every window is done or only one exists) and the signed chip values. **Rost** is real in both modes since `mezo-m6uv` (Σ `FuelMeal.fiberG`); its target is still the static `FIBER_TARGET_G = 30` in `data/fuel/fuelConfig.ts` — no settings field yet.

**Done windows no longer merge into a capsule — a done window is just a done TILE.** `DoneWindowsCapsule` is deleted (F8, `mezo-d20.9.1`) and the AI meal-score re-entry it existed for (`mezo-cs8b`) rides the /fuel/log window blocks instead: `buildWindowLane` joins each done slot's `mealId` against `fuel.meals` for the real macros and the score, and the tile's chip reads **`✨ {n} p`** (a `.is-mid` tone under 90, never red — guardrail: honest, never punitive) or, on a freshly logged and not-yet-scored meal, the honest **`✨ folyamatban`** rather than a fabricated number. The chip is a **button only when the meal actually carries a `breakdown`** — `MealScoreSheet` renders `null` without one, so a breakdown-less chip stays inert text instead of a dead tap — and tapping it opens `MealScoreSheet` unchanged. **What did NOT survive the merge: the per-meal `MealRole` tag** (`EDZÉS ELŐTTI`/`EDZÉS UTÁNI`/`STANDARD`) the capsule showed, and the day's `AI-átlag` summary line — though `aiAverage` still feeds the hub's Napló tile line. `keretHero.ts`'s `deriveMealRole`/`doneMealRows` and `windowIslands.ts`'s `doneGroup` remain in the tree, still table-tested, with **no production consumer**; see §9's deferred list. Nothing was re-derived to fake them.

**Logolás 2.1 (mezo-zeeq, 2026-09-01 — spec [`2026-09-01-fuel-logolas-2.1-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-fuel-logolas-2.1-design.md), prototype `docs/design_2.0/prototypes/fuel-logolas-2.1.html`): the /fuel/log page grew the hub's hero and a re-cut done card.** (1) **The page hero IS the hub's `KeretHero`** — the kcal bignum + `/ cél kcal` line is gone; `FuelLogPage` builds the very same `buildKeretHero({budget, staticEnergy, consumed, meals, water, slots, nowHHmm})` VM `FuelMaiPage` does (so the kcal denominator is the FE `budget`, one dynamic budget across both heroes), passes the new optional **`ofLine`** prop (`{done}/{n} ablak kész · {x} kcal még belefér` / `… fölötte`; the empty-day notes ride the same line) — a deliberate divergence from the hub v3 declutter, which still passes no `ofLine` and renders no `.khero-of` — and wires the chips to `EnergyBreakdownSheet` (guarded on `energyBreakdown`) and the víz ring to `WaterLogSheet` via `useWaterActions(date)`. **On a past day the hero runs through `asPastDayHero`** (pure, `logic/keretHero.ts`, the `asPastDayLane` shape): chips and the now-marker are dropped because `useFuelTimeline(date)`'s energy and wall-clock describe TODAY; consumed kcal, segments, rings and water read the date's own data and stay. The `.flog-khero .khero` rule drops the halo-sage band (the page tone already washes the top). (2) **The done card** (`WindowBlock`): top row `time · LABEL · [context chip] · KÉSZ ✓`, then icon + name (15px), then **ONE row of the AI-score pill and the kcal** (`.flog-srow`; kcal 24px, no cell frame), then **four 44px SVG macro rings — P · C · F · R** (`.fh-wring`, stroke-dashoffset sweep flipped one frame after mount, the KeretHero ring recipe; the conic-gradient `MiniRing` + `useCountUp` are retired), each with the grams and its percent. **Since `mezo-tjua` a LOGGED window's P/C/F rings read the MEAL's own energy split, not the day's keret** — `logic/macroSplit.ts` (pure, Atwater-weighted 4/4/9 kcal/g, largest-remainder so the three add to exactly 100) re-bases a done tile's three rings, `TileRingVM` gained `basis: 'day' | 'meal'`, and the caption drops the „napi” word (`30%` vs `17% napi`); the Rost ring is not part of the energy split so it stays a share of `FIBER_TARGET_G`, and a done meal with no macro energy at all keeps the day-basis rings rather than printing a fabricated split. Planned windows (now/missed/future) keep the share-of-target reading — a keret question is still the useful one before you eat. The same slice retired the keret framing from `MacroPanel` (the per-macro „cél” sublines and the „Kcal a napi X%-a” note; `MacroDimension.macroTargets`/`kcalShareOfDay` stay on the wire contract, simply unrendered). **The Rost ring exists only on a done tile whose meal carries `fiberG`** (`TileRingVM.key` widened to `'p'|'c'|'f'|'r'`; `FuelSlot` has no fiber, so planned windows keep three). **The score pill** (`.fh-aisc`, a button only when `scorable`): 30px ring in the tone colour + 19px number + the tone word + `›` — the tone ladder is `logic/scoreTone.ts` (`jó` ≥ 80 · `közepes` ≥ 60 · `gyenge`), the one rule `MealScoreChip`, the pill and the sheet hero share; it **nudges every 2.6s** (`fh-aisc-nudge` + a tone halo, the `fh-lt-nowpulse` cadence — the user's explicit ask; NN/g + WCAG 2.2.2 flag a perpetual loop, the bounded variant is a follow-up issue) and stops on `:active` and under reduced motion. Unscored stays the honest `✨ folyamatban`. (3) **The context chip** (`.fh-ctx`: `Standard` grey · `Pre-workout` amber · `Post-workout` lavender) is **the role the meal was SCORED under**, read back by `logic/mealContext.ts`'s `mealContextOf(meal)` from the breakdown's context dimension `Szerep` row (`Pre-workout…` / `Post-workout…` prefix; scored without the row → standard; no breakdown → `null`, no chip) — the ONLY wire trace of the backend's `MealScoringService.classifyRole` (the enum is neither persisted nor sent). It is deliberately NOT re-derived on the FE from `plan.workout` (`deriveMealRole`'s 90/120-from-start disagrees with the server's 120/90-from-end rule), so the card and the sheet's context dimension never contradict. `WindowTileVM.context` carries it; `MealScoreSheet`'s header shows the same chip. (4) **The score sheet body** (`ScoreBreakdownBody`, shared with `RecipeScoreSheet` — the change lands on the recipe Pontszám sheet too, by design): `ScoreHero` is a 112px tone-washed ring with the tone word, fact chips (`kcal · P · C · F · Rost`, Rost only with `fiberG`) and the confidence bar; the `Súlyozott bontás` eyebrow became **`Miből áll össze a {score}` · `{n} dimenzió · súlyozva`**; the new **`ScoreLedger`** (segment width = weight, fill = sub-score, Σ = the score) sits above **collapsible `DimensionCard`s** (button header: 52px ring + label + `súly W% → X pont` + the `detail` prose clamped to two lines; expanded: the full detail + the per-id panel; collapsed by default); **`Lehetne jobb` items are cards with the gain in a sage box** (`logic/formatImpact.ts`: `+0.04 score` → `+4 pont`, any other impact text verbatim); **the `Hogyan számoltam` tool list is removed from the body** (`ToolChipRow` keeps its other consumers). The prototype's `Felhúzta / Lehúzta` pair was NOT built — the wire carries one `detail` string per dimension and the pair would be fabrication. (5) **The meal-timing strip** (`MealTimingStrip.tsx`, `mezo-jcpt.3`) — a Stephen Few bullet-graph on the `context` `DimensionCard` (a fixed 0–24h axis, same scale on every meal — Tufte small-multiples — with the eating **window** as a filled band and the **`eatenAt`** instant as one marker; out-of-window draws a coral dot + a dashed bridge to the nearer window edge instead of just floating in space; a snack's `null` window draws a faint full-width band). The window comes over the wire as `MealTimingDetail` (`eatenAt`/`windowFrom`/`windowTo`/`slotLabel`, `api/feature/meal/meal.yml`), populated from the **exact same** `MealScoringProperties.SlotWindows` config that produced the `context` dimension's `timingSub` score (`MealScoringService.windowOf`, shared by both) — the drawn band and the score can never disagree. It renders **only** for a logged meal's `context` dimension (`DimensionCard.tsx`: `dim.id === 'context' && 'timing' in dim && dim.timing != null`) — the recipe/template score sheet never carries a `context` dimension at all (§2/§9 above), so `RecipeScoreSheet` never shows the strip even though it shares `ScoreBreakdownBody`; an old cached score envelope from before `mezo-jcpt.3` simply has no `timing` field and renders nothing, never a fabricated one. The strip itself is `aria-hidden` — the existing „Időzítés" fact-chip row is the one textual source of truth (WCAG: color is never the only signal).

**`/fuel/settings` is the Fuel-beállítások own page** (`FuelSettingsPage`, `mezo-2xzf`; approved design [`2026-09-04-fuel-settings-page-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-fuel-settings-page-design.md)). The hub's `.fh-band` navigates here directly; the old settings drawer and its dialog state are deleted. The Huawei/Mozaik composition leads with a live sage **Napi ritmus** arc, then groups the unchanged controls into floating cards: meals/day and caffeine cutoff, a native **Makróprofil** dropdown, water/fiber goals, protein tier and training-day kcal shift. The macro preview is honest: `fuel.targets` supplies the active kcal/P/C/F grams, `logic/fuelSettingsPreview.ts` derives deterministic whole percentages summing to 100, and a changed diet draft shows **„Mentés után frissül”** instead of projecting unsaved targets. Fuel and diet drafts keep separate touched flags and late-arrival resync effects, so editing one domain never freezes an untouched response from the other. The portaled coral Mentés bar remains above the tab bar; success returns to `/fuel`, while „Étkezési ablakok” is the only in-app entry to `/fuel/slots`.

**Deleted in the F8 cleanup (`mezo-d20.9.1`), component + test, grep-verified zero-consumer:** `components/WindowIsland.tsx` and `components/DoneWindowsCapsule.tsx`; `FuelMaiPage`'s local `EmptyDayIsland`, the `.mai-logrow` standing row and the `?w=` island-selection URL state; the whole `FuelSection.tsx` + `pages/tabs.ts` shell pair; and — on the log side — `sheets/LogMealSheet.tsx` + `sheets/AiLogSheet.tsx`, merged into `pages/LogFlowPage.tsx` (below). Still retired from the earlier waves and NOT resurrected: `KeretBelt`, `NowWindowCard`, `DayZoneCard`/`ZoneSlotRow`, `DayBudgetCard`, `MissedStrip`, `.medcycle-micro`, `MacroHero`. **`logic/windowIslands.ts` itself was NOT deleted** — the pure `buildWindowRiver` module and its table tests are still in the tree with no production caller (§9). **No `FuelMaiSkeleton`** — the hub still has no loading branch, because every composed hook seeds synchronously in both modes. `useMealCoach`/`MealScoreChip`/`MealScoreSheet`/`getScoredMeal` stay reachable, now through the lane's done-tile score chip.

**Honest-null facts (spec §8), carried over verbatim:** `FuelSlot` still carries no per-window `mezoFit` score, and `fuelSwimlane.ts` resolves a score ONLY for a done window's logged meal — so an open window's fit and the old `Nap-score eddig` fact cell stay **permanently absent** rather than fabricated. The swimlane simply has no place for them any more, which is the honest resolution of what the island's `isGhostMeal`/`buildFactCells` guards used to suppress. **Real loss to record, not paper over:** a window's stack doses are gone from the hub. The island's L1 carried an `Ehhez az ablakhoz kötve` row per `matchMealsToStack` verdict whose zone matched that window (deep-linking to `/fuel/stack`, because a `MealMatchVerdict` carries no `pantryItemId`/occurrence id and therefore no honest in-place „tick”). With the L1 gone so is the row: `FuelMaiPage` still composes `useStackDay()`, but only to count the day's remaining doses for the **Stack tile's line** — it no longer calls `matchMealsToStack` at all, which now feeds only `FuelStackPage`'s own `StackMealMatch`. Recorded as a deferred regression against ADR 0024's surface in §9.

**`"Terv"` (`FuelPlanPage`, dual-mode since Fuel P4 · mezo-kpo):** weekly stats card — kcal avg + protein-hit days derive from the real 7-day rollup in real mode (kcal avg over days with any logged kcal, `—` when none; protein-hit = days with `consumed.p ≥ targets.p`), stack adherence renders an honest `—` (null until P8), gym+sport count from the rendered schedule; a `MedicationWeekStrip` (7-day medication cycle — real mode maps `useMedication().cycle.week`, and the whole card **hides when no cycle exists**, which — since the owner tracks no medication — is always, §2; `currentDay` + the `D{n} · ma` badge from the derived `useTodayScenario().medCycleDay`, mezo-d94); the **`WeekRhythmGrid`** — a 24h-axis week grid merging gym sessions + volleyball per day (real mode: Train's derived gym week — meso WHAT × slot WHEN, `DEFAULT_BLOCK_MIN` as the presentational duration — and Train's sport-schedule sessions) with coffee-cutoff (14:00) and kitchen-close markers; recurring `PatternRow`s and the `WeeklySupplementGrid` + stats-card coach note are **mock-only demo surfaces — hidden in real mode** (honest-empty `[]`/null; pattern-engine + protocol-map + prose are P8/proactive work). Gym times are **read-only** here (`mezo-4t43`): the schedule is set in the mesocycle planner (Step 2) + the Gym-page "Időpontok" chip; Fuel renders Train's derived week (day+time from the standing slots joined onto the meso's gym days) and feeds the Mai timeline. The old in-page `GymScheduleSheet` editor + write-through were removed. Title is the mock `"Máj 18 – 24"` in mock mode, date-derived (`deriveWeekTitle(mondayIso())`, e.g. `"Jún 29 – Júl 5"`) in real mode.

**`"Stack"` (`FuelStackPage`, UI-only Stack 3.0 redesign, `mezo-ubxd`; spec [`2026-09-04-fuel-stack-redesign-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-fuel-stack-redesign-design.md)):** `/fuel/stack` is now a next-action hub directly below the shell-owned `AppHeader` — it renders no local page head, fake weekday hero, or second app header. The first sage card names the next due occurrence, time and placement reason, exposes a 44×44 intake control, and shows honest day progress; ticking it writes through `useStackIntakeToggle`, emits a success toast with **Visszavonás**, and that action deletes the exact intake id returned by the completed log mutation (not a later pantry/zone match). Below it, `StackRhythmPreview` gives a compact read-only day arc and four colorful clay-icon tiles open four focused pages: `/fuel/stack/protocol` (the whole living protocol and reasons), `/fuel/stack/today` (the complete timed day), `/fuel/stack/meals` (meal bindings and suggestions), and `/fuel/stack/manage` (the write boundary). The management hub fans into `/fuel/stack/manage/protocol`, `/timing`, `/meals`, and `/add`; protocol/timing pages mutate the existing occurrence CRUD immediately, meals stays an honest read-only explanation, and add uses the pantry-backed occurrence add flow. There is deliberately no fake global Save: every real write autosaves and global mutation errors remain the error surface. All eight child routes use `StackPageScaffold`, the shared `.stk-*` visual family and the project clay sprite set; `StackItemSheet`, `StackDayArc` and `StackMealMatch` remain live. The earlier all-in-one next/zone/picker component composition is removed.

**`"Receptek"` (`FuelRecipesPage`):** the **editorial library redesign** (`docs/design/recipes-library.html`, mezo-lns). The view reads real `recipes` from the dual-mode `useRecipes()`, renders directly into the app-shell `.screen-content`, and shows a **segmented typebar** (`Mind / Reggeli / Ebéd / Vacsi / ★` with live per-filter counts, the Kamra typebar pattern) — the old search + filter-chip row is gone. **Own header since Napiv S6 Task 6 (`mezo-8141`):** `.pghead-np.sage` (`over` = `"Fuel · Receptek"`, `h1` = `"Receptek"`) replacing the legacy `.page-header`/`PageTitle`; the header sub still shows real counts (`"{n} recept · {s} csillagos"`); the fake **`"Avg fit 0.89"`** stat strip is removed. `RecipeCard` is now an **editorial card**: a 118px image band (diagonal-stripe gradient placeholder), a slot tag + bookmark star top-left — **plus, since mezo-uavr, a role tag** (`roleLabel(recipe.role)`, rendered only when `role !== 'standard'`; it borrows the slot tag's typography but stays a plain `.chip` with `--coral-deep` text, so it is not a second brand chip) which is what makes a lifted-or-lowered badge number explainable in the list — and the `RecipeFitBadge` top-right (v1 `mezoFit.score` is `null` → the P2 pending sparkle "Mezo"; since P7 the real read-time deterministic fit renders in that same slot — the sparkle remains only for profiles with no kcal at all); **the Antonio name now renders on the card surface below the band (`var(--ink)`), not overlaid on the media** (de-darkening, `mezo-8141`: the old `--text-on-media` token is retired) — followed by the **`/adag`-basis `MacroCells` strip** (`recipe.macros ÷ servings`, whole numbers, `perLabel="/adag"` on the left rail — switched from the whole-recipe total since mezo-m6uv: the whole-recipe number was misleading on a multi-serving recipe, and it now matches the detail hero's + `MealPickerSheet`'s default basis) + a meta line (`N hozzávaló · {prep+cook} perc · NOVA {n}`). No tápérték strip on the card itself (kept dense — see `RecipeDetailPage` below). The card button carries an `aria-label` of the recipe name. Tapping a card navigates to **`/fuel/recipes/:id`** (the `RecipeDetailPage` page); the header's **`pgact-np` `＋ Új`** chip navigates to **`/fuel/recipes/new`** (the `RecipeEditorPage` page). Those two routed full pages shipped in Phase 7c, **retiring the old `RecipeDetailSheet` / `NewRecipeSheet` overlays** (files + tests deleted). The nested **`IngredientPickerSheet`** (still a modal, opened from the editor) shows per-row **`MacroCells` cards** (`docs/design/recipes-editor.html`): name + a **kind badge** (`Étel/Kieg./Stim./Gyógyszer`) + `SourceBadge` + brand/NOVA subline + a `＋` add button, category-accented left border. Its list is the **unified pickable set** — foods **and** the supplement/stim/med stash (`usePickableIngredients`, mezo-3vu4), so protein powder etc. are recipe inputs too — and it **stays open on pick (multi-add)**, flipping an already-added row to a disabled `✓` "Hozzáadva" (dedup).

**`RecipeDetailPage` (`/fuel/recipes/:id`) — F7.3 MOSAIC face (`mezo-d20.8.3.1`, prototype `fuel-mely.html`, spec `2026-08-31-fuel-mely-kor.md` §B), superseding the two-tab `mezo-n3xa` layout below.** The page now reads: `MozaikPage(coral)` → `PageHead` (‹ Receptek + a `Szerkesztés` pgact) → the hero card (warm gradient image band + slot chip + ★ + `RecipeFitBadge`; name/meta on the card surface, meta line unchanged incl. the `mezo-uavr` role chip) → Makró eyebrow + `ServingToggle` + `mz-statstrip` (kcal · fehérje · szénhidrát · zsír, basis-scaled) → a **2×2 `mz-mosaic`**: **Pontszám** (sage; `ScoreRing` + `{n} szempont · megbízh. {c}%` + the top-2 dimensions; disabled until the breakdown resolves, the twinkle copy is `Mezo értékeli…`/`Mezo újraértékeli…` and the `mezo-uavr` honesty gates hold — a refresh hides the stale reading; tap → **`RecipeScoreSheet`**, whose body is the SAME `ScoreBreakdownBody` the `MealScoreSheet` renders — one surface, two callers — with the role-rubric line in its header) · **Mezo · olvasat** (lav; the `summary` prose clamped + the first `fitsFor` chip, static) · **Hozzávalók** (gold; count + names; tap → a **LOCAL sliding view**, not a route — the prep tile-page precedent: per-line category-bordered cards with `SourceBadge`, amount, per-line `MacroCells` + `NutrientCells`, and the **recipe-level `NutrientCells` moved here** above the lines, still on the shared basis toggle) · **Logok** (sky; count + latest; tap → a small sheet hosting `RecipeLogsList`) → actions (`+ Mai étkezéshez` CTA + Csillag/Törlés ghosts — Szerkesztés moved to the head). All data/behavior below is unchanged. — Historical anatomy (two-tab redesign `mezo-n3xa` — spec + approved mockup `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-recipe-detail-tabs-*`; original page design `docs/design/recipes-detail.html`): the full-page detail. (Since the shell dissolved, „no sub-nav chrome” is simply what every Fuel route is — see §2's opener.) **Shared header:** an editorial **hero** — a 150px diagonal-stripe image band (slot chip + bookmark, `RecipeFitBadge size="hero"` top-right) with the name + meta line moved OFF the band onto the card surface below it (`var(--ink)`/`var(--faint)` — de-darkening, `mezo-8141`); since `mezo-n3xa` the meta line reads `servings adag · totalMins perc · NOVA {n} · létrehozva {createdDate}` (NOVA colored via `NOVA_COLOR` — the old 4-cell meta strip is **deleted**, its only non-redundant datum was NOVA), **and since mezo-uavr a `--coral-deep` role chip is appended to that same line for a non-standard recipe** (`roleLabel(recipe.role)` → „Edzés előtt" / „Edzés után"; a `standard` recipe renders nothing at all — the default must stay invisible so the chip means something) → a **macro hero** with the `ServingToggle` (1 adag ↔ egész; values rounded per basis) **followed by a `NutrientCells` row since mezo-m6uv** (`size="md"`, `empty="hide"` — telített/cukor/rost/só, following the SAME `ServingToggle` basis as the macro hero, `nutrientsByBasis` scaling `recipe.nutrients` the same way the macros scale). **Then two in-page tabs** (the GrowthPage `SegButton` tablist pattern — `role="tablist"`/`role="tab"`, local `useState`, NOT routed; active wash `var(--wash-gym)` + `var(--coral-deep)`): **„Részletek" (default)** — the **Mezo · sablon-olvasat + PONTSZÁM** sections (mezo-bw3y): `useRecipeBreakdown(id)` serves the template breakdown; while the real-mode FIRST open generates (LLM seconds) a twinkle "Mezo értékeli a receptet…" card shows; **since mezo-uavr the SECOND-and-later generate is visible too** — the hook also returns `refreshing` (`!mock && isFetching && !isPending && isInvalidated`) and the same twinkle card takes over with „Mezo újraértékeli a receptet…", suppressing the stale olvasat + dimension cards rather than passing a pre-edit reading off as current (the `isInvalidated` gate is load-bearing: a mere `staleTime`/focus revalidation refetches the SAME envelope, and announcing a re-evaluation there would be a false claim plus a layout jump); with prose, the olvasat card renders `summary` + `fitsFor` chips (hidden while summary is null); the PONTSZÁM mono section header (`{n} szempont · megbízh. {c}%`) — **since mezo-uavr followed, for a non-standard role, by its own `label-mono` line naming the yardstick: `{roleRubricLabel(role)} mérce szerint`** („edzés előtti mérce szerint"), so the number self-explains — is followed by **`ScoreBreakdownBody`** (dimension cards + "Lehetne jobb" + "Hogyan számoltam" — shared with `MealScoreSheet`, pixel-identical; since mezo-7797 the template view carries the **Adag-arány** (portion) card in place of the meal-side Context card — timing is only scored on logged meals); a kcal-less recipe gets an honest "nincs elég adat" card — then a **"LOGOK"** section (**`RecipeLogsList` ← `useRecipeLogs(id)`**, mezo-cki: today's logs of this recipe — a scored card with score + delta-vs-baseline when a log carries a score — **real since P7**: the log's meal score arrives on `RecipeLogResponse.score` and the component computes the delta against the live `baselineScore` (= `recipe.mezoFit.score`); the pending-sparkle chip remains for pre-P7 score-less rows, empty-state card when never logged); **„Hozzávalók · N"** — the ingredient list (each row: category-accented left border, snapshot `name`, `SourceBadge` + optional note, the `amount{unit}`, a per-line `MacroCells` of that line's `contribution`, **and since mezo-m6uv a per-line `NutrientCells` (`empty="dashes"`) of that line's `nutrients`** — a fact-less line renders four `—`, which is what makes a partial rollup on the hero self-explanatory; the count renders on the tab label, no in-tab section header). **Below the tab content on both tabs** (normal flow, not sticky): live **`+ Mai étkezéshez`** (mezo-arb — opens **`LogFlowPage`** pre-filled `{ source: 'recipe', recipeId: recipe.id }`, slot-less so the flow falls back to the wall-clock `defaultMealSlot()`; the `· hamarosan` disabled state is gone), live **Csillag** (toggles `starred` via `update(id, {...recipeToInput(r), starred:!r.starred})`), live **Szerkesztés** (→ `/fuel/recipes/:id/edit`), live **Törlés** (`remove(id)` + `navigate('/fuel/recipes')`). The route guard relies on `useRecipes().recipes` (no raw query status): mock resolves synchronously via `initialData`, real mode briefly shows the **`"Nincs ilyen recept."`** fallback on a cold deep-link until the list resolves. `RecipeDetailPage` also exports **`recipeToInput(r: Recipe): RecipeInput`** (prefills every field — **`role` included since mezo-uavr**, re-keys each line `refId → pantryItemId`) — reused by the star toggle and the editor, which is exactly why the field cannot be omitted: `RecipeInput` is a full replace, so a `role`-less input would silently reset a pre-workout recipe to Általános the next time the user merely starred it (pinned by a test).

**`RecipeEditorPage` (`/fuel/recipes/new` + `/fuel/recipes/:id/edit`, create = edit, `docs/design/recipes-editor.html`):** one full-page form for both create (no `:id`) and edit (`:id` present → prefilled via `recipeToInput`). **Header-only re-skin since Napiv S6 Task 6 (`mezo-8141`):** the back button (`aria-label="Vissza"`, unchanged behavior) sits on its own row above a `.pghead-np.sage` block (`over` = `"Fuel · Receptek"`, `h1` = the current title content — the typed `name`, or the `"—"` placeholder); the earlier `Eyebrow`-based "Recept szerkesztése"/"Új recept" mode label is gone. Captures **név**, **slot** (segmented Reggeli/Ebéd/Vacsora/Snack), **szerep** (a second segmented row under a `SZEREP` label-mono caption since mezo-uavr — Általános / Edzés előtt / Edzés után from `ROLE_OPTIONS`, seeded `editing?.role ?? 'standard'`, written straight into `RecipeInput.role`; this is the ONLY way the role is ever set — it is never inferred from the name or the tags, because a score must not hinge on a typo), a **csillag** toggle, **adag** + **elő+főzés** steppers, **címkék** (add via Enter / remove chips), and **hozzávalók** (picked rows: category-accented border, name+brand, a per-row **typeable gram amount** (a numeric input accepting decimals like `12.5`, flanked by ±10 steppers — `AmountField`, mezo-2567) + delete, and a live `MacroCells` of the line `contribution` at its amount). A live **MAKRÓ-ÖSSZEG** card carries the `ServingToggle` (default 1 adag) with a footer echoing the other basis. A **save bar** — portaled into `.phone-screen` (like `Sheet`) so it floats just above the tab bar instead of scrolling with / clipping the ingredient list, the old in-scroller `position:absolute` footgun (mezo-3vu4) — (`Mégse` → `navigate(-1)`; `Mentés` disabled until `name` + ≥1 line) calls `create(input)` (→ `/fuel/recipes`) or `update(id, {...recipeToInput(editing), ...input})` (→ the detail page). The **contribution = `round(macro * amount/per)`** (per/macros from the **`usePickableIngredients()`-resolved** pantry item — foods **+** the supplement stash, the picker's live source, NOT the static recipe seed; default per 1) — **identical** to `recipeMacros.lineContribution` and the backend `RecipeMapper`. `RecipeInput.ingredients` lines carry **`pantryItemId`** (the boundary contract name; the editor maps each draft `refId → pantryItemId` on save). The edit-mode no-match guard shows the same `"Nincs ilyen recept."` fallback.

**`RecipeWorkshopPage` (`/fuel/recipes/muhely[?recipeId=]`) — Receptműhely, the AI recept-builder chat (mezo-92pb, approved prototype `docs/design_2.0/prototypes/receptmuhely.html`, spec [`2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md)).** Entry points: the **`"Receptek"`** header's **`✨ Műhely`** chip (blank canvas) and `RecipeDetailPage`'s **`✨ Iterálás a Műhelyben`** ghost CTA (seeds `?recipeId=` — the existing recipe's `slot`/`tags`/`starred`/`prepMins`/`cookMins`/`role` are carried through verbatim as `baseMeta` so a save is never a silent full-replace, and a save on that session `update()`s the recipe instead of creating a second copy). **Vászon-first**: the page itself IS the live recipe card — editable name + a goal chip row (the five `WorkshopGoal` presets below, tap = both retarget the canvas AND send a preset instruction to the chat), a deterministic macro card (`WorkshopMacroCard`, per-adag/egész via the shared `ServingToggle` basis), a **serving stepper** (`scaleServings` — proportional rescale of every line's amount, clamped 1..12, rounded to the nearest 5 g/unit; an estimate line's frozen totals rescale alongside it from its own before/after amount ratio, since there is no live pantry row to recompute an estimate from), and the ingredient list (`WorkshopIngredientRow`) — **pantry lines** (macros resolved live against the pantry, same `lineContribution` formula the editor/backend use — an honest dash, never a fabricated zero, when the referenced item can't be resolved) vs **`✨ BECSLÉS` estimate lines** (the LLM's own totals for the stated amount, frozen — the stable base a later rescale multiplies from). A **`WorkshopChatDock`** docks at the bottom: free text + **5 preset chips** (`High protein` / `Pre-workout` / `Post-workout` / `Lefekvés előtt` / `Reggeli` — `GOAL_LABEL`/`GOAL_MESSAGE`, each chip both sets `goal` and sends its own Hungarian instruction, e.g. „Alakítsd át edzés előtti étkezésnek.”); an in-flight turn shows a busy state, and a 502/503 degrade renders an **F7.5 error bubble** with a retry affordance (`ERROR_COPY` — „A Műhely most nem elérhető — az üzeneted megvan.” — the composed text is preserved, not lost). **Patch semantics, not replace:** every turn's response is the model's FULL updated draft (so the LLM never has to describe a delta), but `diffLineKeys` (`data/fuel/workshopState.ts`) diffs it against the PREVIOUS draft by `lineKey` (a pantry ref, or a name-derived key for estimate lines) and gold-flashes (2.6 s, `FLASH_MS`) only the lines that are new or changed amount — an untouched line, including one the user hand-edited, is visually undisturbed even though the wire payload re-sent it. **Save gate:** `draftToInput` (`workshopState.ts`) returns `null` while ANY line is still `source: 'estimate'` — `Mentés` stays disabled and each estimate row itself offers the way out (`IngredientPickerSheet` swap to a real pantry item, or delete), with an honest gate note (`GATE_NOTE` — „✨ becslés-sorok: cseréld kamra-itemre vagy töröld a mentéshez”). A clean save maps 1:1 onto `RecipeInput` (`pantryItemId ← refId`) and writes **`role = goalRole(goal)`** — `pre_workout`/`post_workout` retarget the scoring rubric 1:1 with those two goal presets, every other goal (or none) saves `standard` — **unless** the session was seeded from an existing recipe with no goal chip tapped, in which case the seeded `baseMeta.role` is preserved untouched (an untouched workshop session must never silently retarget a saved recipe's rubric, mirroring the `recipeToInput` role-preservation rule elsewhere in Fuel — §9). All state is **LOCAL, not persisted** — a workshop session lives only until save; a fresh mount starts blank. **Mock mode** (`data/fuel/workshopMock.ts`) serves scripted pure rounds after a 600 ms demo delay, matching the real endpoint's turn-in/turn-out shape.

**`"Kamra"` (`FuelKamraPage`):** the pantry, **redesigned to Direction A** (`docs/design/kamra-mockup-v3-A.html`, mezo-9xu) with a **detail/edit/filter UX iteration** (`docs/design/kamra-detail-edit-v1.html`, mezo-4ag). `buildKamraItems(ingredients, stash)` produces a unified `PantryItem[]` (food + supplements + stimulants + medication). The primary axis is a **type segmented switcher** (`Mind/Étel/Supp/Stim` with live counts). Below it a **compact filter bar** = the search input + a **`⚙ Szűrők`** button carrying a badge with the active category-filter count; tapping it opens **`CategoryFilterSheet`** (a `Sheet` bottom-sheet listing only the categories **present** in the current items, each with a count, as multi-select chamfer chips + a clear + an apply showing the resulting tally). Applied categories render as **removable chamfer pills** above the list; the list filter **ANDs** type AND selected categories AND search (`matches(it, cats)` in `FuelKamraPage`). The 18 categories are deliberately **not** inline. Items render in **type-grouped sections** (a colored square nub divider per kind) as lighter design-system **meal-card** `KamraCard`s (Antonio name · source/brand · `P/C/F · NOVA` · right kcal/dose; supplement/stim get a left inset tint; **padding 16px, name→source 7px / source→macro 9px breathing room** per mezo-4ag). A stats strip (total / food) sits above; an **empty-state** CTA shows when the pantry is empty (real mode starts empty). **Stock/inventory is hidden** behind `SHOW_PANTRY_STOCK = false` (`frontend/src/data/_client/flags.ts`, mezo-6nu) — deferred for now: the `KamraCard` 44px qty slot + `lejár`/`⚠ fogy`, the `Lejár`/`Fogy` stat cells, and the expiry needs-attention strip only render when the flag is on. The backend `pantry_item` stock columns are untouched (mezo-dh6 partial-merge keeps existing values), so flipping the flag restores both data and UI. The view renders directly into the app-shell `.screen-content`. The page-header **`＋ Új tétel`** chip opens **`AddPantryItemSheet`** (manual add). **Import + suggestions are REAL since Fuel P6 (mezo-bka):** the page header carries an **`⌕ Import`** chip next to `＋ Új tétel` that opens the reworked **`ImportItemSheet`** (OpenFoodFacts lookup wizard — see below), and two honest sections render under the item list **only when non-empty**: **„Mezo javaslatok"** (`SuggestionCard`s — the deterministic cheaper-alt / low-NOVA swap heuristics computed server-side per read) and **„Legutóbbi importok"** (the `pantry_import` feed rows: `SourceBadge` + item name + humanized `when`; a `manual-review` row gets an „ellenőrzés" tag). The `SuggestionCard` "Polcra" CTA renders **only when an `onAdd` is wired** — swap suggestions reference items already on the shelf, so v1 shows no false affordance.

Tapping a card now **navigates to `/fuel/kamra/:id`** — the item detail is a full **page** (`KamraItemDetailPage`), not a bottom-sheet drawer. This kills the old "drawer-in-drawer" edit problem (the editor drawer used to open over a detail drawer). The page reads `usePantry()` + `buildKamraItems(...)`, finds the item by `useParams().id` (a small "nincs ilyen tétel" + back if absent), and lays out: back (‹) + eyebrow → source pill → big Antonio name → category label (from `categoryMeta`) · NOVA → **Makrók** (4 `.card` cells: kcal/P/C/F) → **Tápanyag** (4 cells: Rost/Cukor/Tel.zsír/Só from `item.fiberG/sugarG/saturatedFatG/saltG`, `—` when null; section rendered only when the item has macros) → **Ár** (the **Készlet** stock cell is hidden behind `SHOW_PANTRY_STOCK`, mezo-6nu; supplements still show **Dózis**, price kept — section head becomes `Dózis · ár` / `Ár`). **Since mezo-1za9 supplements carry macros/nutrients/price too** (the backend `PantryMapper.toSupplementResponse` now populates them, `macros` null only when `kcal` is unset), so a protein powder shows the same **Makrók** + **Tápanyag** + price blocks as a food item, and a `KamraCard` renders its P/C/F + kcal instead of the dose/protocol line; pure dose/protocol items (no kcal — many vitamins/stim/med) are unchanged → actions: live **`＋ Logolás · mai étkezésbe`** (mezo-arb — opens **`LogFlowPage`** pre-filled `{ source: 'pantry', pantryItemId: backendId }`; the disabled state is gone), **`✎ Szerkesztés`** (opens `AddPantryItemSheet` in edit mode, hosted by the page), **`Törlés`** (`deleteItem(backendId)` then `navigate('/fuel/kamra')`). The `'stash-'` id-prefix strip to the `backendId` + the `inputFromItem(item)` edit-input derivation **moved here** from the retired `IngredientDetailSheet` (deleted). **`AddPantryItemSheet`** (real `Sheet` shell) is the full add/edit form (mezo-4ag): sectioned **Alap** (Típus/Kategória/Forrás selects + Név — the **Adag/basis field is GONE since mezo-0gjr**: the macro basis is not an input) / **Makrók · /100 g** (kcal/P/C/F) / **Tápanyag** (Rost/Cukor/Tel.zsír/Só → `fiberG/sugarG/saturatedFatG/saltG`) / **Ár** (price; the stock qty/unit input is hidden behind `SHOW_PANTRY_STOCK`, mezo-6nu — section head `Készlet · ár` → `Ár`), with `Dózis` replacing the macro sections for supplement/stim/med; `submit()` builds a complete `PantryItemInput` from all state and calls `updateItem(editId)` / `addItem`. The mock `applyIngredientUpdate` (`pantryHooks.ts`) now also persists the extended-nutrition + price fields so an edit round-trips in mock mode; since mezo-1za9 `mockAdd`'s supplement branch + `applyStashUpdate` carry the same macros/nutrition/price so a supplement round-trips too. A **gram-based supplement** (protein powder: per/unit, no discrete dose) now saves cleanly: the backend `PantryService.validatePerKind` requires **dose OR per** for non-food kinds (mezo-2567) — the old dose-only rule 400'd such an update, so editing the item's ADAG silently reverted. (Since mezo-0gjr the basis is no longer editable at all: create pins `per: 100, unit: 'g'`, edit ECHOES the stored basis from `inputFromItem` — required because `validatePerKind` runs on the update request too — and a legacy non-100 basis renders as a read-only „Bázis: /{per} {unit} · örökölt" hint.) (**`ImportItemSheet`** is REAL and re-mounted since Fuel P6 (mezo-bka): a 3-phase **OpenFoodFacts** wizard — `input` (one search field: terméknév VAGY typed vonalkód; the camera/OCR/mic quick-chips stay **inert disabled** affordances for P8+) → `searching` (`usePantryActions().lookupItems` — real: `GET /api/pantry-import/lookup?q=`, all-digit q ≥8 is a barcode fetch; mock: the `pantryLookupFixture` after a demo delay) → `preview` (result list, first hit pre-picked; editable **Név** + **Kategória** draft, **plus a `NutrientCells` row under the kcal/P/C/F `StatCell`s since mezo-m6uv** — the draft already carried the four tápérték facts, they just weren't rendered before) → **`Polcra`** awaits `usePantryActions().importItem` (real: `POST /api/pantry-import` → item + feed row in one tx, then `['pantry']` invalidate; mock: cache-append ingredient + feed row) and closes. **Since the URL-scrape slice (mezo-8vum) the sheet gained a `Link` segment**, and **since the photo-import slice (mezo-d8tr) a third `Fotó` segment** — the mode toggle is now `Keresés (OFF) | Link | Fotó` (barcode is not its own mode: the Keresés field already accepts a typed barcode, so the barcode-scanner chip stays an inert P8+ affordance there). **Link mode** = a URL field + a „Beolvasás" button → `usePantryActions().scrapeItem(url)` (real: `POST /api/pantry-import/scrape`; mock: `MOCK_SCRAPE_DRAFT` after a demo delay) → the same editable preview (**+ `NutrientCells` since mezo-m6uv**, same row as the Keresés-mode preview) → confirm step, carrying the scrape provenance (`sourceUrl`/`confidence`/`priceHuf`/`priceUnit`) through `importItem`; a yellow „ellenőrizd a számokat" hint shows when the draft's `needsReview` is true, and honest-empty (page has no nutrition facts) / fetch-error states offer manual entry. **Fotó mode** (mezo-d8tr) = a required label-photo `<input type=file capture=environment>` (5 MB client pre-check, aria-label „Címke fotó") + an optional front-of-pack photo (aria-label „Előlap fotó") → „Beolvasás" → `usePantryActions().photoExtract(photo, photo2?)` (real: `POST /api/pantry-import/photo`; mock: `MOCK_PHOTO_DRAFT` after a demo delay) → **reuses the exact Link-mode preview** (same editable Név/Kategória card, same `SourceBadge`, same `NutrientCells` row, same needsReview hint) with mode-aware copy for the honest-empty case; a low-confidence/nameless draft additionally surfaces a **„+ előlap fotó (név/márka)"** re-extract chip that re-calls `photoExtract` with both images. Confirm passes `origin: 'photo'` (no `sourceUrl`) through `importItem`, which the backend maps straight to `source: 'photo'` on both the item and the feed row. The former inert „Címke fotó" quick-import chip in the Keresés HAMAROSAN card is retired — photo import graduated to its own mode. Hungarian copy throughout. **Current state since the OFF-search removal (`mezo-ymt4`, 2026-09-02): the `Keresés (OFF)` mode described above is retired from the frontend** — `ImportItemSheet` now offers only **Fotó** (default) **· Link**, `usePantryActions().lookupItems` and the mock `pantryLookupFixture` are deleted, and the MSW `/api/pantry-import/lookup` handler is gone; `GET /api/pantry-import/lookup` itself is untouched in the backend (see the Pantry-Import contract below), just no longer called from the FE.)

**`"Gyógyszer"` (`FuelMedicationPage`, mezo-d94, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-26-fuel-medication-design.md` · mockup `gyogyszer-a-szellos.html`):** a **generic** medication-cycle + dose-log domain, **not** a pantry item — and, since the medication-retirement cleanup (`mezo-lwmq`), **permanently empty**: the owner tracks no medication, the pre-existing seed + dose rows were physically deleted (§4), and there is **no way to add a medication from the UI** — a deliberate YAGNI call, see [ADR 0027](../decisions/0027-retire-retatrutide-generic-medication-domain.md). `useMedication().medication.id === ''` ⇒ the page renders one honest **empty-state card** under the unchanged `"Fuel · Gyógyszer"` eyebrow: *"Nincs aktív gyógyszer"* + an explanatory line that there is currently no add path (`data-testid="medication-empty"`) — no `＋ Beadás` chip, no medication card, no dose log.

**F7.3 (`mezo-d20.8.3.1`, prototype `fuel-mely.html`, spec `2026-08-31-fuel-mely-kor.md` §A) makes the lifecycle real from the UI — the "no add path" call above is superseded.** The empty state stops being a dead end: *"Nincs követett gyógyszer"* + a **`＋ Gyógyszer felvétele`** CTA opening **`MedicationFormSheet`** (`sheets/MedicationFormSheet.tsx`, shared create/edit): név + hatóanyag, beviteli út chips (subQ/IM/orális), dózis + egység, kadencia (heti + nap-chips → `weekly-{day}`, or `daily`), and a **read-only cycle preview** — create sends the exported `DEFAULT_CYCLE` 7-day **2P·3S·2T** template (no phase editor this round, an approved default), edit carries the existing config verbatim. The populated page gains a **Szerkesztés + Leállítás ghost pair** under the dose log: stop is a **two-step inline confirm** (dashed card, `data-testid="medication-stop-confirm"`) that is deliberately **never error-toned** — a decision, not a mistake — and soft-archives via `PUT active:false` (`useMedicationActions().stopMedication`), after which the day read answers "no active medication" and the page falls onto the empty state; the dose history stays server-side.
