The single new contract element is **`POST /api/medication`** (`createMedication`, 201; **one active medication per owner** — creating over an active row is a 400 `MEDICATION_ACTIVE_EXISTS`, re-creating after a stop is the normal medication-change path — `MedicationService.createMedication`, pinned by `MedicationApiIT`). Mock mode mirrors it in the `['medication']` cache: create writes the new medication with the honest-zero ghost cycle, stop resets to the no-medication ghost.

The **populated branch is dead code kept alive as generic machinery** (unreachable in production, since nothing ever creates a medication, but exercised by fixture-backed tests — `medicationFixture`, `frontend/src/test/fixtures/medication.ts`): a page-header **`＋ Beadás`** chip; a single **medication card** (left border `--error`): name · current dose (`defaultDose doseUnit`), a route · cadence subline (humanized via `ROUTE_LABEL`/`CADENCE_LABEL` — `subQ` → `"subQ injekció"`, `weekly-monday` → `"heti · hétfő"`), the **`MedicationCycleBar`** (the 7-cell kinetic strip — one `rad-12` cell per cycle day, phase-tinted P/S/T, the current `cycle.cycleDay` cell outlined with `--text-primary`; ported from the mockup `.cyc` strip to design tokens: peak → `--error`, stable → `--sage`, trough → `--warning`), and a **phase note** (`"{cycleDay}. nap · {phase} fázis · utolsó beadás N napja"`, `data-testid="medication-phase-note"`). Below: the **Beadások** dose log — a `.card` row per dose (`huMonthDayDow` date + `dose doseUnit`), newest first (the hook returns `recentDoses` newest-first), or an empty-state card. The **`＋ Beadás`** button flips a `logOpen` state that mounts the **`LogDoseSheet`** (`features/fuel/sheets/LogDoseSheet.tsx`, mezo-d94) — a chamfer `<Sheet>` (mirrors `AddPantryItemSheet`) with four fields: **Dátum** (default today, `localDateString()`), **Időpont** (optional `<input type=time>`), **Dózis** (prefilled from `doses[0]?.dose`, falling back to `med.defaultDose`), and **Jegyzet** (optional). Save builds a `MedicationDoseInput` whose `administeredAt` is an **offset-bearing** ISO-8601 string (`toOffsetIso(date, time || '00:00')` → e.g. `2026-06-26T00:00:00+02:00`) and calls `useMedicationActions().logDose(...)` then `onClose()`. The offset is the browser's local UTC offset for that wall-clock moment, appended rather than `.toISOString()`'d on purpose: Jackson's `OffsetDateTime` deserializer rejects a **zone-less** datetime (a naive `{date}T{time}:00` → 400 in real mode), and an offset matching local keeps `administeredAt.toLocalDate()` (the backend's day key, `MedicationService.logDose`) equal to the **chosen** `date` — a `.toISOString()` would shift local-midnight to UTC and roll the date back a day in a `+offset` timezone, corrupting the cycle's day math. Because the cycle is **derived** from the newest dose's date part, a today-dated dose re-anchors **`cycleDay` to 1**. The cycle day + phase are **derived** (not entered): you log only the actual injections, and `useMedication` (mock) / the backend `MedicationCycleService` derive `cycleDay`/phase from the newest dose. `MedicationCycleBar` is presentational (`features/fuel/components/MedicationCycleBar.tsx`) — takes the derived `cycle.week`, renders a semantic `<ul role="list" aria-label="Kinetikus ciklus">` with the current cell flagged `aria-current`.

**The unified meal-log flow (`pages/LogFlowPage.tsx`, Design 2.0 F3.2 · `mezo-d20.4.2`, fuel iterations §7).** **`LogMealSheet` and `AiLogSheet` are GONE, merged into ONE surface** — the redesign's single biggest structural change inside Fuel, and an IA change, not a re-skin: manual capture and AI capture were two sheets you had to choose between *before* you knew what you were logging, so a photo-estimated line and a pantry line could never ride in the same meal. Now every entry point opens the same flow and **AI is a SOURCE inside it**, armed or not. Props: `{ initialSlot?, prefill?, aiPanelOpenOnMount?, onClose }`. It is a **page** by design (no bottom-sheet drag-to-dismiss), but it is not routed — it is a full-bleed **self-portaled overlay** into `.phone-screen` (the `LevelUpScreen` technique, z-index between the tab bar and the sheets it hosts), a deliberate deviation recorded in the slice's own file header; `Escape` closes it. Anatomy top→bottom: a `‹ Vissza` chip + a `ma · HH:mm` stamp; **MIKOR** — the four slot segments (Reggeli/Ebéd/Vacsora/Snack); ~~NÉV~~ — **the name field is GONE (mezo-byo1)**: the title is always the shared pure `deriveMealName(lines)` (the same rule `buildDayPlan` falls back to), shown as the totals card's own title line; **HONNAN ADOD HOZZÁ?** — three colored source tiles: 🫙 **Kamra** (gold, grams → `KamraPickSheet`, which stays open for multi-add and flips an added row to `✓`), 🥄 **Recept** (coral, adag → `ReceptPickSheet`, closes on pick) and ✨ **AI** (lavender, an **inline panel inside the flow**, not a separate sheet); **TÉTELEK · N** — the line cards; the sage **EZ AZ ÉTKEZÉS** totals card; and the `Mégse` / **`✓ Logolás · +10 XP`** CTA pair (disabled with zero lines). Every line card carries its source tag (`kamra`/`recept`/`becslés`), a **typeable amount with ± steppers** (the `amountGuard` contract: an invalid or ≤0 entry keeps the previous value), a live per-line `MacroCells` and — on non-estimate lines — `NutrientCells`; the totals card re-sums both live and closes with a daily-context bar (`Mai nap eddig X +Y = Z` against the day's kcal target, read from the composed `useFuelDay`). **The `mezo-ormb` recipe ingredient-override block survived the merge and now lives here**, ported line-for-line out of the retired `LogMealSheet`: a recipe line card gets a `HOZZÁVALÓK · N` disclosure (**collapsed by default**, so the one-tap fast path is untouched; the header appends `· N MÓDOSÍTVA` once anything is touched) expanding into one **`RecipeOverrideRow`** — still `frontend/src/features/fuel/components/RecipeOverrideRow.tsx`, unmoved and unchanged — per ingredient, editing the amount in the **recipe's own unit with decimals** (a half banana is `0,5 db`) via a ± stepper (±10 for `g`/`ml`, ±0,5 otherwise) beside a tap-to-type field accepting a Hungarian decimal comma, plus that line's live kcal and a per-row revert. A blank input is ignored (never read as `0`); an explicit **`0` means the ingredient was left out**. The values describe **the whole recipe as made**, orthogonal to the card's own `adag` amount ("how much of it I ate"); a single `Alaphelyzet` resets every row on that card. Two details are load-bearing: `setOverride` **only records a genuine delta** (stepping back to the recipe's own amount deletes the key, so the MÓDOSÍTVA count and `Alaphelyzet` don't linger on an untouched line), and `save()` **drops any override whose ingredient index no longer resolves** — `useRecipes` refetches on window focus, so a concurrent recipe edit can shrink `ingredients` underneath an open flow, and dropping the stale entry keeps that a no-op instead of a crash. Overridden lines re-roll the whole-recipe rollup through `computeRecipeMacrosWithOverrides`/`computeRecipeNutrientsWithOverrides` in the SAME order as the backend (round per line, divide unrounded, round once at the end), so an un-overridden line stays bit-identical to the plain path. **Slot defaulting is the `mezo-bnsf` contract, now enforced by construction:** `slot` initialises to `initialSlot ?? defaultMealSlot()` and a `slotLocked` ref records whether the launch was window-anchored — because `buildDayPlan` files logged meals by **`slotKey` alone** (`loggedByKey[w.slotKey]`, never by timestamp), a log opened from the Ebéd window but seeded from the wall clock would land in a different window and leave Ebéd `missed`, still offering `Pótold` for a meal already logged. `WindowLane` therefore passes the tapped tile's `slotKey` on **both** the `Logold` and the `✨ AI` action, and a locked slot is not overwritten even when an AI draft proposes a different one. Genuinely context-free launches (the lane's `Ablakon kívül` tile, the recipe/pantry detail CTAs, `EletjelPage`) legitimately keep the wall-clock `defaultMealSlot()`. **`loggedAt` is an OFFSET-BEARING local now (`nowOffsetIso`, `shared/lib/dates.ts`), never `new Date().toISOString()` (mezo-g8qm)** — the backend derives the meal's training role (`classifyRole`) and timing fit from `loggedAt.toLocalTime()`, so a UTC `Z` wall clock 1–2 h off local dropped a pre-workout meal out of its window and scored it under the STANDARD rubric. The live per-line contribution uses the SAME whole-number `round(macro × amount/per)` rule as the data layer's `buildLine` and the backend `MealMapper` (single round at the end), so the preview total matches the persisted meal exactly. **Meal score is REAL since P7 (mezo-yta):** `POST /api/meal` returns the deterministic score + typed breakdown, `mealApi.fromResponse` maps it onto `FuelMeal.score`/`FuelMeal.breakdown`, so a freshly logged meal immediately shows its chip on the hub lane and opens a fully populated `MealScoreSheet`. **Entry points, all of them now this one flow:** the hub lane's per-window `Logold`/`Pótold` and `✨ AI` (slot-seeded), the lane's trailing `Ablakon kívül` tile (`＋ Logolás` / `✨ AI napló`, slot-less), `RecipeDetailPage`'s `+ Mai étkezéshez`, `KamraItemDetailPage`'s `＋ Logolás`, and the Nap tab's `NapRutinPage` (`initialSlot="breakfast"`) / `EletjelPage` (slot-less). **`MealPickerSheet.tsx` was NOT deleted** but has no consumer left — `KamraPickSheet` and `ReceptPickSheet` replaced its two tabs with two single-purpose sheets, because in the new flow the source is chosen *before* the picker opens (§9).

**The flow's body is `components/MealComposer.tsx` since mezo-byo1** — the 556-line editor extracted whole (sources, AI panel, amountGuard, mezo-ormb overrides, provenance rule, totals, save) so `/fuel/log/uj` (`pages/FuelLogNewPage.tsx`, mezo-bq2t) can mount the SAME composer full-screen on its own route (`fixedSlot` hides MIKOR and pins the save slot), while `LogFlowPage` stays as the thin self-portaled overlay wrapper serving the other four entry points with unchanged props (KamraItemDetailPage, RecipeDetailPage, EletjelPage, NapRutinPage). The same slice re-faced the composer's line cards (**kind-wash**: source-tinted gradient + 4px spine per `data-tag`, a kcal cell in the source ink and a 3-cell macro mini row in the shared mz-cell tints, with `NutrientCells` below) and the totals card (derived-name title + a colorful `MCells` row), and both pickers: **`KamraPickSheet`** grew a horizontal **category-chip row** (Mind + one color-dotted chip per category actually on the shelf, labels/colors from `usePantry().categoryMeta`; chip and search compose, an empty intersection says so) and its rows wear the Kamra page's kind-wash card language (category spine + faint wash, `NovaDot`, right-aligned kcal cell); **`ReceptPickSheet`** grew a `★ csillagos` filter chip and coral kind-wash rows (★ on starred names, /adag kcal cell). Multi-add (Kamra stays open) vs. single-add (Recept closes) is unchanged. Design source: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-fuel-logolas-2.0-design.md` + prototype `docs/design_2.0/prototypes/fuel-logolas.html`. **Since mezo-1j3z `MealComposer` also takes optional `logDate?`/`logTime?`/`saveLabel?` props** so a past-day visit — `/fuel/log`'s day stepper landing on `/fuel/log/uj` with `?d=` set — can save onto that day: with a `logDate` the save timestamp is `offsetIso(logDate, logTime ?? SLOT_DEFAULT_TIME[slot])` (per-slot defaults — reggeli 08:00 · ebéd 13:00 · vacsora 19:00 · snack 16:00 — used whenever the caller doesn't pin an explicit time) and the composer's data hooks switch to the date-scoped `useMealActions(logDate)`/`useFuelDay(logDate)`; every existing call site omits `logDate` and stays byte-identical, still timestamping via `nowOffsetIso()`.

**The AI path is a PANEL inside the flow, not a wizard (mezo-78rn's capability, `mezo-d20.4.2`'s surface).** Tapping the ✨ AI source tile (or launching with `aiPanelOpenOnMount`, which is exactly what a window tile's `✨ AI` action does) expands a lavender panel holding a `„Mit ettél?”` textarea, a 📷 **Fotó** picker (`<input type=file accept="image/*" capture="environment">`, with a live object-URL thumbnail and a remove ✕) and an `✨ Elemzés` button enabled as soon as either input is non-empty — **text or photo or both**. Analysis downscales any picked photo via `resizeImage` (canvas → ~1024 px JPEG) and calls `useMealActions().draftMealFromAi({ date, text, photo })`; while it runs the panel is replaced by a twinkle `„Elemzem az étkezést…”`; a failure shows `„Nem sikerült az AI-feldolgozás. Próbáld újra, vagy add hozzá kézzel.”` and leaves the typed text in place. **The recognised lines land in the ordinary TÉTELEK list**, tagged by their REAL source since the pantry-name-match slice (mezo-qrks) — `kamra ✨`/`recept ✨` for a matched line, a bare `becslés` for a genuine LLM estimate — replacing the design-2.0 §7 blanket `becslés` for every AI-produced line (`MealComposer`'s `lineMeta` now returns both a semantic `tag`, which feeds `data-tag` and stays `kamra`/`recept`/`becslés` for styling, and a displayed `tagLabel`, the same word plus ` ✨` on an AI pantry/recipe line); each line carries the same steppers and per-line macros as a manual line and, when the draft flags `needsReview`, an inline note that differs by source: an estimate line asks the user to check the AMOUNT (`„✨ Az AI nem teljesen biztos ebben a sorban — nézd át a mennyiséget.”`), a pantry/recipe line asks them to confirm it's really THAT ITEM (`„✨ Ezt a kamrádból párosítottuk név alapján — ellenőrizd, hogy tényleg ez a tétel.”`). The panel then collapses and clears — so one meal can genuinely mix photo + text + pantry + recipe, which is the entire point of the merge. The old wizard's separate `input → drafting → review` phases, its slot selector and its `„nem ismertem fel ételt” → manual fallback` CTA are all gone: nothing recognised simply adds no lines, and the manual path is already on screen. **`provenance.origin` is an honest approximation, and flagged as such in the code** (`MealProvenanceInput` has no `mixed` arm): origin reflects whether AI genuinely contributed to THIS save — `ai-photo` when a photo was analysed this session, else `ai-text` when free text was — regardless of how many manual pantry/recipe lines ride alongside; a meal built entirely from Kamra/Recept tiles omits provenance entirely, exactly like the retired `LogMealSheet`'s manual-only path. **Per-line origin is not representable in the current contract — flagged, not invented** (§9).

## 3. Architecture & data flow

The single FE↔data boundary is `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts`. For most of Fuel the path **stops at mock data** — no API, no `isMockMode()`, no TanStack Query. **Exception — Pantry (Kamra):** `usePantry`/`usePantryActions` moved to `frontend/src/data/fuel/pantryHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`) and are now dual-mode TanStack Query hooks (mezo-9xu). `usePantry` keeps its exact return shape `{ ingredients, stash, sources, categoryMeta, imports, suggestions }`. `ingredients`/`stash` flow through the **`useDualQuery`** helper (`frontend/src/data/useDualQuery.ts`, mezo-0xl): in **mock mode** the static seed via `initialData` (synchronous first render; `staleTime: Infinity` so `usePantryActions` mock cache edits via `setQueryData` are not clobbered by a refetch); in **real mode** from `pantryApi.list()` (`GET /api/pantry`) and — crucially — `{ ingredients: [], stash: [] }` (NOT the seed) while the query is unresolved, so a live user never flashes fake pantry data on a cold load. **`imports`/`suggestions` are REAL dual-mode since P6 (mezo-bka)** — they ride the SAME `['pantry']` query (`PantryResponse.imports/suggestions`, honest-empty arrays; `pantryApi.list` maps the feed's ISO `when` to the display string: today → `ma · HH:MM`, else `huMonthDay`); `sources`/`categoryMeta` stay static config. `usePantryActions` exposes `{ addItem, updateItem, deleteItem, importItem, scrapeItem, photoExtract }` — mock mode mutates the `['pantry']` cache, real mode calls `pantryApi.create/update/remove` and invalidates (the `useWeight` dual-mode pattern); `importItem` is `mutateAsync`-backed (the sheet awaits + closes). (P6 also added a plain uncached `lookupItems` read, mock-backed by `pantryLookupFixture` — **retired from the FE since the OFF-search removal, `mezo-ymt4` 2026-09-02**, see the Kamra-import note in §2 above; `scrapeItem`/`photoExtract` are the P8 uncached draft reads that remain.) **Real-mode `update` is a partial merge** (mezo-dh6): the backend `PantryMapper.applyRequestPartial` applies only the fields the sparse FE edit payload carries and leaves omitted columns untouched (**null = leave unchanged**), mirroring the mock `applyIngredientUpdate`'s `input.x ?? existing.x` — so a `PUT` from the lean edit form never nulls rich columns (`micros`/`nova`/`stock`/`brand`) it doesn't round-trip. `createItem` stays a full-replace; a future clear-a-field UX would need a real PATCH/JsonNullable contract.

**Exception — Recipes (Receptek):** `useRecipes`/`useRecipeActions` moved to `frontend/src/data/fuel/recipeHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`, mezo-lns) and are also dual-mode. `useRecipes()` returns **`{ recipes, sources, categoryMeta }`** — only `recipes` is dual-mode via **`useDualQuery`** (`['recipes']`: mock `initialData` + `staleTime: Infinity`, real `recipeApi.list` → `GET /api/recipe`, `realEmpty: []` so a cold real-mode load shows no recipes rather than the 6 mock ones — mezo-0xl); `sources`/`categoryMeta` stay static config. **An `ingredients` field was REMOVED (mezo-yew):** it exposed the static mock seed even in real mode, so the editor/detail views — which resolve each line's display name + live macro contribution by `refId` — rendered the raw backend **UUID + zero macros** against real pantry IDs. `RecipeDetailPage` resolves a line's source badge + border color from **`usePantry().ingredients`**; **`RecipeEditorPage` + `IngredientPickerSheet` resolve name + live macro contribution from `usePickableIngredients()`** — foods **+** the supplement/stim/med stash merged by `data/fuel/pantryPickables.ts` `buildPickables` (mezo-3vu4) — so every picked `refId` (food **or** supplement) resolves by construction. The mock recipe builder (`recipeHooks.buildRecipe`) enriches each saved line's `name`/`contribution` through that **same merged pool**, so a stored supplement line never falls back to its raw id. `useRecipeActions()` exposes `{ create, update, remove }`: mock mode mutates the `['recipes']` cache via `setQueryData`, real mode calls `recipeApi.create/update/remove` then invalidates **BOTH `['recipes']` AND `['pantry']`** (a recipe references pantry items, so a write shifts the pantry `usedInRecipes` rollup). **Mock/real parity is exact:** each line's `name` + `contribution{kcal,p,c,f}` and the whole-recipe `macros` are computed by the shared `frontend/src/data/fuel/recipeMacros.ts` formula (`factor = amount / per`, per→1 for discrete units; each macro `= roundMacro(snapshot × factor)` rounded to a **whole number** — `Math.round`, matching the backend `RecipeMapper.setScale(0, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)`; whole-recipe = Σ contributions) — the SAME formula the backend `RecipeMapper` runs at read time. `recipeApi` (`frontend/src/data/fuel/recipeApi.ts`) re-keys each contract line's `pantryItemId → refId` (`fromResponse`) and maps the editor `RecipeInput` back (`toRequest`). **`useRecipeBreakdown(recipeId)` (mezo-bw3y)** is the third dual-mode recipe hook: mock serves the seed `templateBreakdown` + `mezoFit.fitsFor` synchronously (`initialData`), real fires the lazily materializing `recipeApi.getBreakdown` (`['recipeBreakdown', id]`, 5-min staleTime — the backend caches the enriched envelope; `pending` drives the detail page's twinkle card, no mock fallback); a recipe **update** additionally invalidates that one recipe's `['recipeBreakdown', id]` (an edit nulls the server cache), a **delete** `removeQueries`-es it, and a **create** touches neither — the key is per recipe, never the whole prefix, or an untouched recipe would claim a re-evaluation it never had (mezo-uavr). The FE `Recipe.mezoFit.score` is `number | null` — **real since P7**: `RecipeService` computes the deterministic fit at every read (macro+micro+NOVA over the per-serving profile, weights renormalized ÷ .80; null only when the profile carries no kcal), so all recipes light up retroactively and pantry edits reflect immediately; the `recipe.fit_score` column stays unused (reserved for the P8 calibrated fit). `RecipeIngredientLine` gained optional `name`/`contribution`.

**Exception — Medication (Gyógyszer):** `useMedication`/`useMedicationActions` live in `frontend/src/data/fuel/medicationHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`, mezo-d94) and are dual-mode. `useMedication()` returns **`{ medication, cycle, doses }`** off a single dual-mode `['medication']` query (a `MedicationDay`): mock seeds `medicationSeed` synchronously via `initialData` (`staleTime: Infinity`, never background-refetches — the actions own the cache via `setQueryData`; real mode uses the 30s app default `DEFAULT_QUERY_STALE_TIME_MS`, passed explicitly since a bare omission would clobber the client default to always-stale); real fetches `medicationApi.getDay()` → `GET /api/medication`, which **never 404s (mezo-5cmq)**: no active medication is answered with 200 and `{medication: null, cycle: null, recentDoses: []}`, mapped onto the SAME **no-medication ghost** (`cycleDay 0`, empty week/doses, `name ''`) the hook also returns while the query is unresolved — NOT the seed — honoring the "no static fallback in real mode" invariant. The pre-5cmq 404 shape is still absorbed onto that ghost too, via `realEmpty`, since the two deployed images don't switch at the same moment. Since `mezo-lwmq` the mock `medicationSeed` (`data/fuel/medication.ts`) is byte-identical in shape to this same ghost — the owner tracks no medication in either mode, so both branches agree on the honest zero by default. `useMedicationActions()` exposes `{ logDose, removeDose, updateMedication }`: mock mode mutates the `['medication']` cache and **re-derives the cycle** the way the backend `MedicationCycleService.derive` does (`cycleDay = days-since-newest-dose + 1`, clamped to `cycleLengthDays`; the phase/week project that day onto the cycle config — a dose today → `cycleDay 1`); real mode calls `medicationApi.logDose/deleteDose/updateMedication` (path-keyed by the active medication id from the cache) then invalidates **`['medication']` + `['today']` + `['fuelDay']`** (the `cycleDay` broadcast feeds Today + Fuel Terv). `FuelMedicationPage` consumes the read hook; the write path is `LogDoseSheet` (mezo-d94), which calls `useMedicationActions().logDose` — reachable only via the (currently dead, §2) populated branch, since there is no create-medication path.

**Exception — Stack / Protocol (mezo-09g → mezo-vx9v → UI family mezo-ubxd):** the backend contract and occurrence model are unchanged. `useProtocol()` and `useStack()` remain dual-mode reads over the shared TanStack caches (`['protocol']` and `['fuelIntake', date]`), while `useStackDay(date?)` composes them with sleep, training, running and Fuel settings before calling the pure `projectStackDay`. Stack 3.0 adds the pure `buildStackDayView` presentation layer (`logic/stackPresentation.ts`) once above that projection; the hub and all read/manage pages consume the same derived next occurrence, progress, grouped protocol, timeline and meal facts, so navigation does not create parallel state. Writes stay on `useProtocolActions()` and `useStackActions(date)`. `useStackIntakeToggle` is the one intake interaction seam: it awaits `logIntake`, receives the created intake id, publishes a simple success toast with a `Visszavonás` action, and the callback calls `undoIntakeById(id)` so a delayed tap cannot delete a newer equal pantry/zone/dose row. Both action callbacks return Promises; a rejected undo reaches the global `MutationCache.onError`, while the action toast dismisses after invocation. Protocol editing remains occurrence CRUD (`addItem`/`moveItem`/`setDose`/`unpinItem`/`removeItem`/`removeAllFor`) with no selection draft, activate endpoint or fake save boundary.

Any component reading `usePantry`/`useRecipes`/`useMedication`/`useStack`/`useProtocol` — including every Stack hub, read and manage page — needs a `QueryClientProvider` in tests; routed pages also need a router context (`MemoryRouter` + `Routes`). The rest of Fuel (still mock-const spreads in `fuelReadHooks.ts`):

```
View (features/fuel/pages/*.tsx)
  → useFuelTimeline                                  (hooks.ts → timelineHooks.ts — DUAL-MODE, Fuel P5)
  → useFuelWeek                                      (hooks.ts → fuelWeekHooks.ts — DUAL-MODE, Fuel P4; read-only gym times)
  → useStackDay                                      (hooks.ts → stackDayHooks.ts — DUAL-MODE, mezo-vx9v Task 8; composes
                                                       useProtocol/useStack/useIntakes/useSleepGoal/useTrain/useRunning/
                                                       useFuelSettings + projectStackDay(); the Stack page's one data source)
  → useReplanScenarios                               (hooks.ts → fuelReadHooks.ts)
      (usePantry/usePantryActions — dual-mode, data/fuel/pantryHooks.ts; see prose above)
      (useRecipes/useRecipeActions — dual-mode, data/fuel/recipeHooks.ts; see prose above)
      (useStack/useProtocol + actions — dual-mode, data/fuel/stackHooks.ts; see prose above)
      → returns imported static consts:
          data/fuel/fuel.ts        (fuelDay, getScoredMeal; supplementsStash + protocol
                                    now feed the dual-mode stackHooks seeds, not this path.
                                    fuelPlan/fuelToday retired mezo-53su — the timeline computes)
          data/fuel/pantry.ts      (ingredients, recipes[derived], pantryCategoryMeta, pantryImports, pantrySuggestions)
          data/pantrySources.ts (pantrySources)
          data/fuel/fuelWeek.ts    (medCycleWeek, gymSchedule, weeklySupplements, recurringPatterns,
                               weeklyStats, weekTitle, weeklyNote — the fuelWeekHooks MOCK seeds;
                               replanScenarios — its sibling stackRecommendations const was deleted with
                               useStackRecommendations, mezo-vx9v Task 8)
      → useReplanScenarios: NO isMockMode/apiFetch/useQuery
```

**Exception — Terv weekly plan (`useFuelWeek`, Fuel P4 · mezo-kpo; read-only since mezo-4t43).** Moved to `frontend/src/data/fuel/fuelWeekHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`) and now dual-mode + composed. Its MOCK branch returns the `fuelWeek.ts` seeds byte-for-byte (plus the seed `weekTitle`/`weeklyNote`); its REAL branch calls `useTrain`/`useMedication` + a `['fuelWeek', monday]` query unconditionally (rules-of-hooks; only the RETURN branches on `isMockMode()`) and composes: `gymSchedule` = `useTrain().gymSchedule.weeklyTimes` (Train's `deriveGymSchedule` join) run through `withDefaultDuration` (active timed days get `DEFAULT_BLOCK_MIN` — duration has no DB home, presentational only); `volleyball` = `useTrain().sport.schedule.volleyball.sessions`; `medCycleWeek` = `toMedCycleCells(useMedication().cycle.week)` (phaseKey → `Peak|Stable|Trough`, `var(--medcycle-d{n})` colors; ghost cycle → `[]` → the page hides the medication card); `weeklyStats` = `deriveWeeklyStats(mealApi.getWeek(mondayIso()).days)`; `title` = `deriveWeekTitle(monday)`; `patterns`/`weeklySupplements` = `[]`, `weeklyNote` = null (honest-empty). The return shape is the typed **`FuelWeekView`** — the original six keys plus additive `title` + `weeklyNote`. **Gym times are read-only from Fuel (mezo-4t43): `useFuelWeekActions`/`gymDaysToSlots` were removed** — editing now lives in Train (the mesocycle planner Step 2 + the Gym "Időpontok" chip, both → `PUT /api/train/gym-schedule`). Any test rendering `useFuelWeek` (incl. `MedicationWeekStrip`) needs a `QueryClientProvider` even in mock mode.

**Exception — Meal logging (Mai · `useFuelDay`/`useMealActions`/`useWaterActions`/`useRecipeLogs`, mezo-arb + mezo-0z5).** These moved to `frontend/src/data/fuel/fuelHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`) and are now dual-mode. **`useFuelDay(date?)` is COMPOSED so its public return keeps the full `FuelDay` shape `{ targets, consumed, meals, pacing, micronutrients, supplements }`** — only `targets/consumed/meals` are query-driven via **`useDualQuery`** (`['fuelDay', date]`: mock `initialData` from the `fuelDay` seed + `staleTime: Infinity`, real `mealApi.getDay(date)` → `GET /api/fuel/day/{date}`, `realEmpty` = a **zero `FuelDay`** so a cold real-mode load shows 0/empty rather than the seed's fabricated macros + 4 fake meals — mezo-0xl; the `KcalGauge`/`.macror` bars and `LogFlowPage`'s daily-context bar guard the resulting `0/0` percent → `0%` via the shared `pct` util `frontend/src/shared/lib/pct.ts`); `pacing/micronutrients/supplements` stay static from the `fuelDay` seed (supplements = the Stack slice). `useMealActions(date?)` exposes `{ logMeal, updateMeal, deleteMeal }`: mock mode mutates the `['fuelDay', date]` cache via `setQueryData` (recomputing `consumed`), real mode calls `mealApi.create/update/remove` then invalidates **`['fuelDay']` AND `['recipes']` AND `['pantry']`** (logging shifts recipe `recentLogs` + pantry usage). **`useWaterActions(date?)`** (mezo-0z5) exposes `{ logWater }`: mock **increments `consumed.water` in place** on the `['fuelDay', date]` cache (`+250/+500 ml`); real POSTs `/api/water-log` then invalidates `['fuelDay']` **AND `['dailyQuests', date]`** (mezo-gj2y — quest evaluation is read-triggered, so the water write nudges the day's quest read; Today's `+250 ml` quest CTA and the water quest's completion ride this, see [today.md §2](today.md)). Its counterpart on the mock path: **`recomputeConsumed(meals, water)` now carries the running `water` through unchanged** (`fuelHooks.ts:194`) instead of resetting it to `targets.water`, so a meal log no longer wipes the day's logged water. `useRecipeLogs(recipeId)` feeds `RecipeLogsList` (mock: the recipe seed's `recentLogs`; real: `useQuery(['recipeLogs', id])` → `GET /api/recipe/{id}/logs` — since P7 each log carries the meal's real `score`, and the list computes the delta against the live `mezoFit` baseline; **the endpoint moved to the Meal tag/controller in-slice** — the data lives in `meal_item`, and the re-tag removed the only recipe→meal edge, resolving the frozen ArchUnit slice cycle mezo-ah18.16). **Mock/real parity is exact:** each logged line's `contribution{kcal,p,c,f}` uses the same whole-number `factor = amount / per` formula as the backend `MealMapper.scaled(…, setScale(0, HALF_UP))` (`Math.round`), and the meal rollup = Σ line contributions — identical to the Recipes-slice `recipeMacros.ts` precedent. The recipe-arm per-serving snapshot is stored at **full precision** (`MealService.perServing` = whole-recipe ÷ servings, unrounded) so the contribution round is the *only* round — mezo-8xy fixed a backend double-round (per-serving pre-rounded to a whole number) that drifted the recipe arm 1-3 kcal above the FE single-round (e.g. a 297 kcal / 2-serving recipe at 2 adag showed 298 vs the correct 297); the pantry arm was always exact. `mealApi` (`frontend/src/data/fuel/mealApi.ts`) re-keys each polymorphic line by `source` (`recipeId`|`pantryItemId ↔ refId`) in `toRequest`/`fromResponse`.

**Exception — Mai timeline (`useFuelTimeline`, Fuel P5 · mezo-9ys; unified in both modes · mezo-53su).** Moved to `frontend/src/data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts` (re-exported from `hooks.ts`). Since **mezo-53su it runs ONE composition in both modes** — the hand-authored `fuelPlan.today` seed is **retired**. It calls `useFuelDay`/`useRecipes`/`useGoal`/**`useSleepGoal`**/`useProtocol`/`useStack`/`useIntakes`/`useTrain`/`useRunning`/**`useFuelSettings`** unconditionally, then feeds the live day into the pure `buildDayPlan(...)`: `wake`/`bed` off `useSleepGoal()` (the day-anchor owner since `mezo-dbsr`, always set: mock seed / backend ghost), `mealsPerDay` **+ `caffeineCutoff` off `useFuelSettings()`** (Fuel-owned since mezo-53su — the `goal.mealsPerDay` read and `PLANNER_DEFAULTS`/`CAFFEINE_CUTOFF` consts are gone), today's gym/sport/run `blocks[]` (reusing the Train views' own today-derivation), the daily `budget` from the goal's current-week prescription segment (`deriveDailyBudget` — **now activity-aware/dynamic** with a third `EnergyInputs` arg since mezo-1oy5, §5; `FuelDay.targets` fallback), and the zoned `protocolSlots` — **since mezo-vx9v Task 9, projected straight off the living-protocol occurrences** (`projectStackDay({ occurrences, stash, intakes, wake, bed, mealsPerDay, blocks, weightKg })`, the SAME pure projection the Stack page's `useStackDay` uses — Task 6/8), **not** the retired selection-based `buildProtocol()` (deleted Task 9; §9 below). The only mode branch is the injected `nowHHmm`: **mock pins `MOCK_NOW_HHMM = '13:30'`** (spec D6 — a deterministic demo + tests), real reads the wall clock. In mock mode every source hook serves its seed, so the same composition yields a **deterministic computed demo plan** (stable across weekdays; the mock sleep goal 06:45/23:15 → eatingStart 07:30, kitchenClose 21:45; fuel-settings ghost 4/'14:00'; **the partial mock day — Reggeli + Ebéd logged, the Uzsonna snack + Vacsora still open — has exactly one `now` slot at 13:30, the next open MEAL window (protocol/block slots keep their own state, they never get `now`), later windows `pending`, and any past-unlogged window would be `missed`; the dynamic budget is genuinely live: `energy.base 2064` (BMR 1720×1.2 NEAT), `activity > 0` from the always-on gym block — mezo-1oy5**). `getScoredMeal` is id-based against `fuel.meals` in both modes. `useFuelTimeline` is a **composed** hook → any test rendering it needs a `QueryClientProvider` even in mock mode (all composed reads seed synchronously via `initialData`). The remaining `useReplanScenarios` is still a one-liner spread of an imported const; compare `useWeight` (`weightHooks.ts:11`). `useStackDay` (mezo-vx9v Task 8) is a THIRD composed-hook example alongside `useFuelTimeline`/`useFuelWeek` — same rule, tests rendering it need a `QueryClientProvider` even in mock mode. **View and component signatures must not change** — that is the Phase-2 contract.

**Presentation-layer composition — the Fuel hub's TWO pure view-models (`mezo-d20.4.1`, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md); §2 has the full walkthrough).** `useFuelTimeline()` above still owns every DATA composition; `FuelMaiPage` layers two pure VIEW-MODEL steps on top of it — the same two-step shape the window-river/keret-hero pages had, with the first step swapped. **`logic/fuelSwimlane.ts`** (NEW, replaces `windowIslands.ts` on the hub): `buildWindowLane({ slots, budget, meals })` — a much narrower input than `buildWindowRiver`'s eight-field one, because the tile carries no stack doses, no hero projection, no medication fact and no ambient `now`. It filters `plan.slots` to real meal windows (`isMealSlot` + a non-null `slotKey`), sorts by `toMin(time)`, maps each to a `WindowTileVM` (state — `pending` renamed `future`, never re-derived from a clock — clay icon, label, time, name/`ghost`, `fromPlan`, nullable `kcal`, three `TileRingVM`s, plus `mealId`/`scorePct`/`scorable` on a done window joined off `slot.mealId`) and returns `{ tiles, nowKey }`. It is **pure**: no React, no ambient time, no `@/data/*` import — only types plus the shared `toMin`/`pct` helpers (the `heroWindow.ts`/`keretHero.ts` discipline), so it is table-tested independent of any hook. Its exports are imported sight-unseen by `WindowLane.tsx` and `FuelMaiPage.tsx`; the `${time}-${label}` tile key is the same identity the retired island model used, so the page no longer needs to re-derive it locally to find the source `FuelSlot` — the VM carries `slotKey` directly. **`logic/keretHero.ts`** is the second VM, unchanged from the keret-hero slice: `buildKeretHero({ budget, staticEnergy, consumed, meals, water, slots, nowHHmm })` → the hero's ring percents / day-bar segments / now-marker / signed chips (§2), same no-React/no-`@/data/*` discipline; `aiAverage` from the same module feeds the hub's Napló tile line. Its two other exports — `deriveMealRole(mealTimeHHmm, workoutTime)` and `doneMealRows(meals, slots)` — fed `DoneWindowsCapsule` and now feed nothing (§9). **`logic/windowIslands.ts` is still in the tree, still tested, with no production caller** — `buildWindowRiver`, `doneGroup` and the `L1_BASE_ROWS` counting rule included. It is left in place deliberately (the F8 pass removed the components, not the pure logic), and `fuelSwimlane.ts` cites its `fromPlan` rule as the precedent it ports. The presentational consumers are `components/FuelLogHeroTile.tsx` (the hub door) + `components/WindowBlock.tsx` on `FuelLogPage` (both purely presentational — every datum from the VM, every action a prop; mezo-byo1 replaced `WindowLane.tsx`, whose once-only auto-scroll died with the horizontal lane) and `components/KeretHero.tsx` (a bare halo-sage band, never an `Island`). **Nothing in Fuel renders `shared/ui/Island` any more** — the shell Today lent Fuel on the 2nd-consumer rule outlived both its consumers (§5/§9).

**One pure "AI" helper survives from the pre-vx9v selection model** (deterministic, not data):
- `features/fuel/logic/buildProtocol.ts` — **`buildProtocol()` itself (the selection→slots/reasoning/mealMatches builder, its needle-matching helpers, and the `mealMatches` prose table) was DELETED in mezo-vx9v Task 9**, once its last three consumers (`useFuelTimeline`, the notification schedule writer, the `Értesítés` settings preview) moved onto the occurrence-based `projectStackDay` above. What survives in the file: `deriveBlocks` (today's gym/sport/run blocks, re-exported from `data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts` for back-compat), `PRE_WORKOUT_STACK_LEAD_MIN` (the canonical 40-minute pre-workout offset — now applied primarily inside `projectStackDay` itself, straight off `blocks`), and `deriveProtocolAnchors`/`ProtocolAnchors` (the `{wake, preWorkout, bedtime}` shape, kept for any caller that wants that shape instead of the raw block list — as of Task 9 it has **no production caller**, only its own test). See §9 for the retirement and the Stack architecture that replaced it (`PlacementEngine` backend rule table, `projectStackDay` FE projection, `matchMealsToStack` for meal suggestions/verdicts).
- `features/fuel/logic/kamraItems.ts` — `buildKamraItems(ingredients, stash): PantryItem[]`. Merges scraped food ingredients + the supplement stash into one `PantryItem[]`, **de-duping** stash items already present as an ingredient via `Ingredient.stashRefId` (`kamraItems.ts:17-18`; `ing-whey`/`ing-kreatin` carry a `stashRefId` to avoid double cards). Stash cards get a **`'stash-'`-prefixed `id`** (`kamraItems.ts:25`) to stay collision-free against food ingredient ids in the unified list — so `KamraItemDetailPage` strips that prefix to a `backendId` before any mutation (edit/delete), since the mock cache mutators match on the raw stash id and the real `PUT`/`DELETE` target the raw UUID (mezo-9xu; without stripping, every stash delete/update was a silent no-op).

**Derived data — the mock `recipes` seed is NOT a plain const.** In `data/fuel/pantry.ts`, `recipesBase` is enriched at module load: each recipe gets `recentLogs` (mirrored from logged `fuelDay.meals` via the recipe-link mapping), a `templateBreakdown` (the linked meal's breakdown, or a standalone `recipeTemplateBreakdowns[rec-3/5/6]`), and — since mezo-lns — each ingredient line is run through `enrichLine` (snapshot `name` + `contribution`) with the whole-recipe `macros` **recomputed** from those contributions via `computeRecipeMacros`, so the mock seed equals what `GET /api/recipe` returns. This couples `data/fuel/pantry.ts` → `data/fuel/fuel.ts` (it imports `fuelDay`) and → `data/fuel/recipeMacros.ts`. The backend reproduces the contribution/rollup math in `RecipeMapper`; the recipe↔log linkage (`recentLogs`/`templateBreakdown`) is still mock-only and a real backend must reproduce it server-side. **`stripMealOnlyTiming` (`pantry.ts`, `mezo-jcpt.3`):** since a mirrored `templateBreakdown` is a shallow copy of its linked meal's real `breakdown` (same `context` dimension reference), it would leak that meal's `timing` (§2's `MealTimingStrip`) onto the recipe's own score sheet — a surface the real `RecipeBreakdownService` never attaches `context`/`timing` to, because a recipe template has no logged time to draw a strip from. This helper nulls `timing` out of the `context` dimension when building `templateBreakdown`, so the mock matches the real recipe-template breakdown's shape; pinned in `pantryData.test.tsx`.

## 4. Data model & API

**Most Fuel types are still FE-only TypeScript in `frontend/src/data/types.ts`** (no tables/DTOs/endpoints). **Backend-backed exceptions:** Pantry (`/api/pantry`, `pantry_item`, mezo-9xu), **Recipes (`/api/recipe` + `GET /api/recipe/{id}/breakdown` + `POST /api/recipe/workshop/turn`, `recipe` + `recipe_ingredient`, mezo-lns + mezo-bw3y + mezo-92pb)**, **Meal logging (`/api/fuel/day/{date}` + `/api/meal[/{id}]` + `POST /api/meal/ai-draft` + `GET /api/recipe/{id}/logs` + `POST/DELETE /api/water-log`, `meal` + `meal_item` + `water_log`, mezo-arb + mezo-0z5 + mezo-78rn)**, **Medication (`/api/medication` + `…/{id}/dose`, `medication` + `medication_dose`, mezo-d94)**, **Stack/Protocol (`GET/POST /api/fuel/protocol` + `GET/POST /api/fuel/intake` + `DELETE /api/fuel/intake/entry/{id}`, `protocol` + `protocol_item` + `supplement_intake`, mezo-09g)**, **Pantry-Import (`GET /api/pantry-import/lookup` + `POST /api/pantry-import` + `POST /api/pantry-import/scrape` + `POST /api/pantry-import/photo`, `pantry_import`, mezo-bka + mezo-8vum + mezo-d8tr)**, and **Meal-slot-templates (`GET/PUT/DELETE /api/fuel/slot-templates[/{dayType}]` + `POST /api/fuel/slot-templates/evaluate`, `meal_slot_template`, mezo-7102)**.

**Pantry-Import contract (Fuel P6, mezo-bka — spec [`2026-07-05-fuel-p6-pantry-import-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-05-fuel-p6-pantry-import-design.md)).** Rides the `api/feature/pantry/pantry.yml` fragment under a separate **`PantryImport`** tag → its own generated `PantryImportApi`, so the whole feature is bean-gated by `mezo.feature.pantry-import.enabled` (`PantryImportController` + `OffClient` `@ConditionalOnProperty`; off → 404, while `GET /api/pantry` keeps composing feed + suggestions). The paths live **outside `/api/pantry/*` on purpose** — a static segment there would fall into the `PUT/DELETE /api/pantry/{id}` pattern when the switch is off (405, not 404); same collision-avoidance as the fuel-intake `entry` segment. Endpoints: **`GET /api/pantry-import/lookup?q=`** (`lookupPantryItem`) proxies **OpenFoodFacts** — all-digit `q` (≥8) → v2 barcode fetch, else the v1 text search; results map to `PantryLookupResult` drafts (per-100 basis, `nova` = OFF `nova_group` **passthrough** — deterministic, not AI; rows without name or kcal are dropped, unknown barcode → honest-empty, OFF failure → 502 `PANTRY_IMPORT_LOOKUP_FAILED`). **`POST /api/pantry-import`** (`importPantryItem`, 201) persists the confirmed `PantryImportRequest` draft: a `pantry_item` (kind `food`, source **`openfoodfacts`** — the new 11th source enum value, DB CHECK + contract enums + FE `PantrySourceKey` in lockstep) **and** a `pantry_import` feed row in one tx. `PantryResponse` gained **required `imports[]` + `suggestions[]`**: `PantryImportEntryResponse {id, source, when, items, status, ofWhat}` (pins the FE `PantryImport` shape; newest-first, capped by `mezo.pantry-import.feed-size`) and `PantrySuggestionResponse {name, source, price, reason}` (computed per read by `PantrySuggestionService` — cheaper-alternative within a category on the same `priceUnit` basis at `mezo.pantry-suggestion.cheaper-ratio`, + low-NOVA swap (NOVA≥3 → ≤2 neighbour); capped `max-items`; **no table** — deterministic over the user's own rows). One owned table (`202607051415_mezo-bka_pantry_import.sql`): **`pantry_import`** (`source` CHECK, `item_name`, `item_count`, `status synced|manual-review`, `barcode`, `pantry_item_id` FK **ON DELETE SET NULL** — the feed survives item deletion, `imported_at`; + `created_by`/`is_deleted`/`created_at`). `OffClient` is the project's **first outbound HTTP client** (Spring `RestClient` via the new `spring-boot-starter-restclient` dep; UA/timeout/page-size from `PantryImportProperties` `mezo.pantry-import.*`) — and WireMock entered the test stack with it (`wiremock-standalone`, test scope), per the integration_test_framework "first real external API" rule.

**URL-scrape contract (mezo-8vum — spec [`2026-07-18-fuel-url-scrape-import-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-url-scrape-import-design.md), ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)).** A new **`PantryScrape`** tag in the SAME `pantry.yml` fragment → its own generated `PantryScrapeApi`, bean-gated by `mezo.feature.pantry-scrape.enabled` (`PantryScrapeController` + the whole pipeline `@ConditionalOnProperty`; off → 404). **`POST /api/pantry-import/scrape`** (`scrapePantryItem`) takes `PantryScrapeRequest {url}` and returns `PantryScrapeResponse { result? }` — `result: null` is honest-empty (the page loaded but carries no nutrition facts, NOT an error). `PantryScrapeResult` is the P6 lookup field-set **extended** with `category?`, `priceHuf?`, `priceUnit?`, the derived `source`, `sourceUrl`, `confidence` (0–1) and `needsReview` (the server-side threshold verdict, so the FE never duplicates the config). The pipeline: `PantryScrapeService` orchestrates `WebPageClient.fetch` (browser-like UA + `Accept-Language`, timeout, streaming max-body cap, http/https-only + public-host SSRF guard, redirects **never followed**) → `HtmlNutritionStripper.strip` (jsoup drops chrome + flattens nutrition tables to `label: value`, capped at 24k chars) → `ScrapeExtractionService.extract` (one strict-JSON prompt over the consumer-owned `ScrapeLlm` port; the shared Jackson-3 `ObjectMapper` parses it) → `ScrapeDraftValidator.confidence` (deterministic: −0.3 missing macros, −0.4 Atwater kcal-vs-4P+4C+9F beyond 30%, −0.2 NOVA/kcal out of range). **`source` is derived server-side from the URL host** (`PantryScrapeService.sourceFor` — known shop → its value, unknown/malformed → the generic `web`, never trusted from the client or the model). `PantrySource` is now a **shared 13-value schema** (added `gymbeam.hu` + `web`; `PantryImportRequest`/`PantryLookupResult`/`PantrySuggestion` all `$ref` it — the mezo-w3o lockstep: contract enum + both DB CHECKs + FE `PantrySourceKey`). **`POST /api/pantry-import` gained optional `sourceUrl` + `confidence` + `priceHuf`/`priceUnit`:** `PantryImportService` re-derives `source` from `sourceUrl` (never the client), persists the URL + price columns, and a `confidence <= mezo.pantry-scrape.confidence-threshold` (boundary-inclusive — a >30%-off Atwater draft scores exactly the threshold) writes the feed row `manual-review` instead of `synced`. Errors: `PANTRY_SCRAPE_FETCH_FAILED` (502), `PANTRY_SCRAPE_EXTRACT_FAILED` (502), `PANTRY_SCRAPE_LLM_UNAVAILABLE` (503 — companion off). Migration `202607181100_mezo-8vum_pantry_scrape.sql`: both `source` CHECKs +2 values + `pantry_import.source_url text`.

**Photo-import contract (mezo-d8tr — spec [`2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import-design.md)).** A new **`PantryPhoto`** tag in the SAME `pantry.yml` fragment → its own generated `PantryPhotoApi`, bean-gated by `mezo.feature.pantry-photo.enabled` (`PantryPhotoController` `@ConditionalOnProperty`; the path is its own segment so off → 404, no collision). **`POST /api/pantry-import/photo`** (`photoExtractPantryItem`) is `multipart/form-data`: a required `photo` (the nutrition-label shot) + an optional `photo2` (front-of-pack) → **reuses the scrape slice's `PantryScrapeResponse`/`PantryScrapeResult` schema verbatim** (`result: null` = honest-empty, no legible nutrition table — since mezo-a74c ONLY `kcal` decides; an unreadable **name** no longer drops the draft: it comes back with `name: ""` and the FE save-guard blocks „Polcra" until the user types one, with the „+ előlap fotó" re-extract as the assisted path; both photo inputs carry the same 5 MB client pre-check, and the confirm derives `origin` from the DRAFT source, not the toggled mode — mezo-iqf9). `PantryPhotoService` runs ONE multimodal call over the consumer-owned `PhotoExtractLlm` port (a new multi-image `CompanionLlm` overload, companion adapter `PantryPhotoLlmAdapter`; companion off → 503 `PANTRY_PHOTO_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`), reuses `ScrapeDraftValidator` for the same deterministic (boundary-inclusive) confidence, and **hard-sets the draft basis to per-100 g/grams server-side** regardless of the model's answer (the mezo-y9ga structural invariant — §9). `PantrySource` widened to its 14th value **`photo`**, and **`PantryImportRequest` gained `origin` (nullable, pattern `^photo$`)** — the confirm step carries `origin: 'photo'` (no `sourceUrl`) through the SAME `POST /api/pantry-import` path; `PantryImportService` maps it straight to `source: 'photo'` on both `pantry_item` and the `pantry_import` feed row. Errors: `PANTRY_PHOTO_EXTRACT_FAILED` (502, unparseable), `PANTRY_PHOTO_LLM_UNAVAILABLE` (503). Migration `202607231400_mezo-d8tr_pantry_photo_source.sql`: both `source` CHECKs +1 value.

**Stack / Protocol contract (mezo-09g) — the FIRST Fuel-owned `fuel.yml` fragment.** The contract lives in **`api/feature/fuel/fuel.yml`** → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE types in `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (`Protocol{Response,ViewResponse,ItemResponse,ItemCreateRequest,ItemPatchRequest,HistoryEntry}`, `Intake{Request,Response,ListResponse}`) + BE `FuelApi`/`api.dto`. **There is no whole-selection activate endpoint** — the pre-vx9v `POST /api/fuel/protocol` (`activateProtocol`) + `ProtocolActivateRequest {selectedPantryItemIds[≥1], reason?}` was retired in Task 10 (mezo-vx9v); `ProtocolResponse.items` is now a required field instead. Endpoints (controller `FuelController` implements the generated `FuelApi`):
- **`GET /api/fuel/protocol`** (`getProtocol`) → `ProtocolViewResponse` (`{ active?, history[] }`) — `active` is **absent when no protocol exists** (honest-empty; the FE maps that to the version-0 ghost).
- **`POST /api/fuel/protocol/items`** (`addProtocolItem`, 201) — adds one occurrence to the living protocol (`ProtocolItemCreateRequest {pantryItemId, slotKey?, dose?}`); lazily creates the single living `protocol` row (`ensureActive`) on the FIRST occurrence write, engine-places the zone when `slotKey` is omitted (`PlacementEngine`), and version-bumps (`touch`).
- **`PATCH /api/fuel/protocol/items/{id}`** (`patchProtocolItem`) / **`DELETE /api/fuel/protocol/items/{id}`** (`deleteProtocolItem`, 204) — move/re-dose/(un)pin, or remove, one occurrence; each mutation `touch()`es the protocol's version. See §5/§9 (occurrence CRUD, mezo-vx9v Task 1-3) for the full rule table.
- **`GET /api/fuel/intake/{date}`** (`listIntakes`) → `IntakeListResponse` — the day's supplement intakes (feeds `useStack`'s `taken` derivation; the P5 timeline reuses this read).
- **`POST /api/fuel/intake`** (`logIntake`, 201) — logs one intake (`IntakeRequest {pantryItemId, takenAt?, dose?, slotKey?, note?}`; the dose snapshot defaults from the pantry item. The **FE always sends an offset-bearing `takenAt`** (browser wall-clock + local UTC offset, via `nowOffsetIso`/`offsetIso` in `shared/lib/dates.ts` — same rationale as `LogDoseSheet`'s `toOffsetIso` for medication doses) so the server's day key `takenDate = takenAt.toLocalDate()` matches the browser's calendar day; the server's UTC-now default is a fallback for API-direct callers only).
- **`DELETE /api/fuel/intake/entry/{id}`** (`deleteIntake`, 204) — soft-deletes an intake entry (the `entry` path segment **avoids the `/{date}` path-param collision** on `/api/fuel/intake`).

Three **owned** tables (`202607021300_mezo-09g_create_protocol_supplement_intake.sql`): **`protocol`** (the single LIVING protocol row's metadata ONLY — `version`, `built_at`, `status active|superseded`, `confidence`, `last_replan_reason`; created lazily on the first occurrence write and version-bumped in place by every occurrence mutation — since Task 10 there is no more whole-selection activate that supersedes+re-creates it); **`protocol_item`** (the join → `pantry_item`, extended mezo-vx9v with `slot_key`/`dose`/`pinned`/`placement_source`/`placement_reason`/`rest_day_fallback` — one row per occurrence, `protocol_id` FK + `pantry_item_id` FK, so the protocol's contents are a real relation, not a jsonb blob); **`supplement_intake`** (an **append-only taken-ledger** FK-ing `pantry_item` — `pantry_item_id`, `taken_at timestamptz` + a derived `taken_date date` for the day-key index, a `dose` snapshot, optional `slot_key`/`note`; the `medication_dose` ledger precedent). All three carry `created_by` (app-level ownership filter) + `is_deleted` soft-delete.

**Backend zone placement — `PlacementEngine` (mezo-vx9v; ADR [0017](../decisions/0017-living-occurrence-protocol.md)).** Whenever `addProtocolItem`/`patchItem`(unpin)/the lazy backfill needs a `slot_key` and none was given explicitly, `ProtocolService` asks `PlacementEngine.place(PantryItemEntity)` (`feature/fuel/service/PlacementEngine.java`), which resolves in **four ordered stages**, first hit wins: **(1) the deterministic name-substring rule table** (`PlacementRules.RULES` — ~9 ordered rules, e.g. `kreatin`/`creatine` → `wake`, `pwo`/`pre-workout`/`aakg`/`arginin`/… → `pre_workout` with `restDayFallback: skip`, `whey`/`protein`/`fehérje` → `post_workout` with `restDayFallback: breakfast`, `magn`/`magné` → `evening`, `d3`/`k2`/`omega`/… → `lunch`; each rule also carries the Hungarian `reasonHu` explanation surfaced in `StackItemSheet` and an optional `dailyTotalHintHu` dosage note); **(2) the pantry item's own `timing` field** (`PlacementRules.zoneForTiming` — `morning→wake`, `midday→lunch`, `evening→evening`, `dinner→dinner`, `pre-workout→pre_workout`, a `weekly*` cadence → `wake`); **(3) an LLM fallback** for a supplement the rule table doesn't recognize — the consumer-owned **`StackPlacementLlm`** port (`feature/fuel/service`, ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)) with the companion-side `StackPlacementLlmAdapter`, gated at the bean boundary on **both** `mezo.feature.stack-placement-llm.enabled` AND the companion switch (either off ⇒ an empty `ObjectProvider<StackPlacementLlm>` ⇒ stage 3 is skipped, no manual flag read); a strict-JSON one-shot prompt returns `{slotKey, reasonHu}`, validated against `StackZone.fromKey` before use, any parse/validation failure logged and swallowed (never surfaces as an error); **(4) the honest `breakfast` fallback** (`source: 'fallback'`, a generic „Bizonytalan besorolás" reason) when even the LLM stage is unavailable or fails. Each placement records its `source` (`rule|llm|user|fallback` — `user` is set only by an explicit move/pin, never by `place()` itself) and `reasonHu` onto the occurrence, which is exactly what `StackItemSheet`'s "why it landed here" line reads. **`PlacementEngine.dailyTotalHint(name)`** is a SEPARATE, read-time-only lookup (not persisted) over the same rule table's `dailyTotalHintHu` field — `ProtocolService.toItemResponse` calls it fresh on every read so the hint always reflects the current rule table, never a frozen snapshot. The mock layer mirrors only rule-table stage (1)+(2) — `mockPlaceOccurrence` (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`) — since the mock has no LLM stage; the seed `protocolOccurrences` array was hand-placed once to match what the real rule table produces (§9).

**Owner protocol seed (`ProtocolSeedData`, mezo-67rb; reworked onto occurrence ops mezo-vx9v Task 10).** `feature/fuel/ProtocolSeedData` (`@Profile("demofixtures")` since S2 `mezo-qw37.2` — opt-in owner fixture, no longer a prod seed; `@Order(65)` — after `PantryCatalogLoader` at 60) seeds the owner's two **real** stim products — „Tasty Dose gombakávé" (`timing morning`) + „Origin PWO" (`timing pre-workout`), both `kind='stim'` / `category='supplement'` / `caffeine=true` — **by-name-idempotently** (an item whose name the owner still has — however its stock/notes are edited — is never re-seeded; a rename would re-seed it), then **adds each as an engine-placed living-protocol occurrence via `ProtocolService.addItem`, only when the owner has no active protocol yet** (an existing active protocol is never touched): the rule table places „Tasty Dose gombakávé" (matches the `kávé` needle) at `wake` and „Origin PWO" (matches the `pwo` needle) at `pre_workout` — there is no more whole-selection activate step or seed `reason` string. Since S2 this is **not** a prod seed: a plain `demodata` boot seeds no protocol, so every registered account starts with an empty Stack. IT: `ProtocolSeedDataIT` (4 cases, asserting both placements + idempotence).

**Meal-slot-template contract (mezo-7102, PR 1 of 2 — spec [`2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md)).** Rides the SAME `api/feature/fuel/fuel.yml` fragment (tag `Fuel`, `FuelController` — no new tag/controller) as Stack/Protocol above, and is **ungated** the same way (no `mezo.feature.*` switch). Endpoints (`SlotTemplateService`, `feature/fuel/service`):
- **`GET /api/fuel/slot-templates`** (`listSlotTemplates`) → `SlotTemplateListResponse { templates[] }` — all of the owner's saved templates (0–3 rows; a day type with no row stays on the automatic recommendation).
- **`PUT /api/fuel/slot-templates/{dayType}`** (`putSlotTemplate`, `SlotTemplateRequest { slots[] }`) → `SlotTemplateResponse` — whole-document upsert, found-or-created by `(created_by, dayType)`; 400 `VALIDATION_INVALID_VALUE` on a bad `dayType` or a malformed slot (a `fixed` anchor missing `time`, a relative anchor missing `offsetMin`), **`FUEL_SLOT_TEMPLATE_BUDGET_SUM`** when Σ`budgetPct` is off by more than 1, **`FUEL_SLOT_TEMPLATE_ANCHOR_INVALID`** for a `training_start`/`training_end` anchor on a `rest`-type template (`SlotTemplateService.validateSlots` — collect-then-throw, `error_handling.md`, so every problem surfaces in one response).
- **`DELETE /api/fuel/slot-templates/{dayType}`** (`deleteSlotTemplate`, 204) — soft-deletes the row (404 `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` if none exists) — the FE's **"Ajánlott visszaállítása"**.

One **owned** table (`202608051000_mezo-7102_create_meal_slot_template.sql`): **`meal_slot_template`** (`day_type varchar(11)` `ck` `rest|training_am|training_pm`; `slots jsonb NOT NULL` — the typed `MealSlotJson` list [`label`/`slotKind`/`role`/`anchorType`/`time`/`offsetMin`/`budgetPct`], `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` onto `MealSlotTemplateEntity#slots` — the Train provenance-envelope pattern again; `uq_meal_slot_template_user_day_type on (created_by, day_type) where is_deleted = false`; `created_by`/`is_deleted`/`created_at`/`updated_at`). Day type is resolved **client-side per read** (`resolveDayType`, §9) — never stored on a day or on the row itself; the row is keyed purely by `day_type`. **PR 2 of this slice ships the Tier-2 `POST /api/fuel/slot-templates/evaluate` LLM-verdict endpoint as its own contract, directly below.**

**Slot-plan evaluate contract (mezo-7102, PR 2 of 2 — spec [`2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md) §4).** A new **`FuelSlotPlan`** tag in the SAME `fuel.yml` fragment → its own generated `FuelSlotPlanApi`, so `SlotPlanEvaluateController` is `@ConditionalOnProperty`-gated on `mezo.feature.slot-template-ai.enabled` **independently** of the unconditional `FuelController` (the slot-template CRUD above stays ungated). **`POST /api/fuel/slot-templates/evaluate`** (`evaluateSlotPlan`) is **stateless** — `CurrentUserId` is used ONLY as the auth gate, nothing is persisted or read per-user — takes `SlotPlanEvaluateRequest {dayType, slots[2..8], resolvedTimes[]?, budget{kcal,p,c,f}, balanceKcal, blocks[]?}` (the FE's current draft + resolved anchor times + the day's macro budget/training blocks) and returns `SlotPlanEvaluateResponse {verdict: ok|adjust, summary, suggestions[]}`. `SlotPlanEvaluationService` runs ONE cheap-tier call over the consumer-owned **`SlotPlanLlm`** port (`feature/fuel/service`, ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md); companion adapter `SlotPlanLlmAdapter`, two-switch-gated on **both** `slot-template-ai` and the companion switch — the `StackPlacementLlmAdapter` idiom, §4 above) inside an `LlmCallContext("slot_template", "evaluate", …)`; the `SYSTEM_PROMPT` (marker `SLOT-TERV-ERTEKELES`) demands a strict-JSON Hungarian object, and the service **null-normalizes** the answer (`suggestions` null/omitted → `[]`, dropping any entry with a blank `text`) and treats a null/blank `verdict`/`summary` as a parse failure rather than a valid empty answer — both that case and any LLM/adapter exception collapse to a clean **503 `FUEL_SLOT_TEMPLATE_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`** (`requireAvailable()`'s empty-`ObjectProvider` path when the companion switch is off degrades the same way). **Switch off → 405, not 404:** the fixed `/evaluate` path segment falls through to the still-mapped `PUT`/`DELETE /api/fuel/slot-templates/{dayType}` pattern on `FuelController` (Spring matches the URI with `dayType="evaluate"` but finds no `POST` handler there → `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`) — the same collision-avoidance precedent as `MealAiDraftSwitchOffApiIT`. `FakeCompanionLlm` (the `companion-fake` test profile) mirrors the marker (`SLOT_PLAN_MARKER_MIRROR`) and scripts verdicts via a `[fake-slot-plan:{json}]` sentinel — planted in a `ResolvedSlotTime.label` (200-char cap) rather than a `SlotTemplateSlot.label` (40-char cap, too short for a JSON sentinel). ITs: `SlotPlanEvaluateApiIT` (happy path + the scripted sentinel + null-suggestions normalization, companion-fake), `SlotPlanEvaluateLlmUnavailableApiIT` (503, companion off), `SlotPlanEvaluateSwitchOffApiIT` (405, flag off).

**Fuel-settings contract (mezo-53su — the SECOND Fuel-owned fragment; spec [`2026-07-23-fuel-slot-timing-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-fuel-slot-timing-design.md) §3/§4).** A per-user **planner-settings singleton** relocating the eating cadence + caffeine cutoff off the weight goal / habit config into a Fuel-owned home — the `intention_creed`/`sleep_goal` shape, third instance. The contract lives in its own fragment **`api/feature/fuel-settings/fuel-settings.yml`** (tag **`FuelSettings`** → generated `FuelSettingsApi`, `FuelSettingsController implements FuelSettingsApi`, **gated on `mezo.feature.fuel-settings.enabled`**) → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE types `FuelSettingsResponse`/`SetFuelSettingsRequest`:
- **`GET /api/fuel/settings`** (`getFuelSettings`) → `FuelSettingsResponse { mealsPerDay (int 3..6), caffeineCutoff (HH:mm) }` — **never 404s**: `FuelSettingsService.getSettings` returns the row or a **config-ghost** (`4` / `"14:00"` from `FuelSettingsProperties` `mezo.fuel-settings.default-*`), so every user has working planner settings.
- **`PUT /api/fuel/settings`** (`setFuelSettings`, `SetFuelSettingsRequest`) → `FuelSettingsResponse` — find-or-create upsert; 400 (`VALIDATION_INVALID_VALUE` on mealsPerDay out of 3..6 or a non-HH:mm cutoff — contract-generated validation only), 401.

One **owned** table (`202607231933_mezo-53su_create_fuel_settings.sql`): **`fuel_settings`** (`meals_per_day int` `ck` 3..6, `caffeine_cutoff varchar(5)`; `created_by`/`is_deleted`/`created_at`; partial-unique `uq_fuel_settings_user on (created_by) where is_deleted = false`). The caffeine cutoff also has an **UNGATED read seam** — `CaffeineCutoffPort` (interface `LocalTime resolve(UUID)`) + `CaffeineCutoffResolver` (row → parse, else ghost from properties) — deliberately **not** `@ConditionalOnProperty` so the habit `no_stim_after` metric resolves even when the fuel-settings surface is off (the `SleepAnchorResolver` idiom, §5). `HabitProperties.caffeineCutoff` + its `mezo.habit.caffeine-cutoff` yml key were **removed** — one cutoff, three consumers (the Mai `.fuelchips` chip, the day plan, the habit metric).

**Medication contract (mezo-d94).** The contract lives in `api/feature/medication/medication.yml` → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE types in `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (`Medication{Request,Response,DayResponse,DoseRequest,DoseResponse}`, `MedicationCycle{Config,Response,Cell}`, `MedicationPhase`) + BE `MedicationApi`/`api.dto`. Endpoints (controller `MedicationController` implements the generated `MedicationApi`):
- **`GET /api/medication`** (`getMedicationDay`) → `MedicationDayResponse` (`{ medication, cycle, recentDoses }`) — feeds the whole Gyógyszer tab in one round-trip; `cycle`/`recentDoses` are **derived/queried**, never client-supplied.
- **`PUT /api/medication/{id}`** (`updateMedication`) → edits the owned definition + cycle config (`MedicationRequest` → `MedicationResponse`).
- **`POST /api/medication/{id}/dose`** (`logDose`, 201) — logs one actual injection (`MedicationDoseRequest` `{ administeredAt?, dose, note? }` → `MedicationDoseResponse`); a null `administeredAt` defaults server-side to now.
- **`DELETE /api/medication/{id}/dose/{doseId}`** (`deleteDose`, 204) — soft-deletes a mis-entered dose.

Two **owned** tables (`202606261200_mezo-d94_create_medication.sql`): **`medication`** (the drug master — `name`, `active_ingredient`, `route`, `cadence`, `default_dose`, `dose_unit`, `is_active`, + a `cycle jsonb NOT NULL` envelope = `cycleLengthDays` + a `phases[]` of `{key:peak|stable|trough, fromDay, toDay, label}`, mapped via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` onto the typed `MedicationCycleJson` record — the Train provenance-envelope pattern); **`medication_dose`** (an append-only injection ledger — `medication_id` FK `ON DELETE RESTRICT`, `administered_at timestamptz` + a derived `administered_date date` for the cycle lookup index, `dose`, `note`). Both carry `created_by` (app-level ownership filter) + `is_deleted` soft-delete. The **cycle is derived, not stored**: `MedicationCycleService.derive(userId, med, onDate)` finds the newest dose at-or-before `onDate` and projects it — `cycleDay = days-since-last-dose + 1` (1-based, **clamped** to `cycleLengthDays`), phase from the config `phases[]`, a `week[]` of `MedicationCycleCell`s with the current cell flagged; **no dose → honest-zero** (`cycleDay 0`, null `lastDoseAt`, ghost week, never a fabricated day) — since `mezo-lwmq` this is the standing state: no medication row exists at all (§4). The Meal contract lives in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml` → merged → FE types in `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (`Meal{Request,Response}`, `MealItem{Request,Response}`, `FuelDayResponse`, `Macros`, `MacroSet`, `MealScore`, `RecipeLog{Response,ListResponse}`, `WaterLog{Request,Response}`) + BE `MealApi`/`api.dto`. `meal_item` is **polymorphic** — a plain-UUID FK to `recipe` OR `pantry_item` (`ON DELETE RESTRICT`) + a `source` discriminator + an exactly-one-of CHECK + a frozen per-basis macro snapshot **+ a nullable `recipe_overrides jsonb` (mezo-ormb, recipe-arm only) — `null` means "the recipe as written"; a non-null value is a self-describing delta of only the genuinely changed lines, typed `List<MealItemRecipeOverrideJson>` (`lineOrder`, `pantryItemId`, `name`, `unit`, `originalAmount`, `amount`), keyed by `lineOrder` with `pantryItemId` as a consistency check (§9); the item's own `snapshot*` macros/`snapshotNova` above are already computed FROM this override set, so a meal freezes the recipe as eaten, not as written**. On the wire, `MealItemRequest` gained an optional recipe-arm-only **`ingredientOverrides[]`** (`{lineOrder, pantryItemId, amount}`, `amount` `minimum: 0` **inclusive** — deliberately unlike the sibling `amount` fields' `exclusiveMinimum: 0`, since `0` is the legal "left it out" value) and `MealItemResponse` gained the resolved, self-describing **`ingredientOverrides[]`** (`{lineOrder, pantryItemId, name, unit, originalAmount, amount}`), so the meal detail can explain what was adjusted and the payload round-trips through `updateMeal`; **since P7 (mezo-yta) `MealScore.breakdown` is a TYPED contract schema** (`MealBreakdown` + `MealScoreDimension` with per-kind payloads — no more `additionalProperties: true`), populated at write by `MealScoringService`: the envelope is also the MICRO SNAPSHOT (nutrition-quality rows computed from the live sources and frozen into the jsonb), `RecipeLogResponse` carries the meal's `score`, and `recipe.yml` `novaDominant` converged to `integer` (mezo-2dy). **Since mezo-jcpt.1 `MealScoreDimension` carries a nullable `note`** — a 1-2 sentence per-dimension AI prose socket, distinct from the top-level `summary`/`improve[]`; `MealScoringService` always emits it `null` (`Dim.toJson()`), and `MealCoachService`/`MealCoachStore.mergeDimensionNotes` (below) is the only writer, matched by dimension id, id-mismatches dropped, and **never onto a weight-0 ("Nincs adat") dimension** even if the LLM's answer names one — enforced in code (`MealCoachStore.isDegraded`), not just prompted. The older `MacroDetail.notes` field is **`@Deprecated`, superseded by `Dimension.note`, and no longer written or read**. **Since mezo-m6uv ([ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-freeze-nutrient-facts-per-line.md)) the four nutrition-quality facts — `fiberG`/`sugarG`/`saltG`/`saturatedFatG` — are frozen per line too**, on a shared `Nutrients` schema defined once here and cross-fragment `$ref`'d from `recipe.yml` (the `MealBreakdown` merge precedent): an optional, all-nullable `nutrients` object on `RecipeResponse`, `RecipeIngredientResponse`, `MealResponse` and `MealItemResponse`. This **supersedes the earlier live-pantry-read rule** that used to live in `RecipeService.fitLines` and `MealService`'s now-deleted `recipeFacts`/`pantryFacts`/`addFact` trio — the scorer now reads the SAME frozen snapshot the wire serves, with the SAME `amount/snapshotPer` factor the macro contribution already uses, so there is exactly one route to these four numbers. Null semantics extend the macro snapshot's "no fabricated zero" rule to partial sums: a line's field is `null` iff its source pantry item carried no value (never coerced to 0), and a rollup field is `null` iff **every** contributing line is `null` — otherwise it is the sum of the known ones. Storage/summation is at **three decimals** HALF_UP (`RecipeMapper.scaledGram`); the ADR's rounding decision supersedes the design spec's original one-decimal-storage rule (dropped mid-implementation — a per-line one-decimal round compounded across lines). One-decimal HU-comma display (`—` for null, `<0,1` for a positive value that would otherwise round to a misleading `0`) is frontend-only, in `frontend/src/shared/lib/grams.ts`'s `formatGram`. NOVA and the pantry `category` deliberately stay LIVE reads (freezing NOVA is the sibling `mezo-4tzf`). Macros are computed at read (`factor = amount/snapshot_per`, whole-number HALF_UP round; meal = Σ line contributions; day `consumed` = Σ meal macros vs config `nutrition_targets`).

**AI meal-draft contract (mezo-78rn — spec [`2026-07-18-fuel-ai-meal-log-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-ai-meal-log-design.md), ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)).** Extends the SAME `meal.yml` fragment (no new fragment) under a new **`MealAiLog`** tag → its own generated `MealAiLogApi`, bean-gated by `mezo.feature.meal-ai-log.enabled` (`MealAiDraftController` + `MealAiDraftService` `@ConditionalOnProperty`; **off → 405**, see §9). **`POST /api/meal/ai-draft`** (`draftMealFromAi`) is `multipart/form-data`: `date` (required), `text?` (≤2000 chars), `photo?` (binary); at least one of text/photo → else 400. It returns `MealAiDraftResponse { slot (breakfast|lunch|dinner|snack), title?, note?, items[] }` where each `MealAiDraftItem` carries `source (recipe|pantry|estimate)`, `pantryItemId?`/`recipeId?` (validated + owned — a hallucinated id is demoted, warn-logged, never 500, and — since mezo-qrks — first re-tried through the `PantryNameIndex` name+unit match before falling to `estimate`; see §9), `name`, `amount`, `unit`, the per-basis snapshot (`per`, `basisUnit`, `kcal`, `proteinG`, `carbsG`, `fatG`, `nova?`), `confidence` (0–1) and `needsReview` (server verdict — **never** the LLM's self-assessment). Empty `items` = nothing recognized (honest empty, not an error). **`MealItemRequest` gained the `estimate` arm** (`source` pattern `recipe|pantry|estimate` + nullable `name`/`per`/`basisUnit`/`kcal`/`proteinG`/`carbsG`/`fatG`/`nova` — the FK-less line supplies its own verbatim snapshot; `MealService.rebuildItems` validates all-present-or-400 and persists as-is) and **`MealRequest` gained an optional `provenance`** (`MealProvenance { origin manual|ai-text|ai-photo, model?, confidence?, rawText? }`). Backend: `MealAiDraftService` assembles the pantry+recipe catalog prompt from the owner's own rows (via the existing meal→pantry/recipe read seam — no package cycle), makes ONE cheap-tier call over the `MealDraftLlm` port (text-only or the multimodal photo overload), parses first-`{`-to-last-`}` JSON (unparseable → `MEAL_AI_EXTRACT_FAILED` 502), takes matched-line macros from the DB row (the LLM only picks id+amount), and scores estimate lines with `MealAiDraftValidator` (deterministic Atwater — mirror of `ScrapeDraftValidator`, boundary-inclusive `confidence <= threshold` → `needsReview`; per = amount so snapshots carry portion totals). The port is reached via `ObjectProvider<MealDraftLlm>` → companion off = clean 503 `MEAL_AI_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`. Two changesets: `202607181500_mezo-78rn_meal_item_estimate.sql` widens BOTH `ck_meal_item_source` (adds `estimate`) AND `ck_meal_item_arm` (the estimate arm forbids both FKs — §9); `202607181501_mezo-78rn_meal_provenance.sql` adds `meal.provenance jsonb` (typed `MealProvenanceJson`, `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)`, NULL for manual/legacy rows, no backfill). **Water logging (mezo-0z5)** rides the same `meal.yml` fragment + `MealController`: **`POST /api/water-log`** (`WaterLogRequest {date?, amountMl}` → 201, `date` defaults to server-today) appends one owned `water_log` row and **`DELETE /api/water-log/{id}`** soft-deletes it (the weight/sleep-log precedent — smallest owned single-row aggregate: `created_by`, `is_deleted`, `log_date`, `amount_ml`; migration `202607021200_mezo-0z5_create_water_log.sql`). `FuelDayService.consumed.water` = the day's Σ `amount_ml` (was hardcoded `= targets.water`), surfaced through the unchanged `FuelDayResponse.consumed.water` — goal/medication-aware water **targets** stay a config constant (deferred). **The 7-day rollup (Fuel P4, mezo-kpo) rides the same `meal.yml` fragment: `GET /api/fuel/week/{start}`** → `FuelWeekResponse { start, days[7] }`, each `FuelDayRollup { date, targets, consumed }` (no meal bodies — `FuelDayService.getWeek` loops the per-day consumed/water rollup) — feeds the Terv weekly stats and is the designated server aggregate for the Insights Weekly review (Phase-2 roadmap D′). **Since mezo-d20.7.2 the same response also carries two nullable week-level scalars for the week-centric Napló page** (audit gap #28, design-2.0 F6.2): **`mealScoreAvg`** — the mean of the week's SCORED meals' deterministic scores (0..1, mezo-yta), 3 decimals, from a scalar projection (`MealRepository.findScoresBetween`, `score is not null` filtered in SQL) — and **`weightAvgKg`** — the week's weight average in kg, 2 decimals, folded to ONE value per day first (that day's LATEST weigh-in, matching the day-level `findFirstBy…OrderByCreatedAtDesc` semantics, via the new `WeightLogRepository.findByCreatedByAndDeletedFalseAndDateBetweenOrderByDateAscCreatedAtAsc`) so a thrice-weighed day cannot out-vote the other six. **Both are DERIVED AT READ, not stored, and there is NO new table or changeset:** Fuel has no per-week row to hang them on, and both are pure functions of already-persisted data (`meal.score` — itself the denormalized scalar of the score envelope, ADR 0006 §4 — and `weight_log`); a week table would be a cache owing write-through on every meal edit/delete/re-score and every weigh-in, for no new information. **Both are nullable and honest**: an unscored week and a weigh-in-less week return `null`, never a 0-as-a-fake. This is the ONE cross-feature read `feature/meal` takes on `feature/biometrics.weight` — the ArchUnit slice rule stays cycle-free (nothing reachable from `biometrics` reaches `meal`). The Recipe contract lives in `api/feature/recipe/recipe.yml` → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE types in `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (`Recipe{Request,Response,ListResponse,IngredientRequest,IngredientResponse,Macros,Contribution,MezoFit}`) + BE `RecipeApi`/`api.dto`. Endpoints: `GET /api/recipe` (list), `GET /api/recipe/{id}`, `POST /api/recipe`, `PUT /api/recipe/{id}` (full-replace), `DELETE /api/recipe/{id}` (soft-delete cascade), **`GET /api/recipe/{id}/breakdown` (mezo-bw3y)** → `RecipeBreakdownResponse { breakdown: MealBreakdown|null, fitsFor[] }` (the envelope schema is a **cross-fragment `$ref`** onto meal.yml's `MealBreakdown` — the `SystemMessage` merge precedent): a lazy cache-or-generate read — fresh deterministic envelope every call, the persisted `recipe.breakdown` jsonb (migration `202607191100_mezo-bw3y_recipe_breakdown.sql`) served only while its numbers match, LLM prose (Hungarian `summary`/details/`improve`/`fitsFor`, strict-JSON over the recipe-owned `RecipeBreakdownLlm` port, companion `RecipeBreakdownLlmAdapter`, `mezo.feature.recipe-ai-score.enabled`) merged + persisted only on success (`fits_for` finally in real use — `mezoFit.fitsFor` carries it on subsequent reads); `PUT /api/recipe/{id}` nulls both cache columns. **Since mezo-uavr the recipe carries a template meal role** — `RecipeRequest.role` (optional, absent ⇒ `standard`, so an older client payload stays valid) / `RecipeResponse.role` (**required**, always populated), both a `pattern: '^(standard|pre_workout|post_workout)$'` **string**, deliberately not an OpenAPI `enum`: it mirrors the neighbouring `category` field, keeps the generated Java/TS types plain `String`, and avoids a third enum type in the mapping path. The wire values are snake_case; the column is `recipe.role varchar(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'STANDARD'` + `ck_recipe_role` **UPPER-case** (mapped `@Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)` onto the existing `feature/nutrition` `MealRole` — no new enum, and `feature/recipe` already depended on `feature/nutrition` for scoring), so **`RecipeMapper` is the ONE place the case flips** (`MealRole.valueOf(role.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT))` / `name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)`); an unknown value is rejected as a 400 by the contract pattern before it reaches code.
