Migration `202607271200_mezo-uavr_recipe_role.sql`; every pre-existing row defaults to `STANDARD` = the identity overlay, so no numbers move. `PUT` is a **full replace**: an update omitting `role` resets it to `STANDARD` (correct semantics, and why `recipeToInput` must carry the field — §2). `recipe_ingredient` references `pantry_item` by a plain-UUID FK (`ON DELETE RESTRICT`) plus a per-basis macro snapshot; macros are computed at read time (contribution = `amount/snapshot_per × snapshot macros`; whole-recipe = Σ contributions). **`recipe_ingredient` also carries the four frozen nutrition-quality columns since mezo-m6uv** (`snapshot_fiber_g`/`snapshot_sugar_g`/`snapshot_salt_g`/`snapshot_saturated_fat_g`, migration `202608111200_mezo-m6uv_recipe_ingredient_nutrient_snapshot.sql`) — the `nutrients` field on `RecipeResponse`/`RecipeIngredientResponse` reads off these, same `Nutrients` schema and freeze rule as the meal-side contract above ([ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-freeze-nutrient-facts-per-line.md)).

**Receptműhely turn contract (mezo-92pb — spec [`2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md), ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)).** A new **`RecipeWorkshop`** tag in the SAME `recipe.yml` fragment → its own generated `RecipeWorkshopApi`, bean-gated by `mezo.feature.recipe-workshop.enabled` (`RecipeWorkshopController` `@ConditionalOnProperty`; off → the bean is gone and, since this path has no `{id}`-collision sibling — unlike the meal-ai-draft 405 — Spring finds no mapping at all, so `GlobalExceptionHandler` answers the plain no-handler `RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND` 404). **`POST /api/recipe/workshop/turn`** (`workshopTurn`) is **stateless**: `WorkshopTurnRequest { message, goal?, history[] ≤20, draft? }` in, `WorkshopTurnResponse { reply, draft }` out — the client is the only state-holder (chat history + current draft round-trip every call), so a manual edit sitting in the sent `draft` naturally survives the round trip unless the model itself touches that line. `WorkshopDraft { name, category, servings, steps[] ≤20, lines[] ≤30 }`; each `WorkshopDraftLine { source: pantry|estimate, pantryItemId?, name, amount, unit, kcal?, proteinG?, carbsG?, fatG? }` — a `pantry` line's macro fields are always `null` on the wire (resolved live client-side, mirroring the recipe/meal convention that a pantry ref never carries its own macro snapshot on this contract), an `estimate` line carries the model's own totals for the STATED amount (not per-100). `RecipeWorkshopService.turn` builds ONE cheap-tier call (`LlmCallContext("recipe_workshop","turn")`) over the recipe-owned `RecipeWorkshopLlm` port (companion adapter `RecipeWorkshopLlmAdapter`, ADR 0012 — `feature/recipe` never imports `feature/companion`; port absent (companion off) → `ObjectProvider` empty → 503 `RECIPE_WORKSHOP_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`), with a Hungarian strict-JSON system prompt that embeds the owner's live pantry catalog (`id | name | brand | serving basis`, so the model can cite a real `pantryItemId` instead of inventing one) plus a **goal directive** when `goal` is one of the five presets (`high_protein`/`pre_workout`/`post_workout`/`before_bed`/`breakfast` — `GOAL_DIRECTIVES`, e.g. pre-workout: "gyors szénhidrát hangsúly, alacsony zsír, könnyen emészthető"). The user message carries the trailing `mezo.recipe-workshop.max-history-turns` (default 20) history turns + the current draft JSON + the new message. Answer parsing takes the first-`{`-to-last-`}` slice; unparseable → 502 `RECIPE_WORKSHOP_EXTRACT_FAILED`. **`RecipeWorkshopValidator.sanitize`** is the deterministic gate the LLM's draft always passes through before it reaches the wire (mirrors `MealAiDraftValidator`'s "the LLM proposes, this component decides" split): `category` falls back to `dinner` if not one of the four, `servings` clamps to `[1,12]`, `steps`/`lines` truncate to the configured caps (`mezo.recipe-workshop.max-steps`/`max-lines`, defaults 20/30 respectively) with a warn log on truncation. Per line — a `pantryItemId` that parses as a UUID **and** resolves against the caller's own `pantry_item` rows (`findByIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`) becomes a `pantry` line with the **DB row's name** (never the LLM's) and null macros; a malformed or hallucinated id **demotes to `estimate`** (warn-logged, never a 500); a line with no `kcal` or blank `name` (and no resolvable pantry id) is **dropped** outright rather than persisted as a fact-less estimate. `mezo.recipe-workshop.*` properties (`RecipeWorkshopProperties`, `max-lines`/`max-steps`/`max-history-turns`) are all `@Min(1)`-bounded config, no migration — the endpoint is stateless and persists nothing; saving a finished draft rides the existing `POST/PUT /api/recipe` CRUD unchanged. ITs: `RecipeWorkshopApiIT`, `RecipeWorkshopSwitchOffApiIT` (404), `RecipeWorkshopLlmUnavailableApiIT` (503); unit `RecipeWorkshopValidatorTest`.

Key FE shapes:

- **`FuelSlot`** (`types.ts:21-35`): one timeline entry — `kind: FuelKind` (`'wake'|'meal'|'midday'|'snack'|'preworkout'|'workout'|'sport'|'evening'`), **`slotKey?: MealSlot`** (the meal/snack window identity that survives from `PlannedWindow` since mezo-53su — absent on block/protocol/water slots; the slot actions read it instead of the deleted `slotKeyOfLabel` label-guess), **`state: 'done'|'now'|'pending'|'missed'`** (`'missed'` = a past unlogged meal window in the fixed plan, mezo-1oy5), optional `mealName`/`mezoNote`/`windowTip`, macros, `items: SlotItem[]` (`SlotItem` = `{type:'supplement', refId, label, done, primary?, note?}`).
- **`FuelPlanToday`** (`types.ts:36-42`): `workout`/`volleyball` blocks, `bedtime`/`kitchenClose`/`caffeineCutoff`, **`energy: {base,activity,balance,target}`** (the dynamic-energy breakdown feeding the "Mai cél" card, mezo-1oy5), `slots[]`.
- **`FuelSettings`** (`types.ts:43-46`): `{ mealsPerDay, caffeineCutoff }` — the Fuel-owned planner settings (mezo-53su), read by `useFuelSettings` and fed straight into `buildDayPlan` (`DayPlanInput.caffeineCutoff` is now required).
- **`MealDimension`** discriminated union (`types.ts:49-57`) — the eight weighted scoring dimensions (mezo-7797): `MacroDimension` (Kcal & makró arány — macroRatio/macroTargets/kcalShareOfDay), `MicroDimension` (Rost & mikro — **fiber-only** since mezo-7797, `micros[]` with `MicroStatus 'good'|'ok'|'low'`; sugar/salt/satFat moved OUT to WHO/Zsírminőség below), `NovaDimension` (food-processing class `NovaGroup 1|2|3|4`), `ContextDimension` (meal-side timing/slot rows), and the generic **`RowsDimension`** (`id: 'who'|'fat_quality'|'plant_diversity'|'energy_density'|'portion'`, same `context: {label,value}[]` row payload) carrying **Ajánlások · WHO** (free sugar ≤10 E% + salt 5 g/day scaled), **Zsírminőség** (satFat ≤10 E% + sat/total-fat share), **Növényi diverzitás**, **Energia-sűrűség**, and the template-only **Adag-arány** (portion). All share `MealDimensionBase` (id/label/weight/score/color/detail).
- **`MealBreakdown`** (`types.ts:46-52`): `confidence`, `summary`, `dimensions[]`, `improve[]`, `tools[]` (`ToolType 'read'|'compute'|'write'` — the transparency list). **This is the natural jsonb envelope** when Fuel lands (the `MealBreakdown` ⇒ meal `score` typed-jsonb mapping reuses Train's `ProvenanceEnvelope` pattern).
- **`FuelMeal`** (`types.ts`), **`FuelDay`**: `FuelDay` = `targets`/`consumed` (`MacroSet` incl. `water`), `meals[]`, `pacing`, `micronutrients`, `supplements` (`FuelSummary`). Since mezo-arb `FuelMeal` carries structured **`mealItems: MealItemLine[]`** (polymorphic logged lines — `{ source: 'recipe'|'pantry', refId, amount, unit, name, contribution{kcal,p,c,f}, nova? }`; `refId === recipeId|pantryItemId`, mirrors `RecipeIngredientLine`) plus real `loggedAt`/`mealDate`; the legacy free-text `items: string[]` is **kept** (the mock score-sheet seeds still read it). **`MealInput`** (`{ slot: MealSlot, loggedAt?, title?, items: MealItemInput[], provenance? }`) is the capture-sheet save payload → `mealApi.toRequest` → `MealRequest`. `MealSlot = 'breakfast'|'lunch'|'dinner'|'snack'`. **Since mezo-78rn** `MealItemSource` gained `'estimate'` and the editor line is the **discriminated `MealItemInput`** = `MealRefInputItem` (recipe/pantry `refId`) | `MealEstimateInputItem` (its own `name`/`per`/`basisUnit`/macros snapshot, no ref); `MealProvenanceInput { origin manual|ai-text|ai-photo, model?, confidence?, rawText? }` rides on `MealInput.provenance`. The AI-draft read shapes are **`MealAiDraft { slot, title, note, items: MealAiDraftLine[] }`** and **`MealAiDraftLine`** (mirrors `MealAiDraftItem` — `source`, `pantryItemId`/`recipeId`, snapshot macros, `confidence`, `needsReview`); the review sheet maps a confirmed `MealAiDraftLine` → the matching `MealItemInput` arm.
- **`SupplementStashItem`** (`types.ts`): `type 'supplement'|'stimulant'|'medication'`, category, dose, form, stock, protocol, timing, taken, `caffeine?`. Since **mezo-1za9** it also carries the same **nutrition + commerce** facts as `Ingredient` (`source?`, `per?`, `unit?`, `macros?`, `price?`, `priceUnit?`, `pkg?`, `micros?`, `nova?`, `fiberG?/sugarG?/saltG?/saturatedFatG?`) — all optional because pure dose/protocol items (many stim/med) have none. This closes the gap where the backend `SupplementStashResponse` dropped a supplement's macros/price so the Kamra card/detail showed nothing for it.
- **`Protocol`** (`types.ts:75-79`): `version`/`builtAt`/`source`/`status`/`itemCount`/`confidence`/`lastReplanReason`/`history[]`.
- **Pantry:** `Ingredient` (`stashRefId?`, `nova`, `micros`, `source: PantrySourceKey`), `PantryImport`/`PantrySuggestion`, unified **`PantryItem`** (`kind 'food'|'supplement'|'stim'|'med'`).
- **Recipe** (`types.ts`): `Recipe` with `ingredients: RecipeIngredientLine[]` (`{refId, amount, unit, note?, name?, contribution?}` — `refId === pantryItemId`, `name`/`contribution` server-computed), `macros`, `novaDominant`, `mezoFit.score: number | null`, `starred`, **`role: RecipeRole`** (`'standard'|'pre_workout'|'post_workout'`, mezo-uavr — the snake_case FE union mirroring the wire, cast in `recipeApi` exactly as `category` already is; HU copy lives in `features/fuel/logic/recipeRole.ts`, see §7), derived `recentLogs?`/`templateBreakdown?` (mock-only). **`RecipeInput`** is the editor save payload (`ingredients[].pantryItemId` lines, **`role` included**) → `recipeApi.toRequest` → `RecipeRequest`. `RecipeLog` is the per-meal log shape on `recentLogs`.
- **Weekly:** `MedCyclePhase`/`MedCycleDayCell`, `GymScheduleDay`, `WeeklySupplementRow`, `RecurringPattern`, `ReplanScenario`/`ReplanCascade` (cascade `system: 'Fuel'|'Train'|'Sleep'|'Insights'`, `types.ts:304`). `StackRecommendation` was deleted with `useStackRecommendations` (mezo-vx9v Task 8).
- **Retired (mezo-vx9v Task 9):** `ProtocolSlotItem`/`ProtocolSlotData`/`Reasoning`/`MealMatch`/`BuiltProtocol` — the selection-based `buildProtocol()` builder's own types, deleted from `data/types.ts` once `useFuelTimeline`/the notification writer/`NotificationsPage` all moved onto the occurrence-based `projectStackDay` below (zero remaining consumers, confirmed by grep).
- **Stack occurrences + zone timeline (mezo-vx9v):** `StackZoneKey` (the 8-zone wire contract: `wake|breakfast|pre_workout|post_workout|lunch|dinner|evening|bedtime`), `StackPlacementSource` (`rule|llm|user|fallback`), `ProtocolOccurrence` (`id`/`pantryItemId`/`slotKey`/`dose`/`pinned`/`placementSource`/`placementReason`/`restDayFallback`/`dailyTotalHint` — `types.ts`); `STACK_ZONE_ORDER`/`STACK_ZONE_LABEL` (`data/fuel/stackZones.ts`); `StackDayEntry`/`StackDaySlot`/`StackDayInput` (`features/fuel/logic/projectStackDay.ts` — the projection `useStackDay` feeds the page, incl. the optional `weightKg` that only shifts the peri-workout-snack threshold); `MealMatchSuggestion`/`MealMatchVerdict`/`MealMatchResult` (`features/fuel/logic/matchMealsToStack.ts`).
- Supporting: `data/nova.ts` (`NovaGroup`, `NOVA_META`, `STATUS_COLOR`), `data/pantrySources.ts` (`PantrySourceKey = 'kifli.hu'|'myprotein.hu'|'tesco.hu'|'auchan.hu'|'manual'`).

**Where the rest of the backend plugs in (contract-first):** the `api/feature/fuel/fuel.yml` fragment **exists** (mezo-09g: `GET/POST /api/fuel/protocol` + `/api/fuel/intake`); the day/week reads landed in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml` (`/api/fuel/day/{date}` mezo-arb, `/api/fuel/week/{start}` mezo-kpo) and the timeline stayed client-composed (P5) — all merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE types in `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` + BE `io.mrkuhne.mezo.api` interfaces/DTOs, per **[`docs/references/api_contract_conventions.md`](../references/api_contract_conventions.md)**. Remaining tables per the design spec: a fuel-timeline view, UUID PKs, `OwnedEntity` + `created_by`, soft delete, meal `score` as typed jsonb. (`pantry_item`/`recipe`/`meal`/`medication(_dose)`/`protocol`/`protocol_item`/`supplement_intake` are **already shipped**; `nutrition_targets` shipped as `@ConfigurationProperties`, not a table; the spec's `food_item` was superseded by `pantry_item`, Model B.)

**Goal day-planner settings (Fuel P5, mezo-9ys) ride the GOAL contract, not a Fuel table — but `mezo-dbsr` moved the day-anchor OUT to the sleep goal.** The three additive, nullable **goal** fields (`mealsPerDay` 3–6, `wakeTime`/`bedTime` `HH:mm`) still exist on `GoalRequest`/`GoalResponse` + the `goal` columns (migration `202607021500_mezo-9ys_goal_planner_settings.sql`, CHECK `meals_per_day between 3 and 6`), but the Mai timeline **no longer reads any of them**: `wake`/`bed` come from `useSleepGoal()` (the day-anchor owner, spec D3) and — since **mezo-53su** — `mealsPerDay` (+ caffeine cutoff) come from **Fuel settings** (`useFuelSettings`, its own `fuel_settings` table, below), so both `PLANNER_DEFAULTS` and `CAFFEINE_CUTOFF` were **deleted** from `data/fuel/fuelConfig.ts` and the goal's `mealsPerDay`/`wakeTime`/`bedTime` all **pass through unread** on the wire (kept for a later joint drop). The goal's `mealsPerDay` stays **contract passthrough** — no goal-engine involvement (see [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §4) — and since **mezo-53su NOTHING on the FE writes it**: `EditGoalSheet` **lost its whole "Napi ritmus" section** (the cadence now lives in `fuel_settings`, edited via `FuelSettingsPage` at `/fuel/settings`; `mezo-dbsr` had already moved the wake/bed rows to the Alvás page), and `useGoalActions().savePlanner` is **deleted**. `goalApi.update` + the `goalResponseToUpsert` toRequest mapper survive **orphaned-but-kept** (no caller) purely so the goal wire still round-trips the planner columns until the joint cleanup migration. See [`me.md`](me.md) §2. The daily **budget** comes from the goal's `prescription` current-week segment (`kcal`/`proteinG`), carbs/fat preferring that same segment's own prescribed `carbsG`/`fatG` since Diet Plan slice 1 (mezo-xwgb — the engine's split-preset fat-share + ISSN g/kg floor, protein-tier-aware), falling back to the fixed `FAT_KCAL_SHARE` split for a pre-slice segment, with the config `FuelDay.targets` as the final fallback (`deriveDailyBudget`, `features/fuel/logic/buildDayPlan.ts`). **This slice adds NO new Fuel endpoint** — the timeline is a client-side composition of already-real reads; the only backend touch is the three goal columns/contract fields.

**Prescription safety (`mezo-szsj`).** Fuel must never turn a contradictory weight goal into a daily kcal budget. The shared goal invariant rejects new/edited cut, bulk, maintain, and too-short goal combinations before rate derivation; `GoalEngineService.evaluate` additionally fail-closes pre-fix legacy rows by clearing both `prescription` and `tdeeBootstrap` before projection. Fuel therefore falls back through its existing no-prescription path rather than serving a deficit as a surplus (or the reverse). The exact invariant and typed error codes live in [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3.

**Day-type kcal shift (Diet Plan slice 3, `mezo-sxlj`) — the same budget, day-type-aware on BOTH surfaces.** A per-user `dayTypeShiftKcal` setting (0–500 kcal, `diet_settings.day_type_shift_kcal`, ghost 0 = uniform days — see [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §4) drives the goal engine to prescribe each segment's **`trainingDayKcal`/`restDayKcal`** alongside its existing uniform `kcal` (both nullable — null on both ⇔ uniform day; the split math + edge cases are in [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3). Both the backend `FuelDayService` (`targetSet`/`dailyTargets`) and the frontend `deriveDailyBudget` pick the date's number off that split rather than the segment's uniform `kcal`, and both derive the resulting carbs delta **at serve time, never stored** (§9 has the full decision + a worked example). **BE picks by `WorkoutWindowQueryService.hasScheduledTrainingOn`** — a SCHEDULE-derived training source (a gym schedule slot, a sport schedule slot, a dated sport event, a prescribed run) covering the date makes it a training day (`FuelDayService.dayTypeAdjusted`) — while **FE picks by `resolveDayType`/`deriveBlocks`** (the same day-planner blocks the timeline already renders — gym/sport slots by weekday + dated sport events + the active run's prescribed days, schedule-only, deliberately NOT an ad-hoc logged session). Both sides are now schedule-only and classify every date identically (`windowsFor` — which also counts ad-hoc logged sessions — stays reserved for the unrelated meal-role pre/post-workout classification, §5). **A one-off dated sport event still serves the training-day kcal even though events don't enter the engine's weekly T-count** (`WeeklyScheduledActivityService.scheduledTrainingDayOfWeeks`, [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3) — so an event week's served kcal Σ can exceed `7×kcal` by design: "aznap tényleg edzel" wins over weekly-sum purity for that one day.

## 5. Integrations

Fuel is the most cross-coupled mock domain. Each seam below names the **contract** (the type/shape that crosses):

- **Today → Fuel rows (Today consumes Fuel).** `useFuelPreview` (`data/today/todayHooks.ts`) **composes `useFuelTimeline()`** (mezo-9ys) — it slices that same dual-mode plan's slots (the next 3 from `state==='now'`) + finds the next pending supplement stack, so Today and the Fuel "Mai" view render the **same plan** in both modes (never diverge). Crossing type: `FuelSlot[]`; since the three-islands re-composition (`mezo-euze`, [ADR 0022](../decisions/0022-today-three-islands.md)) the hook shape is `{ visible, nextStack, plan }` — the **`plan` field is the additive extension**: the day island's protein/energy fact cells read `plan.slots`/`plan.energy` (the full `FuelPlanToday` the hook already composed), so Today's facts and Fuel's timeline cannot diverge. What Today does with the slots:
  - `FuelTimelinePreview` is **deleted** (`mezo-j7u4`). Each slot is normalized by `features/today/logic/todayItems.ts` into a `TodayItem` (`tone: 'fuel'`, group „Fuel") and rendered as a shared `ItemRow` inside **its own island's unfolded L1 list** (`IslandList`, since `mezo-euze`) — bucketed by `faceOf(slot.time, sleepGoal)`, so a 08:00 slot lands on Reggel and an 18:00 one on Este rather than all three sitting in one preview list.
  - Of the retired preview's two non-row jobs, one survives: the **`MOST` marker** on the live slot lives on in the row's own subtitle (`FUEL_STATUS` folds `now` into `open`, so without it the active slot would be indistinguishable from a later one). The other is **gone since `mezo-euze`**: `nextStack.mezoNote`'s companion line lost its surface when the `.tdc-note` slot retired with `TodoCard` (no prose slots on L0 — an ADR 0022 accepted trade).
  - **Superseded by Design 2.0 (`mezo-d20.2.x` + F8):** `TodayPage` and its whole row/island view layer are deleted, so the „log in place instead of navigating” override is gone with it. The Nap hub (`features/today/pages/NapHubPage.tsx`) renders the now-window as a Mozaik tile that **navigates to `/fuel`**; two Nap sub-pages open the log surface directly instead — `NapRutinPage` mounts `LogFlowPage` with `initialSlot="breakfast"` and `EletjelPage` mounts it slot-less. The `TodayItem` normalizer's `/fuel` `nav` route, once the honest fallback, is simply the behavior now.
  - (The old static `fuelToday` seed that `useToday()` re-exposed is **retired** with `fuelPlan` in mezo-53su — no component consumed it.)
- **Today scenario → Fuel Terv (the medication broadcast).** `FuelPlanPage` calls `useTodayScenario()` for `medCycleDay` (feeds `MedicationWeekStrip`'s `currentDay` + the `D{n} · ma` badge); `FuelMaiPage` instead reads the medication cycle directly off `useMedication().cycle` (its `medPeak` fact, §2) — it no longer goes through `useTodayScenario` at all. Crossing type: `TodayScenario`. `useTodayScenario` (`data/today/todayHooks.ts`) derives `medCycleDay` from the SAME `useMedication().cycle.cycleDay` in **both** modes (mock and real alike — since `mezo-lwmq` there is no separate mock fallback field; the owner tracking no medication means this is honestly `0` in both). The `?medCycleDay=` URL override (clamped 1..7) stays **top priority in both modes**, and `?day=` still drives `dayState`.
- **Me/Goals → Fuel Stack (Stack consumes Me + Goals).** `FuelStackPage` reads `useProfile().user.weekInMeso` and `useGoal().linkedMesocycles` (the active mesocycle's `shortTitle`) for the `"Meso W{n}"` context cell — the consumer was updated from `useGoals` to `useGoal` in the G1 split (`mezo-2hp`). Crossing types: `UserMeta`, the linked-mesocycle map.
- **Train (gym/sport/run) → Fuel — two REAL paths since Fuel P4.** **(1) Terv weekly plan · Train's schedule, READ-ONLY (Fuel P4, mezo-kpo; read-only since mezo-4t43):** in real mode `useFuelWeek` renders Train's derived gym week (`useTrain().gymSchedule.weeklyTimes` — the meso template's WHAT joined with the standalone slots' WHEN) + Train's sport-schedule volleyball sessions. **Fuel no longer edits the schedule** — the in-page `GymScheduleSheet` + `useFuelWeekActions` write-through were removed; gym times are set in Train (the mesocycle planner Step 2 + the Gym "Időpontok" chip, both → `PUT /api/train/gym-schedule`). The old private mock copy (`fuelWeek.ts` `gymSchedule` + `today.ts` `volleyballSessions`) survives only as the mock seed. Crossing type: `GymScheduleDay` (out, read). **(2) Mai timeline · REAL Train blocks (Fuel P5, mezo-9ys):** `useFuelTimeline` composes today's gym/sport/run blocks from the live `useTrain().gymSchedule`/`sport.schedule` + `useRunning().activeRunningBlock` (reusing the Train views' own today-derivation — `runSessionsForDay`/`todayIdx`), feeding them as `PlannerBlock[]` into `buildDayPlan`, where meal windows snap around each block (pre −75m / post +45m) and the supplement pre-workout anchor derives from the first block (−40m). The **sport block's label carries the session's sport identity** — `SPORT_TITLES[sportOf(session)]` (`features/train/logic/sportKinds`, `mezo-rhe5`), so a `cross`/`trx` session on the Mai timeline (and the energy-breakdown sheet, which reads `block.label`) reads **Cross**/**TRX** rather than the old hardcoded **Volleyball**. **Since `mezo-e1sp` `deriveBlocks` emits one sport block per today-session** (`.filter`, was `.find`) — a stacked day (a recurring slot + a dated one-off sport event, which Train merges into `sport.schedule` for the current week) counts every session in the activity kcal + meal-window snapping instead of silently dropping all but the first. Crossing types: `GymSchedule`/`SportSchedule`/`RunningBlockResponse` → `PlannerBlock`.
- **Train (workout windows) → Meal scoring (the training-aware role seam, mezo-ta8p).** At meal write `MealService.applyScore` (`MealService.java:171-185`) asks the **train slice** for the day's workout windows — `WorkoutWindowQueryService.windowsFor(userId, mealDate)` (`feature/train/service/WorkoutWindowQueryService.java:42`) returns each gym/sport/run schedule-slot's start, its derived end, kind, and a `done` signal — **since mezo-tm76 that signal is pinned per window, not stamped per day**: **gym** `done` is coverage-based (true only when the day's completed instances cover ALL that weekday's gym slots — an instance carries no clock time, so a partial day leaves them all not-done rather than fabricating a recovery bonus on the slot that did not happen); **sport** is session-driven (a logged `SportSessionEntity` IS the window — its own time + duration, `done=true` — and consumes the recurring slot nearest to it in time, leaving unmatched slots as planned `done=false` windows; a session with no resolvable time yields no window); **run** windows stay pre-only (`done=false`) and their block week is re-derived from `startDate` for the queried date (`MesoWeeks.weekOf`), never read off the lagging `running_block.current_week` — maps them into the scorer-owned `MealScoringService.WorkoutWindow` and calls the pure `MealScoringService.classifyRole(localTime, windows, preLeadMin, postTrailMin)` (`MealScoringService.java:182`) to derive the meal's `MealRole` (see §9 for the rubric overlay). **`localTime` is `loggedAt.toLocalTime()`** — so the classification is only correct if the client sends an offset-bearing `loggedAt`; a bare UTC `Z` instant is read as UTC wall-clock and mis-roles a pre/post meal by the local offset (mezo-g8qm — both capture sheets now send `nowOffsetIso`). The nutrition slice **owns** the `WorkoutWindow`/`MealRole` types so it never imports train; `MealService` (meal slice) is the only mapper across the edge. Crossing types: `WorkoutWindowQueryService.Window` (train) → `MealScoringService.WorkoutWindow` (nutrition) → `MealRole`.
- **Meal → companion (the coach seam, mezo-mr4n).** The qualitative layer over that number: `MealCoachService` (`feature/meal/service`) assembles ONE cheap-tier prompt per day-view and writes only prose. Its LLM dependency is the **meal-owned `MealCoachLlm` port** with a companion-side `MealCoachLlmAdapter` (ADR 0012 — meal never imports companion; the edge runs companion → meal), gated on `mezo.feature.meal-coach.enabled` **plus** the companion switch. `MealCoachStore` owns the two short transactions (read the day → LLM with NO transaction open → write prose), because a batch would otherwise pin a pooled connection across the whole roundtrip. Prompt assembly is a pure unit (`MealCoachPrompt`), so the prompt's content is unit-tested without Spring. **Since mezo-jcpt.1 the verdict also carries `dimensionNotes: {<dim-id>: string}`** — the prompt asks for one 1-2 sentence Hungarian note per SCORED dimension id and explicitly instructs the model to skip "Nincs adat" (weight-0) ones; `MealCoachService.notes` null-safes/blank-filters/240-char-trims/id-matches the answer before `MealCoachStore.writeProse` calls `mergeDimensionNotes` to splice each note into its dimension — an unrecognized id is dropped, and a note landing on a degraded dimension is **rejected in code** (`isDegraded`), not merely by prompt discipline, since an untrusted LLM answer can't be trusted to honor a prose-only instruction on its own.
- **Goal → Fuel timeline (budget + cadence, Fuel P5, mezo-9ys; anchor split off in mezo-dbsr).** `useFuelTimeline` reads the active goal's `mealsPerDay` (the meal cadence — still a goal-contract passthrough field, §4) and the goal's `prescription` **current-week segment** (`kcal`/`proteinG`, selected via `currentWeekOf` over the goal window) for the daily budget (`deriveDailyBudget`; carbs/fat prefer the segment's own prescribed `carbsG`/`fatG` since Diet Plan slice 1 [mezo-xwgb], `FAT_KCAL_SHARE` the legacy fallback split; config `FuelDay.targets` the final fallback). Crossing type: `GoalResponse` (`mealsPerDay` + `prescription.segments[]`). This wires the Goal→Fuel bridge the goal engine emitted-but-narrated-as-deferred ([`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §5) — the prescription's `kcal`/`proteinG` now feed a real consumer. **Dynamic energy budget (mezo-1oy5 / mezo-eujg): the hook now also passes `deriveDailyBudget` a third `EnergyInputs` arg — `{ bmr, neat, weightKg, blocks }` — `bmr`/`neat` off `goalResponse.tdeeBootstrap`, `weightKg` off `goal.currentWeight` (fallback `startWeightKg`), and today's `blocks[]` — so the target becomes `BMR×neat` (the NEAT lifestyle maintenance) `+ Σ MET activity(blocks, weightKg) + balance`, where **`balance = segment.dailyEnergyBalanceKcal`** — the goal's explicit per-day deficit/surplus read straight from the wire, replacing the old `segment.kcal − static TDEE` approximation (whose static TDEE already folded in the day's scheduled run, so subtracting it double-counted training energy against the live MET activity term — that hidden double-count is now gone). Floored at BMR, with `plan.energy = { base, activity, balance, target }` populated; `weightKg` is also threaded into `buildDayPlan` (peri-snack kcal threshold). When `tdeeBootstrap.bmr`/`neat` is absent (no resolved biometric profile), `deriveDailyBudget` falls back to the static segment path unchanged; mock mode carries `bmr 1720`/`neat 1.2`/`neatBaselineKcal 2064`/`currentWeight 78.6` + the always-on gym block, so the demo plan is now genuinely dynamic (`base 2064`, `activity > 0`).**
- **Goal → Meal scoring (the goal-aware scoring seam, Diet Plan slice 2, mezo-3g5w).** The backend meal scorer no longer judges every meal against the static `mezo.nutrition.*` config alone: `MealService.applyScore` resolves the day's targets via `FuelDayService.dailyTargets(userId, mealDate)` — the active goal's covering prescription segment (kcal/protein/carbs/fat, the SAME `segmentFor` resolution `FuelDayService.targetSet` uses for the MacroHero, §10) or the config fallback when there is no goal/segment — and passes the result as the 5th arg to `MealScoringService.scoreMeal(slot, lines, localTime, role, DailyTargets)`. That resolved `DailyTargets` now backs the **macro dimension** AND every **day-share denominator** (`kcalShareOfDay`, slot kcal budgets, slot protein references) — not just the STANDARD macro rubric (PRE/POST keep their own **role-absolute** ratio targets from the config bundles regardless of goal; only the day-share denominators are goal-based), so a logged meal is scored against the goal's real deficit/surplus, not a generic default. Crossing type: `DailyTargets` (`feature/nutrition/service` — `record DailyTargets(kcal, p, c, f, source)`, `source` is `"goal"` or `"config"`, feeding the provenance tool row). Covered by `MealScoringServiceTest` (`testScoreMeal_shouldUseProvidedDailyTargets_whenBaseGiven`, `testScoreMeal_shouldMatchConfigOverload_whenBaseIsFromConfig`, `testScoreMeal_shouldKeepRoleRubric_whenPrePostWithGoalBase`) and `feature/meal/FuelDayServiceIT` (`testDailyTargets_shouldReadGoalSegment_whenActiveGoalCoversDate`, `testDailyTargets_shouldFallBackToConfig_whenNoActiveGoal`). This does **not** close the goal engine's protein-guard gap — see [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §9.
- **Meal → Goal (the reverse direction — adaptive-review intake-adherence port, Diet Plan slice 5, `mezo-r4n7`).** The goal engine's Monday adaptive review reads meal, not the other way round: `IntakeAdherencePort` (`feature/goal/engine/port`) is implemented by `GoalIntakeAdherenceAdapter` (`feature/meal/service`) off `FuelDayService.getWeek` — context only (logged-day kcal averages), never a Fuel-visible surface. See [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3/§5 for the consumer side.
- **Sleep goal → Fuel timeline (the day-anchor, mezo-dbsr, spec D3).** The wake/bed anchor that drives meal-slot placement (`reggeli = wake+45`, `kitchenClose = bed−90`) and the protocol anchors (`wake` / `preWorkout = firstBlock−40` / `bedtime`) now comes from `useSleepGoal()` — always set (mock seed / backend config-ghost), so there is **no wake/bed fallback const** (`PLANNER_DEFAULTS` was deleted in mezo-53su). A sleep-goal write invalidates `['fuelDay']` (among others), so the timeline recomputes off the new anchor. Crossing type: `SleepGoal` (`wakeTime`/`bedTime`). See [`me.md`](me.md) §3/§5.3.
- **Fuel → Push notifications (the shared pre-workout anchor + zone timeline, `mezo-h4wp.6.3`; ridden onto `projectStackDay` mezo-vx9v Task 9).** `deriveBlocks` — Fuel's own "today's gym/sport/run blocks" derivation — lives in `features/fuel/logic/buildProtocol.ts` (re-exported from `data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts` for back-compat) so it is the **one canonical** source for three callers: Fuel's own `useFuelTimeline`, the notification platform's app-open schedule writer (`useScheduleSnapshotWriter`, the `fuel_slot` category), and the `Értesítés` settings preview (`NotificationsPage`). **Since Task 9 all three compose the SAME `projectStackDay({occurrences, stash, intakes, wake, bed, mealsPerDay, blocks})` projection** — the writer and `NotificationsPage` each call it directly (composed inline, not via `useStackDay()`, so the writer keeps its own `useSleepGoal().isPending` fire-once gate and `NotificationsPage` keeps its own raw `blocks[]` for the gym sub-line — see the task's own reasoning) rather than re-deriving a `ProtocolAnchors` shape; `PRE_WORKOUT_STACK_LEAD_MIN` (still `buildProtocol.ts`'s constant) is the one offset both `projectStackDay` and any `deriveProtocolAnchors` caller apply, so the three surfaces can't quietly disagree on when the pre-workout window fires. `FUEL_WINDOW_LABEL` (`notificationScheduleWriter.ts`) is keyed by the 8 `StackZoneKey`s, and `buildScheduleEntries`/`NotificationsPage`'s `fuelSlotCount` both drop any `StackDaySlot` whose every entry is `skippedToday` (a rest-day zone with nothing live in it fires no notification, mirrors `buildDayPlan`'s zone-drop rule). Crossing type: `StackDaySlot[]` (was `ProtocolAnchors`). See [`_platform-notifications.md`](_platform-notifications.md) §5.
- **Fuel settings → Habit caffeine metric (the caffeine cutoff, mezo-53su, spec D3).** The habit engine's `no_stim_after` (E1) metric now reads the cutoff through Fuel's **ungated `CaffeineCutoffPort`** (`feature/fuel/service`) — `HabitEvaluator` compares each stim intake against `caffeineCutoffPort.resolve(userId)`; `HabitProperties.caffeineCutoff` + `mezo.habit.caffeine-cutoff` are gone. The port is deliberately **not** switch-gated so the habit metric resolves even with the fuel-settings surface off (the `SleepAnchorPort` precedent). On the FE, `useFuelSettingsActions().setSettings` invalidates **`['fuelSettings']` + `['habitDay']`** so a cutoff edit re-centres the habit day immediately. Crossing type: `LocalTime` (the resolved cutoff) / `CaffeineCutoffPort`. See [`habit.md`](habit.md) §5.
- **Fuel settings → Fuel timeline (meal cadence + cutoff, mezo-53su).** `useFuelTimeline` reads `mealsPerDay` + `caffeineCutoff` from `useFuelSettings()` (the config-ghost is always present) instead of `goal.mealsPerDay` + the deleted `CAFFEINE_CUTOFF` const; the timeline is derived, so it recomputes off `['fuelSettings']` automatically. Crossing type: `FuelSettings`.
- **Recipes → Fuel timeline (slot suggestions, Fuel P5, mezo-9ys).** For each un-logged meal/snack window `buildDayPlan` ranks the recipe catalog (`useRecipes().recipes`): candidates of the window's slot category with per-serving kcal within **±20%** of the slot budget, ranked `|Δkcal| → starred → |Δprotein|`; the top pick becomes the slot's `suggestedRecipeId` + `mealName` (the `ajánlott` chip, tap → a `LogFlowPage` prefilled from that recipe and seeded with the window's `slotKey`). No fit → a budget-only slot. Crossing type: `Recipe[]` → `FuelSlot.suggestedRecipeId`.
- **Medication cycle → cross-cutting.** `medCycleWeek`/`medCycleDay` is a Fuel-owned concept (a generic 7-day dose-kinetic cycle: D1-2 Peak → D3-5 Stable → D6-7 Trough) but is surfaced on Today and across all Fuel sub-views — even though, since `mezo-lwmq`, the owner tracks no medication and every one of these surfaces is honestly empty in practice. `medCycleDay` arrives via `useTodayScenario`, whose base is **derived from the medication cycle in both modes** (`useMedication().cycle.cycleDay`, mezo-d94) — the single FE broadcast point every medication-cycle surface (Fuel Terv's `MedicationWeekStrip`) reads. **Since the Mozaik hub (`mezo-d20.4.1`) Mai carries NO medication signal at all except the Gyógyszer tile's `D{n} · {phase}` line** — the window-island subtitle that leaked the peak phase in as a fact went with the islands, so the paragraph below is history: **Mai no longer carries a standing cycle strip** (the `.medcycle-micro` block — itself the guided-redesign's `mezo-rrtj` replacement for the older `MedCyclePhaseBar` primitive — was retired whole with the window-river recomposition, `mezo-jgh9`, [ADR 0023](../decisions/0023-fuel-window-river.md) §2): the cycle then leaked into Mai only as a **fact**, the now-island's subtitle appending `"Csúcsfázisban megnőtt étvágy"` when `useMedication().cycle.phaseKey === 'peak'` (`FuelMaiPage.tsx`'s `medPeak` const, fed into `buildWindowRiver`) — **that subtitle and the `medPeak` const are both gone**; the hub reads `useMedication().cycle` only for the Gyógyszer tile's line. Because `useTodayScenario`/`FuelMaiPage` still issue a `['medication']` query, any test that renders one of these surfaces needs a `QueryClientProvider`.
- **Replan cascade → Sleep / Insights / Train (Fuel exposes the ripple model).** `ReplanScenario.cascades[].system` is typed `'Fuel'|'Train'|'Sleep'|'Insights'` (`types.ts:304`); `ReplanSheet` color-codes per system. This is the explicit "context change ripples across domains" model — all simulated prose in `data/fuel/fuelWeek.ts`.
- **Supplements ↔ Pantry (internal seam, now a real read composition — mezo-09g).** `useStack` no longer reads the `supplementsStash` const directly — it **composes `usePantry().stash`** (`stackHooks.ts:80-85`), the single source of truth, and only overlays each item's `taken` from the day's intakes. So in mock mode the stash is the seed via `pantryHooks.ts`, and in real mode it comes from `pantryApi.list()` — one source, no divergence (`supplementsStash` survives only as the mock seed behind `usePantry` + the intake-seed derivation). The Kamra still merges the same stash into pantry items, de-duping via `Ingredient.stashRefId` (`kamraItems.ts:17-18`).
- **Recipes ↔ FuelDay (internal seam).** `data/fuel/pantry.ts` imports `fuelDay` to derive recipe `recentLogs` from logged meals — so scoring a meal and viewing a recipe's log history share one source (`pantry.ts`). Note: since mezo-arb the mock `fuelDay.meals[].score` ships **NULL** (pending-sparkle), so the recipe seed's historical `recentLogs` scores are now sourced from a decoupled `recentLogScore` map in `data/fuel/pantry.ts` (nulling the day-view display score must not erase the recipe log history).
- **Meal logging → FuelDay + Recipes + Pantry (the real write seam, mezo-arb).** `useMealActions` (`data/fuel/fuelHooks.ts`) is the write path for logging/editing/deleting a meal. In real mode each write calls `mealApi` then invalidates **`['fuelDay']` + `['recipes']` + `['pantry']`** — logging a meal shifts a recipe's `recentLogs` (`RecipeLogsList`) and pantry usage, so all three caches must refetch. In mock mode it mutates the `['fuelDay', date]` cache in place and recomputes `consumed`. Crossing types: `MealInput` (in), `FuelMeal`/`FuelDayData` (out).
- **Supplement intake → Pantry + Stack (the real intake write seam, mezo-09g).** `useStackActions` (`data/fuel/stackHooks.ts`) is the write path for logging/undoing a supplement/stim intake (from the Stack timing slots' tap-to-log). The intake ledger FK-s `pantry_item` — an intake references a pantry supplement row by `pantryItemId`, and `useStack` reads that same pantry stash back to overlay `taken`, so **one intake log updates the Stack's taken-state directly** (no second store). In real mode a log/undo hits `POST /api/fuel/intake` / `DELETE /api/fuel/intake/entry/{id}` then invalidates `['fuelIntake', date]` → `useStack` refetch; **since `mezo-u6jx` it also invalidates `['habitDay']` + `['dailyQuests', date]`** — a stim intake can satisfy the habit engine's `stim_intake_today` (M4) or affect `no_stim_after` (E1), and the habit/quest day READ is the evaluation trigger, so the log/undo must nudge it too (the `mezo-pquo` fan-out pattern — see [habit.md](habit.md) §3/§5); mock mutates the intake cache in place. Crossing types: `pantryItemId` (in), `Intake[]` (out, → `taken`). **The `protocol` write seam is the occurrence-CRUD `useProtocolActions()`** (`addItem`/`moveItem`/`setDose`/`unpinItem`/`removeItem`/`removeAllFor`, mezo-vx9v — there is no whole-selection apply/"Bekapcsolás · ma" step anymore, retired Task 8/10) → each call touches one `protocol_item` row FK-ing the same `pantry_item`. (The P5 fuel-timeline **now** reuses this same `GET /api/fuel/intake/{date}` read + the occurrence-projected `projectStackDay` slots — Task 9 — to light up the supplement half of the merged agenda — mezo-9ys.)
- **OpenFoodFacts → Pantry (the app's FIRST external HTTP seam, Fuel P6 · mezo-bka).** `OffClient` (`feature/pantry/service`, Spring `RestClient`) proxies OFF's v1 text search + v2 barcode endpoint into `PantryLookupResult` drafts; NOVA arrives as the `nova_group` **passthrough** (deterministic — the one NOVA source that needs no backfill). Config `mezo.pantry-import.*` (base-url/timeout/UA/page-size/feed-size), switch `mezo.feature.pantry-import.enabled`, failure → 502 `PANTRY_IMPORT_LOOKUP_FAILED`; ITs stub OFF with **WireMock**. Crossing types: `q` string (in), `PantryLookupResult[]` (out), `PantryImportRequest` (write-back).
- **Web product page → Pantry (the second external HTTP seam, mezo-8vum).** `WebPageClient` (`feature/pantry/service`, its own `RestClient`) GETs an arbitrary shop URL with browser-like headers, a streaming body cap and an SSRF guard (public hosts only, redirects never followed); the HTML is jsoup-stripped and handed to the LLM. Config `mezo.pantry-scrape.*`, switch `mezo.feature.pantry-scrape.enabled`, failures → 502 `PANTRY_SCRAPE_FETCH_FAILED`/`PANTRY_SCRAPE_EXTRACT_FAILED`; ITs stub the product pages with **WireMock**. Crossing types: `url` string (in), `PantryScrapeResult` (out), `PantryImportRequest` (write-back).
- **Pantry → Companion LLM (the consumer-owned-port seam, mezo-8vum · [ADR 0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)).** The scrape extractor needs the cheap-tier LLM ([`companion.md`](companion.md) `CompanionLlm`, [ADR 0008](../decisions/0008-companion-llm-spring-ai-2-gemini.md)), but a direct `pantry → companion` edge would cycle the feature graph (companion already depends transitively on pantry). Instead pantry **owns the port it needs** — `ScrapeLlm` (`feature/pantry/service`, a one-method `complete(system, user)`) — and the companion feature provides the adapter `PantryScrapeLlmAdapter` (`feature/companion/llm`, gated on the companion switch) that delegates to `CompanionLlm`. So the only cross-feature edge runs **companion → pantry** and the ArchUnit slice-cycle rule stays green; pantry reaches the port via `ObjectProvider<ScrapeLlm>`, so companion-off degrades the endpoint to a clean 503. Crossing type: `ScrapeLlm` (the pantry-owned interface). The same pattern now also backs the meal AI-log (mezo-78rn, next bullet).
- **Meal → Companion LLM (the second consumer-owned-port seam, mezo-78rn · [ADR 0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)).** The AI meal-draft extractor (`MealAiDraftService`) needs the cheap tier PLUS the vision overload, but a direct `meal → companion` edge would cycle the feature graph (companion already reads meal). So meal **owns the port** — `MealDraftLlm` (`feature/meal/service`, a text-only + multimodal `complete`) — and companion provides `MealDraftLlmAdapter` (`feature/companion/llm`, gated on the companion switch) delegating to `CompanionLlm` (incl. its mezo-78rn `Media` image overload). The only cross-feature edge runs **companion → meal**; ArchUnit stays green; meal reaches the port via `ObjectProvider<MealDraftLlm>`, so companion-off degrades the endpoint to a clean 503. Crossing type: `MealDraftLlm` (the meal-owned interface). Sibling of the pantry-scrape seam above; see [`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.3.
- **Stack placement → Companion LLM (the fourth consumer-owned-port seam, mezo-vx9v · [ADR 0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md), [ADR 0017](../decisions/0017-living-occurrence-protocol.md)).** `PlacementEngine`'s zone-assignment fallback (§4) — for a supplement the deterministic rule table + timing hint don't recognize — needs one cheap-tier completion, but `feature/fuel → feature/companion` would cycle the graph the same way pantry/meal would. So fuel **owns the port** — `StackPlacementLlm` (`feature/fuel/service`, a one-method `complete(systemPrompt, userMessage)`) — and companion provides `StackPlacementLlmAdapter` (`feature/companion/llm`, gated at the bean boundary on **both** `mezo.feature.stack-placement-llm.enabled` and the companion switch) delegating to `CompanionLlm`. The only cross-feature edge runs **companion → fuel**; ArchUnit stays green; `PlacementEngine` reaches the port via `ObjectProvider<StackPlacementLlm>`, so either switch off simply skips stage 3 and falls through to the honest `breakfast` fallback — never an error. Unlike the other three LLM seams this one has **no dedicated user-facing endpoint or switch-off HTTP status** — it is an internal fallback inside the ordinary occurrence-CRUD write path, so a companion-off day is invisible to the user (a rule-table miss just lands in `breakfast` with a generic reason instead of a model-derived one). Crossing type: `StackPlacementLlm` (the fuel-owned interface). See [`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.3.
- **Shared design primitives.** Fuel reuses `frontend/src/shared/ui/` heavily: `Sheet`, `StatCell`, `ProgressBar`, `Eyebrow`, `PageTitle`, `Chip`, `Icon`, `ScoreRing`, `ToolChipRow`, `SourceBadge`, `NovaDot`, `SafeMarkdown`, `Toggle`, — all "Deep Current v2" tokens from `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css`. **Since Mozaik 2.0 ([ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)) Fuel's primary primitives are the `shared/ui/mozaik/` family** — `Tile`, `Mosaic`, `StatStrip`/`StatCell`, `MCells`, `MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`, `CollapsibleStrip`, and `motion.tsx`'s `EntranceGroup`/`useCountUp` — plus **`shared/ui/clay/`**'s `ClayIcon`/`ClaySpot` sprite components, which replace emoji wholesale; their tokens are the `--mz-*` custom properties in `prototype.css`. **`shared/ui/Island.tsx` is no longer a Fuel primitive at all**: `mezo-jgh9` promoted it out of `features/today/components/` on the 2nd-consumer rule ([ADR 0023](../decisions/0023-fuel-window-river.md)), and F8 removed both consumers — the file is still in the tree with only its own test importing it (§9). (`MedCyclePhaseBar` was deleted with the guided Mai redesign, `mezo-rrtj`, superseded in turn by `.medcycle-micro`, itself retired whole by the window-river recomposition — §2.)
- **Account XP/coins/streak** (`mezo-k7rn`, mock-mode only). `useMealActions`'s mock write (`data/fuel/fuelHooks.ts:73`, `type: 'MEAL'`) and `useMedicationActions`'s mock `logDose` (`data/fuel/medicationHooks.ts:69`, `type: 'MEDICATION'`) each call `awardGamificationEvent` — a separate, additive award into the account-wide XP/coins/streak store (`data/gamification/gamificationStore.ts`), independent of Fuel's own meal-score/cycle logic above. Real mode never calls it. **The `AppHero` header that used to render that progression on every section was deleted with the shells (F8, [ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md))** — the store is unchanged, but Fuel no longer displays it; see [growth.md](growth.md) for where the account-progression domain surfaces now.

## 6. How to use it (consume)

Import any of the nine Fuel hooks from the single boundary `@/data/hooks`:

```ts
import {
  useFuelDay, useMealActions, useRecipeLogs,
  useFuelTimeline, useStack, useProtocol, useStackActions, useProtocolActions, useStackDay,
  usePantry, useRecipes, useFuelWeek,
  useReplanScenarios,
} from '@/data/hooks'

const { fuel } = useFuelDay()                       // FuelDay (composed, dual-mode): targets/consumed/meals/pacing/micronutrients/supplements
const { logMeal, updateMeal, deleteMeal } = useMealActions()  // write path: MealInput → POST/PUT/DELETE /api/meal (+ mock cache mutate)
const { logs } = useRecipeLogs(recipeId)            // RecipeLog[] for RecipeLogsList
const { plan, wake, bed, nowHHmm, getScoredMeal } = useFuelTimeline()   // plan: FuelPlanToday; wake/bed/nowHHmm anchor the Mai zones/now-pin; getScoredMeal(slot) → FuelMeal|null
const { stash } = useStack()                        // SupplementStashItem[]
const { protocol, occurrences } = useProtocol()     // Protocol + the living-protocol ProtocolOccurrence[] (mezo-vx9v; no selectedIds — retired Task 10)
const { logIntake, undoIntake } = useStackActions() // (pantryItemId, slotKey?, dose?) — the Stack page always passes slotKey explicitly
const { addItem, moveItem, setDose, unpinItem, removeItem, removeAllFor } = useProtocolActions() // occurrence CRUD (mezo-vx9v)
const { slots, dayType, wake: stackWake, bed: stackBed } = useStackDay()  // the Stack page's ONE data source — zoned StackDaySlot[] (mezo-vx9v Task 8)
const { ingredients, stash, sources, categoryMeta, imports, suggestions } = usePantry()
const { recipes, sources, categoryMeta } = useRecipes() // ingredients come from usePantry() (mezo-yew)
const { breakdown, fitsFor, pending, refreshing } = useRecipeBreakdown(recipeId) // template envelope + prose; pending = FIRST generate, refreshing = a write-driven REgenerate (mezo-uavr) — render the twinkle on `pending || refreshing`, never the stale reading
const { title, medCycleWeek, gymSchedule, weeklySupplements, patterns, weeklyStats, volleyball, weeklyNote } = useFuelWeek() // FuelWeekView (dual-mode, P4; gym times read-only — mezo-4t43)
const { scenarios } = useReplanScenarios()          // ReplanScenario[]
```

Pure helpers to reuse: `projectStackDay(input: StackDayInput)` (`features/fuel/logic/projectStackDay.ts` — occurrences+stash+intakes+anchors → zoned `StackDaySlot[]`; the Stack page's `useStackDay`, `useFuelTimeline`, and the notification writer/preview all feed it; NOTE a multi-block day may emit TWO `pre_workout` slots — the stim-aware split, mezo-j6c9 — so never key or index by zone alone; `isStimFreeName(name)`/`STIM_FREE_NEEDLES` are exported from the same module), `matchMealsToStack(slots, recipes, todayMeals, yesterdayMeals)` (`features/fuel/logic/matchMealsToStack.ts` — recipe suggestions + ✓/⚠ verdicts for the Stack's "Étkezés-egyeztetés" section), `deriveBlocks(gymSchedule, sport, activeRunningBlock)` (`features/fuel/logic/buildProtocol.ts` — today's training blocks; `buildProtocol()` itself was retired mezo-vx9v Task 9, §9), `buildKamraItems(ingredients, stash)` (`features/fuel/logic/kamraItems.ts`), `getScoredMeal(slot, meals)` (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`). To embed a full sub-view, render the route component (e.g. `<FuelMaiPage />`) under a `<MemoryRouter>`/router context, since views read `useTodayScenario` (URL params). For a lighter Today-style preview, use `useFuelPreview()` → `{ visible: FuelSlot[], nextStack }` (Today itself now feeds those slots through `features/today/logic/todayItems.ts` into per-face `ItemRow`s — see §5).

## 7. How to extend it

**To make Fuel real (Phase-2 Slice C), use the biometrics slice as the template** (`useWeight` + `frontend/src/data/me/biometricsApi.ts` are the proven pattern). Recipe:

1. **Contract-first** (**[`api_contract_conventions.md`](../references/api_contract_conventions.md)**): add `api/feature/fuel/fuel.yml` (endpoints + DTO schemas: FuelDay, MealBreakdown, PantryItem, Recipe, Protocol…). Merge: `cd api/generate && npm run generate:api`; regenerate FE types: `cd frontend && pnpm generate:api`; backend DTOs regenerate in `./mvnw generate-sources`.
2. **Backend** under `io.mrkuhne.mezo.feature.fuel/{controller,service,repository,entity,dto,mapper}` per **[`java_package_structure.md`](../references/java_package_structure.md)** + **[`spring_patterns.md`](../references/spring_patterns.md)**. Entities: `food_item`/`meal`/`meal_item`/`recipe`/`supplement_intake`/`medication(_dose)`/`nutrition_targets` + a fuel-timeline view. UUID PKs, `OwnedEntity` + `created_by`, `@SQLDelete`/`@SQLRestriction`. **Meal `score` → typed jsonb** via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` onto a typed record — reuse the Train `ProvenanceEnvelope` pattern (`MealBreakdown` is the natural envelope).
3. **Liquibase** (**[`liquibase_conventions.md`](../references/liquibase_conventions.md)**): changesets `{YYYYMMDDHHMM}_{bd-id}_{desc}.sql`, explicit constraint names, **seed data in Java `@Profile("demodata")`** (never SQL). Add new tables to the `ResetDatabase` TRUNCATE list and add a populator per aggregate (**[`integration_test_framework.md`](../references/integration_test_framework.md)**).
4. **FE client:** add `frontend/src/data/fuel/fuelApi.ts` mirroring `biometricsApi.ts` (`apiFetch`, `satisfies` on request bodies, contract types from `api.gen.ts`).
5. **Hooks:** rewrite the nine Fuel hooks in `hooks.ts` to branch `isMockMode() ? mockConst : fuelApi.x()` with `useQuery`/`useMutation` + `initialData` for synchronous mock parity — **copy `useWeight` (`weightHooks.ts:11`) exactly. Do not touch view/component signatures.**
6. **Config** (**[`configuration_conventions.md`](../references/configuration_conventions.md)**): scoring dimension weights, the kcal floor (hardcoded `2500` in `FuelMaiPage`), and `weeklyStats` factors must become `mezo.*` `@Validated` properties — never `@Value`, never literals.
7. **Tests** (**[`testing_standards.md`](../references/testing_standards.md)** + **[`integration_test_framework.md`](../references/integration_test_framework.md)**): integration-first (`@SpringBootTest` + Testcontainers PG, AssertJ, populators), an ownership-isolation test per owned entity. FE: add MSW handlers for real-mode hook tests; keep **both** mock and real modes green.
8. **Docs:** an **ADR** in `docs/decisions/` for the scoring/jsonb-envelope decision; update **[`docs/milestones/roadmap.md`](../milestones/roadmap.md)** when the slice lands.

**Within the mock layer** (no backend), the established recipes are: add a logged metric → extend the type in `data/types.ts` + the const in `data/fuel/fuel.ts`/`fuelWeek.ts`/`pantry.ts`; add a Stack placement rule (mock side) → extend `mockPlaceOccurrence`/the seed `protocolOccurrences` array in `data/fuel/fuel.ts` to mirror a change to the backend's `PlacementRules.RULES` (real side lives server-only — §4/§7-backend); add a pantry source → extend `PantrySourceKey` + `pantrySources` (`data/pantrySources.ts`); add a Fuel surface → add a flat route in `router.tsx`, a `Tile` on the `FuelMaiPage` hub mosaic pointing at it, and a `MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero` page in `pages/` (there is no `FUEL_TABS` array any more — the hub tile IS the nav entry). **Adding a Stack placement rule (real side, mezo-vx9v):** extend `PlacementRules.RULES` (`backend/.../feature/fuel/service/PlacementRules.java`) — ordered, first-match-wins, each rule needs `needles`/`slotKey`/`restDayFallback`/`reasonHu`/`dailyTotalHintHu` — and mirror the same needles in the mock's `protocolOccurrences` seed (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`) so mock/real placement stays visually consistent; no code path other than `PlacementEngine.place` should ever hardcode a supplement→zone mapping.

**Rendering the recipe meal role on a NEW surface (mezo-uavr):** `features/fuel/logic/recipeRole.ts` is the single source of HU copy and it exposes **two** maps — pick by grammatical position, never `.toLowerCase()` one to get the other. `roleLabel` is **postpositional** („Edzés előtt") and belongs on the editor segments, the detail hero chip and the library card tag; `roleRubricLabel` is **adjectival** („edzés előtti") and is the only one that can attribute a noun, as in the score header's `{roleRubricLabel(role)} mérce szerint` — „edzés előtt mérce" reads as a dropped word. `ROLE_OPTIONS` is the ordered option list for any picker. Every read surface must also honour the **`standard` renders nothing** rule: the default role is invisible everywhere, which is what makes a visible chip informative.

## 8. Testing

Fuel ships ~26 test files (data + views + components + sheets + helpers). The backend-backed slices (Pantry, Recipes, Meal-logging) run dual-mode — mock-mode tests stub `VITE_USE_MOCK=true`; real-mode tests exercise `mealApi`/`recipeApi`/`pantryApi` via the MSW handlers (`frontend/src/test/msw/handlers.ts`). Notable:
- `features/fuel/logic/buildProtocol.test.ts` — since mezo-vx9v Task 9 (buildProtocol() itself retired) trimmed to just the `deriveProtocolAnchors` describe block: gym-present → `preWorkout` = gym−40min, no training → `preWorkout` honestly `undefined`.
- **Stack occurrence, presentation and page-family suite (mezo-vx9v + mezo-ubxd).** The existing projection/API coverage remains in `projectStackDay.test.ts`, `stackDayHooks.test.tsx`, `stackHooks.test.tsx`, `fuelApi.test.ts` and backend Fuel ITs. Stack 3.0 adds `stackPresentation.test.ts` for honest next/progress/grouping/empty derivation; `useStackIntakeToggle.test.tsx` and `stackHooks.test.tsx` pin the exact created-id undo race; hub/read/manage page tests pin the eight routes, read/write boundaries, autosave actions and honest empty protocol/meals states; `stackLayout.test.ts` asserts 320/390/430 px no-overflow plus the 44px intake target; `stackCssContract.test.ts` pins `.stk-*` scoping and reduced motion; `tests/visual/fuel-stack.spec.ts` supplies five Stack routes × light/dark = ten goldens.
- `features/fuel/logic/kamraItems.test.ts` — unified-item merge + stash de-dup.
- `data/fuel/pantryPickables.test.ts` — `buildPickables` foods+stash merge, kind mapping, `stashRefId` de-dup, supplement default-fill (raw id, per/unit/macros defaults · mezo-3vu4).
- View tests: `pages/Fuel{Mai,Plan,Stack,Recipes,Kamra,Medication}View.test.tsx` (the Medication test asserts the seed medication name/phase, the cycle bar's current-day cell, and the 3-dose log — mode-agnostic, green in both modes) (the Mai suite is now `pages/FuelMaiPage.test.tsx` + `pages/FuelMaiPage.logMeal.test.tsx` — the hub's tiles/lane/bands, and separately that a recipe-suggestion window opens `LogFlowPage` pre-filled while a budget-only window opens it with the tapped window's slot pre-selected, the `mezo-bnsf` contract; the Kamra test covers navigate-on-card-click + the category-filter sheet AND-filtering) and `pages/KamraItemDetailPage.test.tsx` (renders macros+nutrients, missing-id fallback, the `＋ Logolás` CTA opens the pre-filled `LogFlowPage`, Szerkesztés opens the editor, Törlés deletes via the unprefixed backend id for both food and stash and navigates back).
- Meal-logging wiring tests: the `+ Mai étkezéshez` CTA opening a pre-filled `LogFlowPage` (`pages/RecipeDetailPage.test.tsx`), and the **unified flow's own five-file suite** — `pages/LogFlowPage.test.tsx` (slot segments, source tiles, live totals, save), `.prefill.test.tsx` (recipe/pantry prefill arms), `.ai.test.tsx` (the armed AI panel, BECSLÉS tagging, provenance origin), `.overrides.test.tsx` (the ported `mezo-ormb` ingredient block) and `.timestamp.test.tsx` (the offset-bearing `loggedAt`). The old `TodayPage.test.tsx` „a fuel row logs IN PLACE" case is gone with `TodayPage` itself (F8); `FuelTimelinePreview.test.tsx` was deleted with its component (`mezo-j7u4`).
- Recipe page tests: `pages/RecipeDetailPage.test.tsx` (F7.3 mosaic: hero + statstrip + the four tiles with ingredient lines confined to the local view, the Pontszám tile opens `RecipeScoreSheet` with the shared dimension cards + the role-rubric header line, the Logok tile opens the log sheet, the Hozzávalók tile opens/closes the local view with the recipe-level `NutrientCells`, the `mezo-uavr` re-evaluation honesty trio rewritten onto the tiles; NOVA in the hero meta line; missing-id fallback, serving-toggle basis switch, Szerkesztés (head pgact) navigates to `/:id/edit`, Törlés removes + navigates back, Csillag toggles `starred`) and `pages/RecipeEditorPage.test.tsx` (create: Mentés gated on name+ingredient, saving appends to the `['recipes']` cache + navigates back; edit: prefill + update; picked-row `MacroCells` contribution; **mezo-3vu4:** the picker lists stash supplements, stays open for multi-add, disables an already-added row, and a picked **supplement** resolves to its name + macro contribution; **mezo-2567:** a line's gram amount is typeable — an exact value recomputes the contribution and a decimal like `12.5` is preserved). Both stub `VITE_USE_MOCK=true` and render under `MemoryRouter` + a `QueryClientProvider`. `data/fuel/recipeHooks.test.tsx` also asserts a created **supplement line** enriches to its stash name (not the raw refId).
- Sheet tests: `MealScoreSheet`, `ReplanSheet`, `ImportItemSheet`, `AddPantryItemSheet` (real `usePantry` cache append on add; edit round-trips name + extended-nutrition + price; the mezo-2567 ADAG-edit test is RETIRED — since mezo-0gjr the basis is not an input, replaced by fixed-basis guarantees: create lands per-100/g, edit preserves a legacy basis, inherited-basis hint renders), `LogDoseSheet` (mezo-d94 · the app seeds no medication, so the populated-branch cases preload `medicationFixture` (`frontend/src/test/fixtures/medication.ts`) into the `['medication']` cache before rendering; mock: a saved dose lands in that cache via a co-rendered `useMedication()` read + the cycle recomputes to `cycleDay 1`; real: `medicationApi.logDose` spy asserts the dose + today; both modes assert the dose prefills from the last dose + the date defaults to today). (The retired `RecipeDetailSheet`/`NewRecipeSheet` tests were deleted in Phase 7c; the Fuel `GymScheduleSheet` + its test were deleted in `mezo-4t43` — gym times are now Train-owned.)
- Component tests: `FuelTimeline`, `KcalGauge` (**NEW, Napiv S6** — asserts the consumed/target/percent text, the `.gauge-p` `stroke-dasharray` for a normal day, a 0/0 cold-window day with no `NaN`, and an overshoot day where the arc caps at the full `282.7…` dasharray while the text reads `117%`), `RecipeCard`, `MedicationWeekStrip` (mock-pinned + QueryClient since P4 — the strip reads the composed `useFuelWeek`), `WeekRhythmGrid`, `kamraCards`, `weekWidgets` (both fed the seeds directly as props). **`FuelSubNav.test.tsx` was deleted** with the component (compact-header redesign, `mezo-ugqb`), and **`FuelSection.test.tsx` followed it in F8** — there is no Fuel shell left to test. The hub's Mozaik contract is covered by `FuelMaiPage.test.tsx` plus the shared `shared/ui/mozaik/Mozaik.test.tsx` / `motion.test.tsx` / `mozaikCssTokens.test.ts` / `prototypeCssStructure.test.ts` and `shared/ui/clay/Clay.test.tsx`.
- Data tests: `data/fuel/fuelData.test.tsx`, `data/fuel/pantryData.test.tsx`, `data/fuel/fuelWeekData.test.tsx` (mock seed parity), `data/fuel/fuelWeekHooks.test.tsx` (P4 — pure helpers `mondayIso`/`deriveWeekTitle`/`toMedCycleCells`/`withDefaultDuration`/`deriveWeeklyStats` + both hook modes: real composes the MSW Train/medication/week fixtures + honest-empty deferred surfaces; the gym-time write-through tests were removed in `mezo-4t43` — Fuel is read-only). `FuelPlanPage.test.tsx` runs a mock describe (demo title, 92% adherence) + a real describe (derived title, hidden patterns/supplement sections, `—` adherence, the medication card present from the medication fixture) and asserts there is **no** gym-time edit affordance (`mezo-4t43`).
- **Score fixes + dimension notes (mezo-jcpt.1).** Backend: `MealScoringServiceTest` (macro kcal-significance scaling — a below-ref-share meal's ratio-deviation penalty scales down linearly, an at/above-ref-share meal is unaffected; the emitted weights renormalize to Σ==1.0 over live dims and Σ(weight·score)==`value`, including the degraded-dim-present case), `MealCoachServiceTest` + `MealCoachPromptTest` (`dimensionNotes` extraction: null-safe, blank-filtered, 240-char-trimmed, id-matched against the envelope's dims, a note aimed at a weight-0 dim is dropped even when the LLM sends one). FE: `components/DimensionCard.test.tsx` (the ghost-tile render for a degraded dim, `dim.note` via `SafeMarkdown`), `components/ScoreLedger.test.tsx` (a degraded dim is excluded from the bar/%-row and listed in the "Nincs adat" line), `data/fuel/mealApi.test.ts` (degraded dims kept unconditionally, colors assigned).
- **AI meal-log (mezo-78rn; the `PantryNameIndex` arm since mezo-qrks).** Backend: `MealAiDraftServiceIT` (9 — pantry+estimate match, real-recipe match, hallucinated-id demotion, nothing-recognized empty, unparseable → 502, Atwater → `needsReview`, plus the name-match arm's id-less pantry-by-name match, unit-disagreement staying an estimate, and hallucinated-id-then-name-match), `PantryNameIndexTest` (9 — exact name across case/accents, brand-prefixed name, pack-size-stripped name, percentage NOT stripped from a fat-content name, an ambiguous stripped key misses, a unit disagreement misses, unit synonyms accepted, a null serving unit defaults to grams, an empty index/name misses), `MealAiDraftApiIT` (6 — text-sentinel draft, photo-bytes-sentinel draft, 400 neither-input / bad-mime / too-large, 502 unparseable) over the new `postMultipartForResponse`/`photoPart` helpers on `ApiIntegrationTest`, `MealAiLlmUnavailableApiIT` (companion-off → 503 `MEAL_AI_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`), `MealAiDraftSwitchOffApiIT` (feature-off → **405 `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`**, NOT a `MEAL_AI_*` code — §9), `MealAiDraftValidatorTest` (4 Atwater edges), `CompanionLlmFakeIT` (+4 — the `[fake-meal:{json}]` sentinel in text + image bytes + nested-items greedy match), and the confirm-path deltas `MealApiIT` (+3 estimate/provenance), `MealServiceIT` (+2), `MealRepositoryIT` (+1). FE: `AiLogSheet.test.tsx`'s 7 cases (draft render, badges, `needsReview` hint, editable amount, confirm → `logMeal`, empty draft, error path) were **re-homed onto `pages/LogFlowPage.ai.test.tsx`** when the wizard merged into the unified flow (`mezo-d20.4.2`) — minus the manual-fallback case, which has no meaning once the manual path is on the same screen, 2 `fuelHooks` `draftMealFromAi` cases, and `shared/lib/resizeImage.test.ts` (3 `fitWithin` cases; the canvas `resizeImage` itself is DOM-only, proven in live smoke).
- **Day-type kcal shift (Diet Plan slice 3, `mezo-sxlj`).** FE: `features/fuel/logic/buildDayPlan.test.ts`'s `deriveDailyBudget day-type shift (slice 3)` describe (training/rest delta composition, the BMR floor, the static-path base-kcal swap, null-fields-mean-uniform, an `isTrainingDay === undefined` byte-identical-to-legacy regression guard, and a partial-split edge case — `trainingDayKcal` set/`restDayKcal` null on a rest day serves the uniform `kcal` unchanged) and `pages/FuelSettingsPage.test.tsx`'s `'edzőnap-shift stepper steps by 50 and saves the value'` (the 0–500/step-50 Finomhangolás stepper, `'ki'` at 0). Backend: `feature/meal/FuelDayDayTypeIT` (a training day serves `trainingDayKcal` + the serve-time carb delta, a rest day the negative delta, a pre-slice-3 segment with null day-type fields serves the uniform kcal unchanged, a partial split — one of the two day-type fields null — also serves the uniform kcal, an ad-hoc LOGGED sport session on an otherwise schedule-free day does NOT make it a training day, and a dated sport EVENT does) — see [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §8 for the engine-side split-math tests (`DayTypeShiftCalculatorTest`, `WeeklyScheduledActivityTrainingDaysIT`, `DietSettingsDayTypeShiftIT`, the `GoalProjectionServiceIT` additions).

Commands (from `frontend/`):
```bash
pnpm test                       # vitest — REAL mode default
VITE_USE_MOCK=true pnpm test    # mock mode — BOTH modes must be green
```
When Slice C lands, add MSW handlers so real-mode hook tests exercise `fuelApi`, and add backend ITs per the references above.

## 9. Decisions, gotchas & deferred

- **Stack 3.0 is a page family, not another all-in-one dashboard (`mezo-ubxd`).** `/fuel/stack` begins directly under the shell-owned `AppHeader` with the next-action card; it must not grow its own header, a decorative local weekday hero, or a duplicate whole-protocol list. The four hub tiles are read doors and the management tile is the explicit write boundary. Protocol mutations autosave, so a global `Mentés` control would be theater and is forbidden. Intake undo is intentionally bound to the created intake id captured after the log mutation resolves; looking up “the matching” pantry/zone row at undo time is race-prone when duplicate occurrences exist. Detail pages share `StackPageScaffold` and `.stk-*`, but keep distinct sage/gold/coral/lavender faces. See the approved [Stack redesign spec](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-04-fuel-stack-redesign-design.md).
- **HISTORICAL (the sky itself is retired — Mozaik 2.0, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)): the Mai sky SCROLLS — Today's "L0 nem görgethető" does not survive a hero above it (`mezo-gllr`).** The hub is a plain scrolling page with no viewport-fill flex container anywhere, so this bug class cannot recur; the lesson (a golden at 956 px hides what a 852 px phone clips) is why the Design 2.0 slices verified at real phone heights. Original finding: `.sky-islands`'s viewport-fill model (`flex: 1; min-height: 0` + `.isl.isl-big { flex: 1 1 100% }`) silently assumes the sky IS the whole page, as it is on Today. With the ~255px `KeretHero` above it, the leftover budget on a real phone falls **below the big island's own content height**, and because a shrink-to-fit flex container never overflows, the page could not scroll to the remainder either — `.isl { overflow: hidden }` simply clipped it. Measured on the mock demo day: **66px of the big island's content cut at a 852px viewport (iPhone 15 Pro), 118px at 800px, 0px at the goldens' 956px** — which is exactly why every golden stayed green while the shipped phone lost its `Logold`/`✨ AI` row. The fix is the **`sky-flow`** modifier (`FuelMaiPage` only; Today's plain `.sky-islands` is untouched): `flex-shrink: 0` with a content-sized basis on the sky and its islands, so the big island keeps `flex-grow: 1` (still fills spare room on a tall screen) but can never be squeezed under its content; the sky then genuinely overflows `.screen-content`, the app's one scroller. **Accepted trade:** the capsule↔big morph's height step no longer interpolates (a `flex-basis: auto` is not animatable) — the cross-fade and the border-radius morph still do. **The regression class is now covered by `frontend/tests/visual/layout.spec.ts`** — non-screenshot Playwright layout invariants at *phone-sized* viewports (393×852, 360×800), because neither other suite can see it: jsdom computes no layout, and the goldens' 440×956 viewport is taller than the phones people hold. It also asserts the inverse for Today (its sky must still NOT scroll), so the two models can't silently converge.
- **The keret-hero iteration retired the window-river's OWN belt a day later (mezo-c9t5, Task 4, [ADR 0024](../decisions/0024-fuel-keret-hero.md)) — a rare same-week double retirement.** `KeretBelt.tsx` + its test are deleted (zero remaining consumers, grep-verified), along with `shared/ui/Island.tsx`'s `belt: boolean`/`beltContent: ReactNode` props and the `'keret'` `IslandTone` value — `KeretBelt` was their only consumer. **CSS deleted whole:** the entire `.kbelt*` family (`-kv/-v/-vu/-l/-bars/-bar/-bar-track/-bar-t/-stackbar/-eaten/-left/-stackcap/-bd/-bdrow/-ic/-bdlabel/-val/-bdsep/-bdrow-total/-macrofull/-mf*`), `.isl.isl-belt`/`.isl.isl-big.isl-belt`/`:where(.isl.isl-belt)`, the `.isl[data-tone="keret"]` blob tint + its two nested override rules. `WindowIsland`'s `doneSummary` prop (+ its `.isl-doneline` render branch — the CSS class itself survives, still used by Today's `IslandMorning`/`IslandDay`/`IslandEvening`) goes the same way — it was always passed `null` by `FuelMaiPage`, dead weight since the belt-era done-summary line was superseded by `DoneWindowsCapsule` before ever shipping wired. New CSS, Fuel-scoped: `.khero*` (`KeretHero.tsx`) and `.kdone*` (`DoneWindowsCapsule.tsx`) — see [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md) §3.
- **The window-river recomposition retired five presentational components + orphaned CSS whole (mezo-jgh9, Task 6, [ADR 0023](../decisions/0023-fuel-window-river.md)).** `NowWindowCard`, `DayZoneCard`, `DayBudgetCard`, `MissedStrip` (all named in the design spec's §7 "Visszavonul a Mai-ról") and — a direct, mechanical, one-hop consequence of `DayZoneCard`'s retirement, since it was `ZoneSlotRow`'s only host — `ZoneSlotRow` itself, deleted component + test, verified zero-consumer by `grep -rn` before each deletion. **CSS families deleted whole:** `.nowcard*`, `.medcycle-micro*`, `.missedstrip*`, `.daybudget`/`.dayseg`/`.brk`/`.ebchip*` + the base `.macror`/`.mac*` bars (an older Napiv-S6-era family `DayBudgetCard` had inherited — §9 "Napiv S6 Mai recomposition" below), the standalone `.misstag`, `.coachline`/`.cskel`, and the `np-hero-pulse`/`np-seg-grow`/`np-sk-slide` keyframes those rules alone animated. **CSS families pruned, not deleted:** the shared `.zcard`/`.zrow` family survives — the former Stack zone surface rode the exact same base rules (`.zcard`/`.zh`/`.zn`/`.zk`/`.zrow`/`.zt`/`.a`/`.b`) — only the `DayZoneCard`/`ZoneSlotRow`-only nested rules were pruned (`donez`/`openz` zone-state tints, `.caps`/`.burn` stack-pip dots, `.zf`/`.zv`/`.zacts`+`.log`/`.ai`, `.missedrow`/`.act`(`.sport`)/`.anchor`, `.mm` macro dots) — verified by grepping each remaining consumer's `className` list before touching a shared selector. **A real, backend-integrated feature went dark, not deleted:** `MealScoreChip.tsx`/`MealScoreSheet.tsx`/`ScoreHero.tsx`/`useMealCoach` (+ its `.aiscore*` CSS) all still exist and pass their own tests — `MealScoreSheet` had **zero** opening path anywhere in the app the instant `ZoneSlotRow`'s `onOpenScore` went with it (confirmed against `main`: it was Mai-only even before this branch). This is flagged in ADR 0023 as a deliberate non-deletion — a page recomposition is not license to delete a P7-real, LLM-coached feature — pending a decision to either re-wire it into a future `WindowIsland` score fact or retire it formally. **Resolved the very next day:** `mezo-cs8b`/[ADR 0024](../decisions/0024-fuel-keret-hero.md)'s `DoneWindowsCapsule` (§2) re-wires `MealScoreChip`/`MealScoreSheet` back onto a real opening path — the score dark-period lasted exactly one day of shipped history.

- **The meal score is HYBRID since mezo-mr4n: a stable deterministic number + an LLM coach verdict.** The number, its 8 dimensions and their details stay exactly as the deterministic engine wrote them; the coach fills only the envelope's prose sockets — `summary`, `improve[]` and the new **`tagline`** (a ≤60-char card cut). Two lazy reads serve it: `GET /api/meal/coach?date=` (one BATCHED call for that day's still-verdictless meals, and it **only generates for today** — browsing history costs zero LLM calls) and `GET /api/meal/{id}/coach` (an explicit score-sheet open, which generates on any date). **Every prompt input is frozen at the meal's `loggedAt`** — the day state fed to the model is the totals *before that meal*, never "now" — which is what makes a verdict reproducible and therefore cacheable. The cache is the meal's own `breakdown` jsonb and **invalidation is free**: editing a meal re-runs `applyScore`, which rewrites the envelope with null prose, so a verdict can never outlive the numbers it explained. Degradation is silent by design (flag off · companion off · LLM throw · unparseable answer · unknown `mealId` · blank summary → nothing persisted, empty verdict list, 200 not 5xx), and self-heals on the next read. FE: the timeline card shows the tagline when present (`SlotCard`, no row otherwise), the `MealScoreSheet` fetches its own verdict with a skeleton. Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-llm-meal-coach-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-llm-meal-coach-design.md)**.

- **The meal score + recipe fit are REAL and deterministic since P7 (mezo-yta)** — config-driven math (`mezo.fuel.scoring.*`), never LLM, never fabricated: a dimension with zero input coverage degrades to weight 0 + „Nincs adat" and the emitted weights are RENORMALIZED over the live (weight>0) dimensions — `MealScoringService.Dim.renormalized(weightSum)` (the same helper `scoreMeal` and `recipeTemplateBreakdown` both call), so on the wire Σ(live weights) == 1.0 and Σ(weight·score) == the emitted `value`, the invariant `ScoreLedger` (§2) relies on to size its bar segments; a degraded dim keeps `weight 0` (never dropped from the array) so its "Nincs adat" line still has an id+label to render. **Since mezo-jcpt.1 the FE KEEPS degraded dimensions instead of dropping them** — `mealApi.fromDimension` now unconditionally retains any weight-0 known-id dimension (base fields only), so `fromBreakdown` needs no opt-in anymore and its old `keepDegraded` option is **gone** (both call sites, meal and recipe-template, now behave the same); `ScoreLedger` (§2) still excludes weight-0 dims from the bar/%-row, but `DimensionCard` renders each as a dashed "ghost" tile (`tone: 'ghost'`, `—` sub-score, „nincs adat, nem számít bele") instead of it vanishing from the sheet — the pre-jcpt.1 drop hid genuine coverage gaps as if every meal were fully scored. **Since mezo-jcpt.1 the macro dimension also scales its ratio-deviation penalty by the meal's kcal significance** — `significance = min(1, kcalShare / macro-significance-ref-share)` (config `mezo.fuel.scoring.macro-significance-ref-share`, default `0.25`) multiplies the deviation term in `macroDim`, so a meal under the reference kcal-share (a small snack) has its off-ratio penalty scaled down linearly instead of counting in full — `kcalShare` had been display-only on the wire (`MacroDetail.kcalShareOfDay`) until this change made it also feed the score itself. **Data-gap CLOSED (mezo-32ko, 2026-07-05):** the catalog now ships per-item NOVA classes (145/147 classified — 72×N1 · 6×N2 · 32×N3 · 35×N4; the 2 non-food rows stay null), `PantryCatalogLoader` seeds them AND additively backfills existing live rows (name-match, only where `nova IS NULL` — hand-set values survive), so the score's NOVA dimension + the P6 low-NOVA swap suggestion work on live data. The read-mapper's dishonest `null → 1` default is GONE — `IngredientResponse.nova` is nullable and an unclassified item renders no NOVA dot. Remaining gap: 0 vitamin `micros` (the Micro dimension still runs on the nutrition-quality facts). Catalog `priceUnit` stays deliberately empty — package prices across different pack sizes aren't comparable, so the cheaper-alt suggestion staying silent is honest (a price-normalization pass would be its own slice). **The URL-scrape import IS real LLM extraction since mezo-8vum** (the first LLM-backed feature endpoint — `PantryScrapeService` over the consumer-owned `ScrapeLlm` port; the mock-mode `MOCK_SCRAPE_DRAFT` remains the demo seed only, real mode calls the model), though the confidence + source derivation stay **deterministic** (`ScrapeDraftValidator` + `PantryScrapeService.sourceFor`), never LLM self-assessment. The REST of the "AI" is still theater: the Stack's `PlacementEngine` is deterministic-FIRST (a rule table + pantry timing hint decide almost every real placement; the LLM is only a last-resort fallback for a supplement neither recognizes — §4); improve-suggestions and replan cascades are pre-written prose/fixtures.
