Deeper intelligence (patterns, RAG, companion prose) is **🟣 Phase 3** (Spring AI / pgvector / RAG), not in scope for Slice C — now mapped: companion epic `mezo-fnnq` ([roadmap](../superpowers/plans/2026-07-03-companion-roadmap.md), [design spec](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-03-phase3-companion-chat-design.md)); the Fuel-side AI items stay under the P8 umbrella `mezo-0h6w`, layered on the companion slices (LLM port V0.1, embeddings V2.x, patterns V3.x). **A TÁROLT pre-jcpt.1 envelope-ok viszont megsértették ezt az invariánst (mezo-jcpt.2):** a `scoreMeal` csak a `d51ec268b` commitban kapta meg a `Dim.renormalized` hívást (a `recipeTemplateBreakdown` már előtte is renormalizált), így a korábban írt meal-envelope-okban a `dimensions[].weight` a NYERS config-súly, miközben a `value` már el volt osztva a súlyösszeggel — a `ScoreLedger` kliensoldali Σ-ja ezért látványosan széttartott a fejléc-pontszámtól ugyanabban a sheetben. A javítás egy **verzióbélyeg + egyszeri backfill**: `MealBreakdownJson.formulaVersion` (a jsonb-n belül, NEM a wire-en — `BreakdownDtoMapper` kihagyja) rögzíti a scorer formula-generációját (`MealScoringService.FORMULA_VERSION`), a bélyeg nélküli sorokat pedig a `MealRescoreRunner` (`@Profile("demodata")`, `@Order(210)`) pontozza újra a valódi write-path-on (`MealService.rescore`), étkezésenként külön tranzakcióban, idempotensen. **Vállalt következmények:** a re-score a nem-fagyasztott bemeneteket (a cél-előírásból származó `DailyTargets`, a mai gym-ütemtervből származó `MealRole`, a live pantry `category`, a jcpt.1-es `macro-significance-ref-share`) a MAI állapotukban olvassa, tehát a régi számok mozognak — ez a fix célja, nem mellékhatás; és az envelope próza-fészkei kiürülnek, betartva a coach-invariánst („egy elavult verdikt nem élheti túl a számokat, amiket magyarázott"), amit a coach a következő score-sheet-nyitáskor tölt újra. **Ráadás-gyógyulás:** a `mezo-g8qm` sorokban a tárolt `Instant` helyes UTC volt, csak az írás-idejű falióra-idő csúszott el, ezért a helyes instantból levezetett helyi idő most a helyes `MealRole`-t adja — az issue jegyzete szerint ezek addig csak újralogolással gyógyultak volna. A `weekly_score` cache-t egy egyszeri changeset üríti (a frissesség-próba `created_at`-et olvas, a re-score viszont UPDATE); a `day_review` az `inputsHash`-en keresztül magától invalidálódik. **Lokális idő tárolt `Instant`-ból:** a `rescore` a ház konvencióját követi ugyanerre az oszlopra — `ZoneId.systemDefault()`, ahogy `MealCoachService` is (NEM a `MealService.recipeLogs` `ZoneOffset.UTC`-je, és NEM az `app_user.timezone`, aminek ma nulla backend-fogyasztója van); a konvenció korlátja a SZERVER zónája, nem a felhasználóé.
- **The meal score is TRAINING-AWARE since mezo-ta8p — a role-rubric overlay with zero STANDARD regression.** At write a logged meal is classified into a `MealRole` (`feature/nutrition/service/MealRole.java`): **PRE_WORKOUT** when it falls in `[start − preLeadMin, start)` of any scheduled workout (plan-based, looks forward), **POST_WORKOUT** when it falls in `[end, end + postTrailMin]` of a workout that was actually **done** (the `done` gate), else **STANDARD**; on overlap the nearest window wins and a done-post beats an upcoming-pre tie (`MealScoringService.classifyRole`, `MealScoringService.java:182`; the windows come from the train slice — §5). The role overlays only the **three role-sensitive dimensions** — `scoreMeal(…,MealRole)` (`MealScoringService.java:85-102`) swaps in the role's macro targets (`p/c/f`), WHO free-sugar/salt limits, and NOVA class scores for pre/post, so **fast carbs count as fuel, not a penalty**; every other dimension + the weights stay role-independent, and **STANDARD is byte-for-byte the v0 rubric — zero regression** (the legacy `scoreMeal(slot,lines,time)` overload just delegates with `STANDARD`, `MealScoringService.java:75`). The role surfaces ONLY as a **„Szerep" row** in the `context` dimension (`contextDim`, `MealScoringService.java:448-450`; STANDARD adds no row), so the FE renders it generically — **no frontend change**. The **recipe/template surface stays context-free** — it has no logged time and no training window, so it is never *classified* — but **since mezo-uavr it is no longer role-BLIND**: `recipeTemplateBreakdown`/`recipeFit` gained a 3-arg form taking the recipe's own declared role (the 2-arg overloads delegate with `STANDARD`), and the overlay selection that used to be inlined in `scoreMeal` was extracted into one shared `MealScoringService.rubricFor(MealRole)` that both surfaces now call — see the next bullet. Config: `mezo.fuel.scoring.pre-lead-min`/`post-trail-min` + `roles.pre`/`roles.post` (`MealScoringProperties` `RoleRubric` — each role FULLY specified, no partial merge over the base). Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-training-aware-meal-scoring-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-training-aware-meal-scoring-design.md)**.
- **A recipe's meal role RETARGETS the rubric — it is NOT a bonus (mezo-uavr).** This is the counter-intuitive part and the reason the surface names the yardstick in the UI. Setting a recipe to `pre_workout` swaps in that role's macro target, which is **carb-heavy**; so a protein/fat-dominant recipe **legitimately scores LOWER** under `pre_workout` than it did as `standard`. Nothing is broken when that happens — the number is answering a different question („mennyire jó edzés előtti üzemanyag?" instead of „mennyire jó általános étkezés?"). The role is a yardstick, not a reward, and the only recipes it lifts are the ones actually built for the job. Pinned by a unit test rather than left to fold-lore, and mirrored in the prose prompt, which must therefore neither scold deliberate fast carbs nor imply that a training role by itself makes a recipe good. Practical consequences: `standard` is the identity overlay (**every pre-existing recipe keeps byte-for-byte its old fit numbers** — the regression guard); only `macro`/`who`/`nova` move, and **`Adag-arány` (portion) deliberately stays role-independent** (the role moves the *nutrient* rubric, not how big a serving should be — that is already keyed on the recipe's `category` budget share; a pre-workout snack expresses a smaller budget through `category: snack`). And **the recipe's role never leaks into the logged-meal score** — a logged meal keeps deriving its role from the actual training windows, because a pre-workout recipe eaten on a rest day was not a pre-workout meal. Deferred by design: no side-by-side all-three-roles reading, no role-based filtering, no auto-suggested role (a wrong guess would silently move the number), no manual re-evaluation button. Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-recipe-role-scoring-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-recipe-role-scoring-design.md)**.
- **Re-evaluation is visible, but only for the paths that actually invalidate (mezo-uavr).** `useRecipeBreakdown().refreshing` (`!mock && isFetching && !isPending && isInvalidated`) drives the „Mezo újraértékeli a receptet…" twinkle and suppresses the stale reading. **`isInvalidated` — not raw `isFetching` — is the load-bearing gate:** a `staleTime`-expiry or focus revalidation refetches the *same* envelope, so announcing a re-evaluation there would be a false claim plus a layout jump. The flag therefore covers exactly the **recipe-write** paths (a recipe edit; a role change is one), which `useRecipeActions` invalidates — **and only on the edited recipe's own `['recipeBreakdown', id]`:** a whole-prefix invalidation would light the banner on every other cached recipe detail and hide its perfectly valid score section, the same lie one level up. **The third cause — a PANTRY edit moving a recipe's numbers — is covered since mezo-b9gv**, and it is scoped twice over for the same honesty reason. `usePantryActions` invalidates `['recipes']` + the `['recipeBreakdown', id]` of **only the recipes whose lines reference the edited item** (`recipesUsingPantryItem`), and **only when a fact the scorer actually re-reads live changed** (`movesRecipeScores`, both in `frontend/src/data/fuel/pantryImpact.ts`). That second gate is not an optimization but a correctness rule: a saved recipe freezes its macro snapshot per line, so `RecipeService.fitLines` takes kcal/P/C/F from the **line**, not from the pantry row. **Since mezo-m6uv the four nutrition-quality facts (fiber/sugar/salt/saturated fat) are frozen per line too** and are read from `recipe_ingredient`'s own snapshot with the SAME factor as the macros — only **NOVA** and the **category** (plant-diversity input) are still re-read live (freezing NOVA is the sibling `mezo-4tzf`). Editing a price, a brand **or even the macros** therefore cannot move an existing recipe's number, and firing the banner for it would be the same false claim. ⚠️ The FE gate `movesRecipeScores` (`pantryImpact.ts`) still counts a fiber/sugar/salt/satFat/`per` edit as score-moving — since the freeze it no longer is, so that list is now over-eager (banner on a recipe nothing happened to) and needs narrowing to `category` + `nova` — tracked as **mezo-ax1m**. A **delete** always invalidates the referencing recipes (the source's facts drop out of every line that used it); an **add/import** touches no recipe cache (a brand-new id cannot be referenced yet). The Fuel query keys now live in one place (`frontend/src/data/fuel/queryKeys.ts`) because this cross-invalidation is mutual — a recipe write touches `['pantry']`, a pantry write touches `['recipes']`/`['recipeBreakdown']` — and a key imported from the neighbouring hook module would make the two circular.
- **AI meal logging (mezo-78rn) — the second LLM-backed endpoint, deterministic where it counts.** The LLM only parses + picks ids + amounts; **matched-line macros come from the DB row, never the model**, and `confidence`/`needsReview` are the deterministic `MealAiDraftValidator` (Atwater, boundary-**inclusive** `confidence <= threshold` — a >30%-off draft scores exactly the 0.6 threshold in IEEE-754 and must be reviewed; a demoted line always forces review). **Since the pantry-name-match slice (mezo-qrks) a third arm sits between the LLM's own catalog match and the estimate**: `PantryNameIndex` (`feature/meal/service`, unit-tested standalone, no Spring context) runs a strict deterministic normalized-name + unit match against the owner's pantry rows for every id-less line AND every demoted (hallucinated-id) line, before either falls to an estimate — so the full order is **LLM catalog-id match (ids copied from the prompt's PANTRY CATALOG) → `PantryNameIndex` name+unit match → LLM estimate**. A name-index hit is `source=pantry` with DB macros, `confidence=1` and **`needsReview=true`** — the macros are exact, only the line's identity stayed uncertain. Two rules a future reader will otherwise re-litigate: a normalized key claimed by two different pantry rows is dropped from the index entirely (an ambiguous key resolves to no match, because a wrong match would silently write wrong macros into the log), and the draft line's unit must equal the pantry row's serving unit after synonym canonicalization (`gramm`/`gr`→`g`, `milliliter`→`ml`, `darab`/`piece`→`db`) — a unit disagreement blocks the match. Matching is normalized FULL-name equality only (accent-/punctuation-stripped, brand+name and pack-size-stripped variants included) — no substring, no similarity score — and **recipes stay out of scope for name matching** (id-only, unchanged). `MealAiDraftService` now loads the owner's pantry list exactly once per call, serving the prompt catalog, the id lookup and the name index off the same read. **Estimate-line macro basis: `per = amount`** — the snapshot carries the whole-portion totals (the LLM estimates for the stated amount), so the read-time `factor = amount/per` is 1; NOT per-100g. **Switch-off is a 405, not a 404** (unlike the pantry-scrape switch-off): `/api/meal/ai-draft` collides with the `/api/meal/{id}` path-variable pattern, so with the `@ConditionalOnProperty` controller gone the surviving `POST /api/meal/{id}` route yields a 405 — a NEW techcore handler (`GlobalExceptionHandler.handleMethodNotAllowed`, `HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException` → clean `METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED` SystemMessage) replaces the old stack-trace-noisy 500. **Companion-off is a different axis → 503** (`ObjectProvider<MealDraftLlm>` empty). The DB widened **both** `ck_meal_item_source` (adds `estimate`) AND `ck_meal_item_arm` (its both-FKs-null estimate branch) — the arm CHECK previously forbade both FKs being null. `meal.provenance jsonb` records origin only; the photo is **ephemeral** (extraction only, never stored — the app has no binary storage). The cross-feature LLM edge is the **consumer-owned `MealDraftLlm` port** (ADR 0012, §5) — meal never imports companion. See the spec's [Implementation deviations](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-ai-meal-log-design.md#implementation-deviations-2026-07-18).
- **The override key is `lineOrder` + a `pantryItemId` consistency check (mezo-ormb).** `recipe_ingredient` has no unique `(recipe_id, pantry_item_id)` (`202606231400_mezo-lns_create_recipe.sql`) — a recipe may legitimately list the same pantry item twice — so `pantryItemId` alone is an ambiguous key, while `lineOrder` alone is positional and would silently land on the wrong ingredient if the recipe were reordered between the client's read and the write. `MealService.resolveOverrides` (`MealService.java:353`) therefore requires both: it resolves `lines.get(lineOrder)` and rejects the request if that line's `pantryItemId` differs, plus rejects an out-of-range/duplicate `lineOrder`, a negative `amount`, a null override element, or `ingredientOverrides` on a non-recipe arm — every anomaly is a 400 `VALIDATION_INVALID_VALUE` on `items`, never a silently mis-applied number.
- **All three derived values consume the same override map, or they would disagree with each other.** `MealService.buildItem` (`MealService.java:248`) resolves the map once and threads it through: the frozen macro snapshot via `RecipeMapper.rollupWithOverrides` (`RecipeMapper.java:141`), the frozen nutrient snapshot via `RecipeMapper.rollupNutrientsWithOverrides` (`RecipeMapper.java:170`), and `snapshotNova` via `MealService.dominantNova` (`MealService.java:401`) — recomputed over lines whose *effective* amount is `> 0`, rather than trusting the recipe's stored `novaDominant`, because zeroing out the single highest-NOVA ingredient would otherwise freeze a NOVA the meal never contained. **Since mezo-m6uv ([ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-freeze-nutrient-facts-per-line.md)) there is exactly ONE route to the four nutrition-quality numbers: the write-time freeze.** `MealService.toScoredLine` (`MealService.java:187`) reads fiber/sugar/salt/saturated fat straight off `meal_item`'s own snapshot, scaled by the SAME `amount/snapshotPer` factor as the macros — the old live-pantry `recipeFacts`/`pantryFacts` pair (which re-derived them at scoring time off the persisted override envelope) is **deleted**, so a pantry row that drifts after the log can no longer rewrite a logged meal's score. `hasMicroFacts` is true iff at least one of the four snapshots is non-null (a null is never read as 0 — coverage stays honest); only `category` is still a live pantry read, and a recipe-arm line keeps its honest `null` category because a composite has no single one.
- **One rounding formula, two places (the mezo-8xy discipline, extended by mezo-ormb) — and where the two places still diverge.** `RecipeMapper.contributionWithAmount` (`RecipeMapper.java:126`) is the ONE per-line formula (`round(snapshot × amount/snapshotPer)`, whole-number HALF_UP), and `rollupWithOverrides` (`:141`) sums it per line before dividing by servings **unrounded** — the frontend's `recipeMacros.computeRecipeMacrosWithOverrides` (`frontend/src/data/fuel/recipeMacros.ts:77`) mirrors the same round-per-line-then-sum order. The two are **not** fully symmetric, though: an **untouched** line reuses the server-frozen `contribution` the recipe response already carries (never re-derived from the live pantry row, which may have drifted since the recipe was saved), while an **overridden** line has no frozen rate for its new amount on the wire and is rescaled from the *live* pantry row — so if that pantry item's macros changed since the recipe was last saved, the sheet's live preview for that one overridden line can disagree with what the backend freezes. This residual gap is tracked as **mezo-10by** (expose the recipe line's frozen per-basis rate on `RecipeIngredientResponse` so the FE scales from it instead of the live row); the untouched-line path is exact today.
- **Pantry macro basis = per-100 g, dosed in grams (mezo-y9ga, 2026-07-23).** Every `pantry_item`'s macros (`kcal`/`protein_g`/…) are stored on a **per-100 g/ml** basis, and the recipe contribution is `factor = amount / serving_amount` (`RecipeService.buildLine` freezes `serving_amount → snapshot_per`; `RecipeMapper.contribution` / `RecipeService.fitLines`) — so **`serving_amount` MUST equal the macro basis (100); it is NOT a portion/serving size.** The imported catalog + several hand-added rows conflated the two (`serving_amount` set to a portion like `20` g while the macros stayed per-100 g), over-scaling recipe macros by `100/serving_amount` (olive oil per=20 → 5× → thousands of kcal; per>100 under-scaled). The fix normalized **live prod data AND `seed/pantry-catalog.json`** to `serving_amount=100`, and — per the owner's "everything is measured/dosed in grams" rule — `serving_unit='g'` on every row (`unit`/`snapshot_basis_unit` are display-only; the arithmetic uses only `amount`÷`serving_amount`). Existing frozen `recipe_ingredient.snapshot_per`≠100 rows were re-set to 100 too (snapshots don't self-heal until a re-save). **Exception:** a genuine per-serving row (macros already scaled to a non-100 basis, e.g. `Iso Whey Nutriversum Vanilla` per=30, kcal 112) is left untouched — spot it by its kcal being implausibly low for the food's true per-100 g energy density (the Atwater `4P+4C+9F≈kcal` check can't distinguish the basis, since both are self-consistent). Recurrence-prevention CLOSED (**mezo-0gjr**, 2026-07-23): the manual form's "Adag" field is gone — macros are entered on the fixed /100 g basis (section titles say so), create pins `per: 100, unit: 'g'`, and edit echoes the stored basis so the legacy per-serving row survives (spec: `2026-07-23-pantry-manual-per100-design.md`).
- **Photo import (mezo-d8tr) — the third pantry-import mode, deliberately reusing rather than duplicating.** `PantryPhotoService` reuses the URL-scrape slice's `ExtractedDraft` type + `ScrapeDraftValidator` (same deterministic, boundary-inclusive confidence math) rather than a parallel draft/validator pair — only the LLM port and prompt are new. The draft basis is **hard-set to per-100 g / grams server-side**, never trusting the model's stated basis (the mezo-y9ga structural invariant, §9 above — the prompt demands per-100 but the response overrides `per`/`unit` regardless of what comes back). The cross-feature LLM edge is the same **consumer-owned-port idiom** as scrape/AI-meal-log (ADR 0012): a new `PhotoExtractLlm` port + a new **multi-image `CompanionLlm` overload** (companion adapter `PantryPhotoLlmAdapter`). Two independent off-switches: `mezo.feature.pantry-photo.enabled` off → the controller bean vanishes → **404** (its own path, no route collision like the meal-ai-draft 405); companion off (`ObjectProvider<PhotoExtractLlm>` empty) → **503** `PANTRY_PHOTO_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`. The label photo(s) are **ephemeral** (multipart → memory → LLM call → dropped, never stored — the app has no binary storage). The two-photo case (label + front-of-pack) pushed the container's `server.tomcat.multipart.max-request-size` from **7 MB → 12 MB** (`application.yml`, `max-file-size: 6MB` per part) — the service-level `PantryPhotoProperties.maxPhotoBytes` (5 MB) check is the message-bearing 400 layer, the container cap is the safety net behind it.
- **Inert mutations (shrinking).** Replan apply still shows UI feedback (phase change / local state) but **never persists** — and since the X audit (mezo-t16y.4) it is **mock-only**: `useReplanScenarios` is honest-empty in real mode and the Mai Replan CTA hides with it (the replan engine is P8); **`"Mentés protokollként"` is deferred** (`· hamarosan`, disabled). **No longer inert:** (a) **Stack occurrence edits persist (mezo-09g, reworked mezo-vx9v)** — there is no `"Bekapcsolás · ma"` apply step anymore (retired Task 8, its backend endpoint retired Task 10): every tick-to-log/move/dose/add/remove writes straight through `useProtocolActions()`'s occurrence-CRUD mutation hooks (real `POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/fuel/protocol/items[/:id]`, mock cache mutators), and the Stack timing slots' **tap-to-log** intakes persist too (`useStackActions.logIntake`/`undoIntake` → `supplement_intake`). (b) Meal **logging** persists since mezo-arb — the `+ Mai étkezéshez` / `＋ Logolás` CTAs, the hub lane's per-window `Logold`/`Pótold` and its trailing out-of-window tile all open `LogFlowPage` → `useMealActions().logMeal` (real `POST /api/meal` + dual-cache invalidate, or the mock `['fuelDay']` cache mutate). (c) Recipe-detail Csillag/Szerkesztés/Törlés have persisted since mezo-lns. (d) **Import `"Polcra"` persists since Fuel P6 (mezo-bka)** — the confirmed OFF draft really lands as a `pantry_item` + `pantry_import` feed row (mock: cache-append).
- **Hardcoded literals (deliberate prototype fidelity — real mode CLEANED by the X audit, mezo-t16y.4).** kcal floor `2500` (`FuelMaiPage`) stays. The Stack page's `"conf 0.86"` protocol-confidence badge and the `"Mit nézek most"` context card (+ the load/medication-cycle/MAV narrative prefix, `useStackContext` returning nulls in real mode) were **both retired outright in mezo-vx9v Task 8** along with the rest of the pre-vx9v selection-model page — the reworked page has no confidence badge or context card at all; the `"Görög joghurt 10%"` import fixture lived on only as the mock-mode `pantryLookupFixture` (real mode did live OFF lookups since P6) — **that fixture, and the FE OFF-lookup mode itself, were retired `mezo-ymt4` 2026-09-02** (see the Kamra-import note in §2/§3). (`MedicationWeekStrip currentDay` derives from `useTodayScenario().medCycleDay` since mezo-d94; the week title `"Máj 18 – 24"` is mock-seed-only since P4 — real mode date-derives it.)
- **Mock stash carries the owner's REAL stim products (mezo-67rb).** The generic `supplementsStash` placeholders `aakg` / `betaalanin` / `caffeine200` (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`) were replaced by the two real items the backend `ProtocolSeedData` seeds — **`origin-pwo` „Origin PWO"** (pre-workout, `caffeine`) + **`tastydose` „Tasty Dose gombakávé"** (`timing morning`, `caffeine`). **Since mezo-vx9v placement is no longer a runtime FE needle-match** — the mock's `protocolOccurrences` array (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`) is a hand-placed seed that mirrors what the backend `PlacementRules.RULES` table would produce for the exact same items (both `kohi` and `tastydose` land at `wake` via the `kávé`/`koffein`/`caffeine` needle, `origin-pwo` at `pre_workout` with `restDayFallback: 'skip'` via the `pwo` needle, `whey` at `post_workout` with a `breakfast` rest-day fallback — §4). Any NEW occurrence added at runtime (mock `addItem`) goes through `mockPlaceOccurrence`/`resolvePlacement` (`stackHooks.ts`), which mirrors only the rule table's cheaper **timing-hint** stage (`zoneForTiming`) — the mock has no name-substring rule table or LLM stage, so an unrecognized new item's mock placement can differ from what the real backend's full engine would choose (an accepted, documented mock/real placement gap — projected timing/zone content is otherwise identical). `ing-aakg` lost its `stashRefId` (no matching stash item survives), dropping it from `kamraItems`' de-dup list (§3).
- **`recipes` is module-load-derived, not static** — couples `data/fuel/pantry.ts` → `data/fuel/fuel.ts` (`recentLogs`/`templateBreakdown` from `fuelDay.meals` + `recipeTemplateBreakdowns`, `pantry.ts:548-573`). The backend must reproduce this recipe↔log linkage server-side.
- **The Terv schedule is READ from Train (Fuel P4, mezo-kpo; read-only since mezo-4t43)** — real mode reads Train's derived gym week + sport schedule; `fuelWeek.ts` `gymSchedule` + `today.ts` `volleyballSessions` survive as mock seeds only. **Editing moved out of Fuel (mezo-4t43):** gym times are set in Train (the mesocycle planner Step 2 + the Gym "Időpontok" chip → `PUT /api/train/gym-schedule`); the Fuel `GymScheduleSheet` + `useFuelWeekActions` write-through were removed. `type`/`active` remain mesocycle-owned and `duration` has no DB home → `DEFAULT_BLOCK_MIN` renders the grid block.
- **Medication was designed FIRST-CLASS from the start (mezo-d94)** — a real `medication` + `medication_dose` aggregate, **NOT** a `pantry_item` row (the design spec's earlier "med in pantry" idea was superseded; see `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-26-fuel-medication-design.md`). The generic 7-day dose-kinetic cycle (D1-2 Peak → D3-5 Stable → D6-7 Trough) is **derived from the dose log** by `MedicationCycleService` (real) / the mock re-derivation in `medicationHooks.ts` — `cycleDay = days-since-newest-dose + 1`, clamped to `cycleLengthDays`; a dose today re-anchors `cycleDay` to 1. `cycleDay` **broadcasts** through `useTodayScenario().medCycleDay` (base = `useMedication().cycle.cycleDay` in both modes), the single FE point every medication-cycle surface (Fuel Terv's `MedicationWeekStrip`, and Mai's now-island subtitle fact — §2) reads. The `?medCycleDay=` URL override **remains** top-priority in both modes (demos/tests); since P4 the weekly-plan `medCycleWeek` cells also derive from the medication cycle in real mode (`toMedCycleCells(cycle.week)` — the `fuelWeek.ts` cells are the mock seed). **Since `mezo-lwmq` the domain carries no active medication at all** — see §2/§4 and [ADR 0027](../decisions/0027-retire-retatrutide-generic-medication-domain.md); the machinery above is unchanged and generic, only the data is gone.
- **`DAYS_HU` quirk** (`data/fuel/fuelWeek.ts:22`): `['H','K','Sz','Cs','P','Sz','V']` has a duplicate `'Sz'` (Szerda + Szombat) — index-based, matches the prototype.
- **Fixed plan · `now` paints STATE, never re-flows the timeline (mezo-1oy5, spec D4 — SUPERSEDES the mezo-53su now-reflow).** The old `reflowPendingWindows` pass is **deleted**: `placeWindows` sits each meal/snack window at its ANCHORED time (wake/training/bed span fractions + the single training snap) exactly once, and `buildDayPlan` step 6 only *classifies* each unlogged meal window against the injected `nowHHmm`. `nowWindow` = the latest unlogged window at/before `now` **while the day is still awake** (before bedtime); if `now` precedes every window the earliest is `now`; **past bedtime there is no `now` and every unlogged window is `missed`** (the gate is bedtime, not the 90-min kitchen cutoff — the last window stays `now` until sleep). Windows before `nowWindow` become `missed` (faded, still `Pótlás`-loggable, §2), after it `pending`. Block slots (`end ≤ now → done`) and protocol slots keep their own state — no global now-flag. This is precisely why the timeline no longer drags a breakfast forward onto `now` (the old reflow's 23:18-breakfast artifact): the plan is a genuine fixed schedule and breakfast simply stays on its wake anchor.
- **Dynamic budget: macro-split asymmetry + peri-snack thresholds (mezo-1oy5 / mezo-eujg).** `deriveDailyBudget`'s dynamic path (`target = max(BMR, BMR×neat + Σ MET activity + balance)`) splits macros deliberately: **protein is fixed** (segment/bodyweight-driven), **fat prefers the segment's own prescribed `fatG`** (Diet Plan slice 1, mezo-xwgb — the split preset's energy-share of the BASE segment kcal + the ISSN g/kg floor, so it stays stable on high-activity days), falling back to the legacy `FAT_KCAL_SHARE` share for a pre-slice segment, and **carbs absorb the whole activity bonus** (`carbs = round((target − 4·protein − 9·fat)/4)`, floored at 0). `balance = segment.dailyEnergyBalanceKcal` — the goal deficit/surplus read straight from the wire (mezo-eujg; no longer approximated as `segment.kcal − static TDEE`, whose static TDEE already folded in the day's scheduled training, so subtracting it double-counted training energy against the live MET term) — so adding activity back never double-counts. A **significant training block (≥ `PERI_SNACK_MIN_DURATION` 90 min OR ≥ `PERI_SNACK_MIN_KCAL` 300 kcal, MET×weight×hours)** earns a `Pre-workout snack` window in `placeWindows`, deduped against existing windows by the 90-min min-gap; the post side is covered by the existing post-workout main snap. Two concurrent blocks (gym + volleyball at 18:00) drive a SINGLE envelope snap — one post-main to the LATEST end (+45, weighted up), one pre-fuel to the EARLIEST start (−75) — so they can't each drag their own main and collide. No BMR (no resolved biometric profile — every current real-mode FE fixture) → the static segment path passes through unchanged.
- **Day-type kcal shift — serve-time carbs, no double counting (Diet Plan slice 3, `mezo-sxlj`).** Storing per-day-type carb/fat fields would be two more jsonb+contract fields wholly derivable from the kcal delta (protein and fat are constant across day types — ISSN, the delta is carbs by definition) — so **the day's carbs are derived at the two serve points, never persisted**: `carbsG + round((dayKcal − kcal) / 4)`, where `dayKcal` is the picked `trainingDayKcal`/`restDayKcal` and `kcal` the segment's uniform target. Backend: `FuelDayService.dayTypeAdjusted` computes this once and both `targetSet` (the FuelDay MacroHero) and `dailyTargets` (the meal scorer) apply the SAME pick — one classification, two projections, so the hero and the score can never judge a day against different numbers (the `segmentFor` precedent, §3). Frontend: `deriveDailyBudget`'s `carbs(target)` remainder absorbs the same delta on the dynamic path; the static (no-profile) path uses the day-type kcal as `baseKcal` directly. **The FE composition rule is additive, not double-counting:** the dynamic budget is `target = max(bmr, maintenance + eat + balance + dayTypeDelta)` where `eat` is TODAY's actual MET expenditure (already day-varying, the paragraph above) and `dayTypeDelta = isTrainingDay ? (trainingDayKcal − kcal) : (restDayKcal − kcal)` (0 when the fields are null) — `eat` answers *what did I burn*, `dayTypeDelta` answers *where do I prefer my calories*, and since the weekly Σ of `dayTypeDelta` is 0 (the engine's weekly-sum invariance, [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3), the weekly FE budget is unchanged. Worked example (mock numbers, `bmr 1720, neat 1.2 → maintenance 2064, balance −516, S=200, T=4, R=3`): a training day with a 60′ gym block at 78.6 kg has `eat = 6.0×78.6×1 = 472`, `delta = 2300−2150 = +150` → `target = 2064+472−516+150 = 2170`; a rest day has `eat = 0`, `delta = 1950−2150 = −200` → `2064+0−516−200 = 1348`, floored to `bmr = 1720` (the same BMR floor `deriveDailyBudget` already enforced pre-slice-3). **BE/FE day-classification agreement — full parity (final fix wave landed).** The backend classifies a date as a training day via `WorkoutWindowQueryService.hasScheduledTrainingOn(userId, date)` — **schedule-only**: a gym schedule slot whose weekday matches, a sport schedule slot likewise, a dated sport EVENT, or a prescribed run session in the active running block's week, deliberately EXCLUDING ad-hoc logged sessions. The frontend classifies via `resolveDayType(deriveBlocks(...))`, the identical schedule-only set (gym/sport slots by weekday + dated sport events + the active run's prescribed days) — matching the engine's own weekly training-day count too (`WeeklyScheduledActivityService.scheduledTrainingDayOfWeeks`, schedule-only, [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §3). The two now agree on every date, including the case that used to differ: an owner-logged unscheduled ad-hoc sport session no longer flips the day — it still serves `restDayKcal` on an otherwise schedule-free day (covered by `FuelDayDayTypeIT`). `WorkoutWindowQueryService.windowsFor` (which DOES count logged sessions) is untouched and stays reserved for its own concern, meal-role pre/post-workout classification (§5) — the two query methods now intentionally answer two different questions from the same repos.
- **Meal-slot templates overlay the planner (mezo-7102, PR 1 of 2 — spec [`2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-05-fuel-meal-slot-templates-design.md)) — additive, no template ⇒ byte-identical.** `buildDayPlan` step 1 (`features/fuel/logic/buildDayPlan.ts:337-340`) branches on the optional `DayPlanInput.template`: `input.template ? compileTemplate(...) : placeWindows(...)` for the windows, `input.template ? splitBudgetPct(...) : splitBudget(...)` for the per-window budget. Four new pure modules feed this, all under `features/fuel/logic/`: **`resolveDayType(blocks)`** (`resolveDayType.ts` — no blocks → `rest`; earliest block before noon → `training_am`; else `training_pm`); **`compileTemplate(template, {wake,bed,blocks})`** (`compileTemplate.ts` — resolves each row's `fixed`/`wake`/`bed`/`training_start`/`training_end` anchor to a clock-minute off the SAME wake/training/bed anchors `placeWindows` already uses; internally, resolution and clamping run on the UNWRAPPED continuous day axis (`daySpan`/`unwrapDayMinute`, mezo-7102 fix wave) so a midnight-crossing bed (e.g. wake 07:00/bed 00:30) never collapses `kitchenClose` negative, then the result is converted back to wall-clock minutes mod 1440 for the returned `PlannedWindow.time` — the same convention `placeWindows` and every downstream consumer (`dayZones.ts`, `FuelMaiPage.tsx`) already use; clamps into the `[wake+45, bed−90]` eating span, defensively drops a training-relative row on a blockless day — never a NaN/garbage time — then sorts + `MIN_SLOT_GAP_MIN`-forward-pushes, replaying `placeWindows`'s tail discipline rather than duplicating it. Its raw, unclamped anchor resolution is factored out into the exported **`resolveAnchorTimes(rows, ctx)`** — the single source of anchor resolution both `compileTemplate` and `validateSlotPlan` read off); **`splitBudgetPct(budget, windows)`** (`buildDayPlan.ts:259-281` — kcal straight by `budgetPct`; P/C/F by pct × `ROLE_MACRO_MULTIPLIERS[row.role]` then column-renormalized so Σ==daily budget exactly per macro; drift absorbed into the largest-pct window, the `splitBudget` dinner-absorbs idiom generalized to "biggest slot", since a template's biggest slot need not be dinner); and **`validateSlotPlan(rows, compiled, ctx)`** (`validateSlotPlan.ts` — Tier-1 deterministic guardrails run live by the editor: **errors** block save — Σ`budgetPct`≠100±1, <2 or >`MAX_TEMPLATE_SLOTS`(8) rows, a window outside wake→bed, a `training_start`/`training_end` anchor on a `rest` template; **warnings** only advise — a gap under `MIN_SLOT_GAP_MIN`, an oversized `pre_workout` slot, >`EVENING_SHARE_WARN`(40%) of budget in the day's last third, a slot past kitchen-close — mirrored server-side as the errors subset only, §4. It re-unwraps `compiled`'s wall-clock times before comparing them against `daySpan`'s wakeMin/bedMin/kitchenClose — the same axis convention `compileTemplate` documents. `ctx.rawTimes` (mezo-7102 fix wave, F2 — `resolveAnchorTimes`'s output, index-aligned with `rows`) is optional: when `FuelSlotsPage` supplies it, the out-of-span and past-kitchen-close checks evaluate the row's RAW authored anchor instead of the already-clamped `compiled` window, so an anchor placed far outside the eating span is a save-blocking error rather than something the clamp silently repairs first (spec §4); `gap`/`evening_heavy` always read the clamped `compiled` windows, since they describe the final layout). `useFuelTimeline` (`data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts:22,32,80,91-95,126,146`) is the one wiring point: it calls the new dual-mode `useSlotTemplates()` unconditionally, resolves today's `dayType` via `resolveDayType(blocks)`, looks up the matching cached template (absent ⇒ `null` ⇒ today unchanged), threads it into `buildDayPlan`, and additively returns `{ dayType, template }` alongside the existing `plan`/`budget`/… shape (so the `/fuel/slots` editor's own `useFuelTimeline()` call can read today's resolved type, `FuelSlotsPage.tsx:150`). **Entry point:** `FuelSettingsPage`'s **"Étkezési ablakok szerkesztése ›"** row navigates to the full-page `/fuel/slots` editor (§2). **Notifications are unaffected by construction, not by new code:** the `fuel_slot` notification writer (`data/notification/notificationScheduleWriter.ts`) derives its schedule from `projectStackDay` — the supplement/stack zone projection — never from `buildDayPlan`'s meal windows, so a custom template only ever changes the Mai timeline (the design spec's own §3 ride-along question resolves this way: Mai timeline yes, notifications N/A).
- **`getScoredMeal` matches a slot to its scored meal by `mealId` (and only when the meal carries a `breakdown`)** — `slot.mealId ? meals.find(m => m.id === slot.mealId && m.breakdown) : null` (`data/fuel/fuel.ts`); a title-only slot with no `mealId` resolves to null. The computed plan carries each done meal's `mealId`, so the id-join is exact in both modes.
- **Napiv S6 Mai recomposition (`mezo-8141`) — everything the old layout showed still shows, just relocated; no functionality was dropped.** **Superseded by the guided Mai redesign (`mezo-rrtj`, §2 has the current walkthrough)** — the bullets below stay for the historical deletions they explain (`MacroHero`, the old context strip, `PacingCard`'s eyebrow/tool-chips), which remain true; two of them were updated in place where their OWN file has since also been retired:
  - The old context-strip card's four `StatCell`s split in two: **gym/volleyball** were already redundantly surfaced by the timeline's own workout/sport slot cards, so dropping the strip's copy loses nothing (`plan.workout`/`plan.volleyball` are unchanged, schedule-derived in real mode); the old context strip showed an explicit "Volleyball off" cell per day, but post-relocation a no-volleyball day simply has no sport slot in the timeline (absence-as-signal). **coffee-cutoff/kitchen-close** moved into the gauge card's `.fuelchips`.
  - **`MacroHero.tsx` + `MacroHero.test.tsx` were DELETED in Napív S8** (`mezo-mifi`) — orphaned since S6 (`FuelMaiPage` unmounted it in favor of `KcalGauge`/`.fuelchips`/`.macror`/the water `.slot`), the S8 cleanup pass verified `FuelMaiPage` no longer imported it and removed both files. (`shared/ui/MacroRow.tsx` + its `fuelPrimitives.test.tsx` case were deleted in the same pass, closing `mezo-azmu` — zero consumers.)
  - **Water logging moved from `MacroHero`'s water row into a NEW dedicated `.slot`-styled block rendered directly in `FuelMaiPage`, below `FuelTimeline`** — it is visually consistent with the timeline's `.slot` vocabulary but is **not** one of `plan.slots`; it calls the same `useWaterActions().logWater(ml)` with the same `aria-label="Víz +{ml} ml"` `+250`/`+500` buttons as before, so the write path and test-facing contract are unchanged.
  - **`KcalGauge` itself was retired by the guided Mai redesign (`mezo-rrtj`), its successor `DayBudgetCard` (whose `.dayseg` bar carried the capped-fill/honest-percent split forward) was retired whole by the window-river recomposition (`mezo-jgh9`, §2/§9 above) into `KeretBelt`'s stacked eaten/remaining bar, and `KeretBelt` itself was in turn retired the very next day by the keret-hero iteration (`mezo-c9t5`, [ADR 0024](../decisions/0024-fuel-keret-hero.md)) — the fourth component in this lineage is `KeretHero`'s `.khero-dayseg` segmented day-bar (§2): one segment per done meal window (sharing the same `budget.kcal` denominator as the ghost remainder, so a window can't blow the bar past 100%) next to the honest, uncapped `{remaining} kcal maradt` hero number. The stacked-bar/eaten-vs-remaining framing itself didn't survive the fourth hop — `KeretHero`'s bar is per-window segments + a now-marker, not an eaten/remaining split — the "remaining kcal, always uncapped, always the headline number" contract is what actually persisted across all four components.**
  - **`PacingCard`'s old drug-referencing eyebrow and its `PACING_TOOLS` `ToolChipRow`** (`get_meal_pacing(d=7)`/`predictAppetite(d=3)`) were **hardcoded decorative chrome** — static consts, never fed by live data — so restyling the card down to just `✨` + the `SafeMarkdown` message (the shared `.aistrip`) dropped no real capability. **S8 (`mezo-mifi`)** then removed the residual dead `eyebrow` — from `PacingCard`'s prop type, the `FuelDay.pacing` shape in `types.ts`, and the `fuelDay` seed — so `pacing` is now just `{ msg }`.
  - **The wake-slot fav emoji doubling as the water glyph (`SlotCard → ZoneSlotRow`'s `FAV_EMOJI.wake`/`FAV_WASH.wake`, 💧 on `--wash-run`) is gone with `ZoneSlotRow` itself** (`mezo-jgh9`, §9 above — the napszak zones retired outright, no successor row). Water logging then moved to `KeretBelt`'s kibontott-view water `ItemRow`, and since the keret-hero iteration (`mezo-c9t5`) lives in **`KeretHero`'s víz ring** — tap opens `WaterLogSheet.tsx` (§2) — still exactly one water-logging affordance on Mai, just not this one and not the belt's `ItemRow` either.
- **`--text-on-media`/`--text-on-media-dim` are retired (Napiv S6 Task 6, `mezo-8141`)** — the recipe hero/list image bands (`RecipeCard.tsx`, `RecipeDetailPage.tsx`) no longer overlay title/meta text on the (still hardcoded-dark) media; that text moved onto the card surface below the band in ordinary theme-aware `var(--ink)`/`var(--faint)`. Both tokens are deleted from `prototype.css` (`grep -rn "text-on-media" frontend/src frontend/src/styles` → zero hits); see [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md) §3.
- **The Mozaik 2.0 recompose is the FIFTH render layer over the same unchanged model, and the first to dissolve the shell itself ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md) + [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)).** The lineage on this one tab reads: Napiv S6 gauge page → guided Mai (`mezo-rrtj`) → window-river islands (`mezo-jgh9`, ADR 0023) → keret-hero sky (`mezo-c9t5`, ADR 0024) → Mozaik hub (`mezo-d20.4.1`, ADR 0033). **Not one of those five touched the model.** `buildDayPlan`, `useFuelTimeline`, `deriveDailyBudget`, `projectStackDay`, the scoring engine, every contract and every migration are exactly what they were; each redesign replaced only the view layer plus one pure view-model module (`windowIslands.ts` → `fuelSwimlane.ts`). That is what makes the churn affordable, and it is the reason the F8 deletion list is long but shallow — components and CSS, never data. ADR 0033 supersedes [ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-today-ios-list-language.md) (the Napív iOS-list language); ADRs 0023/0024 stay linked as the superseded Fuel-specific layers.
- **DEFERRED — three modules survived F8 with no production consumer, deliberately not deleted and deliberately not faked.** (1) **`features/fuel/logic/windowIslands.ts`** — the whole `buildWindowRiver`/`doneGroup` view-model plus its table tests; the F8 pass removed the components (`WindowIsland`, `DoneWindowsCapsule`) but left the pure logic, and `fuelSwimlane.ts` cites its `fromPlan` rule as the precedent it ports. (2) **`keretHero.ts`'s `deriveMealRole` + `doneMealRows`** — the FE-only MealRole derivation and the done-row list that fed the capsule. (3) **`sheets/MealPickerSheet.tsx`** — the 2-tab Receptek/Kamra picker, replaced in the unified flow by `KamraPickSheet` + `ReceptPickSheet` because the source is now chosen *before* the picker opens. All three are dead code by the F8 definition and should be either resurrected by a surface that needs them or removed in a follow-up cleanup; leaving them documented as dead is the honest state, not an oversight. The same is true one level up for `frontend/src/shared/ui/Island.tsx`, whose last two renderers (`WindowIsland`, `TodayPage`) both went in F8.
- **DEFERRED — the Mozaik hub genuinely LOST two facts the keret-hero sky carried, and neither was re-derived.** (1) **Per-window stack doses.** The window island's L1 listed an `Ehhez az ablakhoz kötve` row for every `matchMealsToStack` verdict whose zone matched that window (deep-linking to `/fuel/stack`, since a `MealMatchVerdict` carries no occurrence id and so no honest in-place tick). A swimlane tile has no L1; `FuelMaiPage` still composes `useStackDay()` but only to count the day's remaining doses for the **Stack tile's line**, and `matchMealsToStack` is now a `FuelStackPage`-only composition. (2) **The per-meal `MealRole` tag and the day's `AI-átlag` line** the `DoneWindowsCapsule` showed; a done tile carries the score chip alone (`aiAverage` survives, feeding the Napló tile's line). Also gone with the islands: the medication **peak-phase appetite subtitle** (`medPeak`) — Mai's only remaining medication signal is the Gyógyszer tile's `D{n} · {phase}` line. Restoring any of these needs a designed surface, not a hurried re-insert (F7.3 Fuel deep rounds).
- **DEFERRED — `LogFlowPage` is a page by design but is NOT routed, and `provenance` still cannot describe a mixed meal.** The flow renders as a full-bleed self-portaled overlay into `.phone-screen` (the `LevelUpScreen` technique) rather than owning a URL, because `router.tsx` was outside the F3.2 slice's scope; the deviation is recorded in the file's own header and means the flow is not deep-linkable and does not survive a reload. Separately, `MealProvenanceInput` has **no `mixed` arm and no per-line origin**: a meal combining an AI-estimated line with manual Kamra/Recept lines is persisted as `ai-photo`/`ai-text` (whichever the AI actually contributed this session) with no way to say which lines were which. Both are flagged in the code, neither is papered over in the UI.
- **RESOLVED (F7.3, `mezo-d20.8.3.1`): the Fuel deep round landed.** `RecipeEditorPage` and `FuelSlotsPage` now ride `MozaikPage(sage)`, `RecipeDetailPage` traded its tabs for the mosaic (§2), the Gyógyszer lifecycle is real from the UI (§2), and the whole sheet family shifted generation in ONE place — `.sheet` carries the warm `--mz-sheet-bg` ground and the sheets' `.card` field containers the washed mz face (`prototype.css` F7.3 block), with the tinted `mz-sheet-hero` band only where there is something to headline (`ScoreHero`, `StackItemSheet` placement). `FuelStackPage`/`FuelRecipesPage` were already re-faced in F3.
- **RESOLVED (mezo-bq2t, 2026-09-01)** — a `/fuel/log` blokk-CTA-i most a saját `/fuel/log/uj` oldalra navigálnak a helyben nyíló composer helyett (§2); a helyben nyíló well, `WindowBlock` `open`/`children` propjai és a `.flog-composer` CSS törölve. Ezzel egy időben javítva egy picker-bug: a `.fkp-item` sor `overflow: hidden`-je nullázta a flex-elem auto min-height-jét, ezért a Kamra/Recept picker `max-height: 400px` görgethető listája sok találatnál minden sort ~20px-re lapított; a fix `flex: none` a soron (`prototype.css`), valós böngészős regresszióval fedve (`frontend/tests/visual/layout.spec.ts`) — jsdom nem számol layoutot, ezt a bugot nem tudta volna elkapni.
- **RESOLVED (mezo-1j3z, 2026-08-31)** — a `/fuel/log` nap-léptetőt kapott (max 7 nap vissza, `?d=` deep link); múltbeli napon a lane `asPastDayLane`-nel missed-re normalizálódik, a mentés `offsetIso(date, time)`-mal a választott napra íródik (`MealComposer.logDate/logTime`), a hub csali-chipet mutat a tegnapi pótolhatókról. Backend-változás nem kellett — a `MealRequest.loggedAt` mindig is elfogadta a múltat. Csak a `meals`/`intakes` dátum-igazak: a múltbeli nap WINDOW SET-je a mai `plan.slots`-ból (edzésblokkok/nap-típus/sablon) épül fel — nincs plan-történeti tár —, így egy eltérő nap-típusú múltbeli nap a mai ablak-elrendezést mutathatja.
- **Receptműhely (mezo-92pb) — the fifth LLM-backed Fuel endpoint, and the first that is genuinely stateless + patch-not-replace.** Every other AI surface in Fuel persists something at write time (a draft confirmed into `pantry_item`/`meal`, a breakdown cached on `recipe.breakdown`); the workshop turn persists **nothing** — the client is the sole state-holder, resending the full history + draft on every call, which is what lets a manual edit or an earlier turn's line survive a later turn unless the model itself rewrites it. **The save gate is stricter than the AI-draft precedents**: `MealAiDraftItem`/`PantryScrapeResult` estimate lines can be confirmed as-is (their FK-less snapshot is a legitimate persisted shape), but a workshop `estimate` line **cannot be saved at all** — `RecipeInput`/`recipe_ingredient` has no estimate arm, only a `pantryItemId` FK, so `draftToInput` gates on zero estimate lines remaining (§2/§4) rather than merely flagging them `needsReview`. **Goal presets double as both a canvas retarget and a chat instruction** — tapping `Pre-workout` doesn't just set local UI state, it sends `GOAL_MESSAGE[goal]` to the model in the same action, so the chat and the goal chip can never visibly disagree about what was asked. **The saved `role` is goal-driven only on an explicit tap** (`goal ? goalRole(goal) : baseMeta.role`, `RecipeWorkshopPage.tsx`) — an untouched `?recipeId=` session (no goal chip tapped this visit) preserves the seeded recipe's own role verbatim, the same wipe-class guard `recipeToInput` enforces for the plain editor (§2/§9 mezo-uavr) — a Receptműhely session must not be able to silently retarget a recipe's scoring rubric as a side effect of merely opening it.

## 10. Key files

- **Views** (`frontend/src/features/fuel/pages/`): `FuelMaiPage.tsx` (**the Fuel tab's Mozaik hub** — header recipe + `KeretHero` + Logolás hero tile + 6-tile mosaic + settings band; `mezo-d20.4.1` + `mezo-byo1`, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md); §2/§3), `FuelLogPage.tsx` (**/fuel/log — the stacked-window logging page**, `mezo-byo1`; since `mezo-bq2t` its block CTAs NAVIGATE to `FuelLogNewPage.tsx` instead of opening a composer in place; §2), `FuelLogNewPage.tsx` (**/fuel/log/uj — the logolás own page**, `mezo-bq2t`: the full-screen `MealComposer`, context in the URL (`d`/`w`/`ai`), sticky save bar, FAB hidden on this route; §2), `LogFlowPage.tsx` (**the unified meal-log flow's overlay wrapper** — Kamra/Recept/AI sources + line cards + totals live in `components/MealComposer.tsx` since `mezo-byo1`; replaces `LogMealSheet` + `AiLogSheet`, `mezo-d20.4.2`; §2), `FuelPlanPage.tsx` (weekly rhythm), `FuelStackPage.tsx` (next-action hub, mezo-ubxd) + `FuelStack{Protocol,Today,Meals,Manage,ManageProtocol,ManageTiming,ManageMeals}Page.tsx` + `FuelStackAddPage.tsx` (the eight focused Stack child routes), `FuelRecipesPage.tsx` (recipe library), `RecipeDetailPage.tsx` (`/fuel/recipes/:id`, also exports `recipeToInput`), `RecipeEditorPage.tsx` (create=edit, `/fuel/recipes/new` + `/:id/edit`), `FuelKamraPage.tsx` (pantry list) + `KamraItemDetailPage.tsx` (`/fuel/kamra/:id`), `FuelMedicationPage.tsx`, `FuelNaploPage.tsx` (the week-centric Napló sibling, F3.6), `FuelSettingsPage.tsx` (the routed Huawei/Mozaik settings editor; `mezo-2xzf`), `FuelSlotsPage.tsx` (meal-slot template editor), plus the `KamraSkeleton.tsx`/`RecipesSkeleton.tsx` loading branches.
- **Shell / nav:** there is no Fuel shell any more. **`features/fuel/pages/FuelSection.tsx` and `features/fuel/pages/tabs.ts` (`FUEL_TABS`) are DELETED** (F8, `mezo-d20.9.1`), as `FuelSubNav.tsx` was before them — the tab bar (`frontend/src/app/TabBar.tsx`), the floating quick-log FAB (`frontend/src/app/QuickLogFab.tsx` → `features/quickinput/sheets/QuickInputSheet.tsx`) and the flat route block in `frontend/src/app/router.tsx` are the whole navigation story. [ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md).
- **Sheets** (`features/fuel/sheets/`): `KamraPickSheet.tsx` + `ReceptPickSheet.tsx` (the log flow's two single-purpose pickers — Kamra stays open for multi-add and `✓`-marks added rows, Recept closes on pick; `mezo-d20.4.2`), `MealScoreSheet.tsx`, `WaterLogSheet.tsx`, `EnergyBreakdownSheet.tsx`, `IngredientPickerSheet.tsx` (nested modal from `RecipeEditorPage`), `StackItemSheet.tsx` (the still-live focused occurrence sheet; adding moved from a picker sheet to a routed page), `AddPantryItemSheet.tsx` / `ImportItemSheet.tsx` / `CategoryFilterSheet.tsx` (Kamra), `LogDoseSheet.tsx` (medication), `ReplanSheet.tsx` (still mock-only). **`LogMealSheet.tsx` and `AiLogSheet.tsx` are DELETED** (merged into `LogFlowPage`); `MealPickerSheet.tsx` survives with no consumer (§9).
- **Logic helpers:** `features/fuel/logic/fuelSwimlane.ts` (**NEW, mezo-d20.4.1** — pure `buildWindowLane({slots, budget, meals})` → `{tiles, nowKey}`, the hub swimlane's one view-model builder; §2/§3), `features/fuel/logic/keretHero.ts` (**mezo-c9t5** — pure `buildKeretHero` + `aiAverage` + **`asPastDayHero` (mezo-zeeq)**; its `deriveMealRole` and `doneMealRows` exports are consumer-less since the capsule's retirement, §9), **Logolás 2.1 (mezo-zeeq):** `features/fuel/logic/scoreTone.ts` (`toneOf(pct)` — the jó/közepes/gyenge ladder shared by the block pill, `MealScoreChip` and `ScoreHero`), `features/fuel/logic/mealContext.ts` (`mealContextOf(meal)` — Standard/Pre/Post from the scored `Szerep` context row, §2), `features/fuel/logic/formatImpact.ts` (`+0.04 score` → `+4 pont`), **mezo-tjua:** `features/fuel/logic/macroSplit.ts` (`macroSplit({p,c,f})` — a meal's OWN P/C/F energy split, whole percents summing to 100, `null` on a macro-less meal; consumed by `fuelSwimlane.ts`'s done tiles), `features/fuel/logic/defaultMealSlot.ts` (the wall-clock fallback the log flow uses only when no window seeded it), `features/fuel/logic/amountGuard.ts` (`parseAmountInput`/`stepAmount` — the typeable-amount contract), `features/fuel/logic/deriveMealName.ts` / `mealDisplayName.ts`, `features/fuel/logic/windowIslands.ts` (**mezo-jgh9/mezo-c9t5, now caller-less** — the retired window-river's `buildWindowRiver`/`doneGroup`, kept in the tree with its table tests, §9), `features/fuel/logic/dayZones.ts` (**mezo-rrtj** — `buildDayZones`/`slotRole`, still consumed by `compileTemplate`/`validateSlotPlan`/`heroWindow`/`buildDayPlan`/`fuelSwimlane`'s `isMealSlot`).
- **Components** (`features/fuel/components/`): **Mozaik hub (`mezo-d20.4.1`, §2/§3, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)) —** `FuelLogHeroTile.tsx` (the hub's one live coral logging door) + `WindowBlock.tsx` (one eating window as a stacked block on `/fuel/log`; `WindowLane.tsx`, the horizontal swimlane both replaced, is DELETED — mezo-byo1), `KeretHero.tsx` (the halo-sage hero band — kcal count-up + day-bar + signed energy chips + 5 rings; never an `Island`, which is why it transplanted unchanged). **Deleted in F8:** `WindowIsland.tsx`, `DoneWindowsCapsule.tsx`. `MealScoreChip.tsx` (the honest AI-score chip — renders nothing without a real `meal.score`). `MacroPanel/MicroPanel/NovaPanel/ContextPanel.tsx` (score dimensions), `DimensionCard.tsx` (**collapsible since mezo-zeeq** — ring-header button, the detail clamped until expanded), **`MealTimingStrip.tsx`** (**mezo-jcpt.3** — the `context` dimension's 0–24h `eatenAt`-vs-window bullet-graph, rendered from `DimensionCard` only when `dim.timing != null`; the tools list left it in mezo-zeeq), `ScoreHero.tsx` (**mezo-zeeq** — 112px tone-washed ring + tone word + fact chips + confidence), `KamraCard.tsx`, `RecipeCard.tsx` / `RecipeIngredientList.tsx` / `RecipeIngredientRow.tsx` / `RecipeLogsList.tsx` (**pending-aware** since mezo-arb), **`RecipeOverrideRow.tsx`** (**mezo-ormb** — the per-ingredient override row; unmoved by the redesign, now wired into `LogFlowPage` instead of the retired `LogMealSheet`), the shared recipe primitives `MacroCells.tsx` / `NutrientCells.tsx` / `RecipeFitBadge.tsx` / `ServingToggle.tsx`, `StackNextHero.tsx` / `StackRhythmPreview.tsx` / `StackPageScaffold.tsx` / `StackTimeline.tsx` / `StackManageCard.tsx` / `StackManageOccurrenceList.tsx` + still-live `StackMealMatch.tsx` / `StackDayArc.tsx` (Stack 3.0; the former all-in-one zone/next pair deleted), `SuggestionCard.tsx`, `PatternRow.tsx`, `WeekRhythmGrid.tsx`, `WeeklySupplementGrid.tsx`, `MedicationCycleBar.tsx` / `MedicationWeekStrip.tsx`, `SourceBadge.tsx`, `NovaDot.tsx`.

The file and its test are still in the tree (§9).

- **Dynamic day-plan · fixed timeline + activity-aware budget (FE-only, mezo-1oy5):** energy config `data/fuel/fuelConfig.ts` (`NEAT_BASELINE 1.2`, `MET_BY_KIND` {gym 6.0/sport 4.5/run 9.5/default 5.0}, `DEFAULT_RUN_MIN 45`, `FAT_KCAL_SHARE 0.275`, `PERI_SNACK_MIN_KCAL 300`/`PERI_SNACK_MIN_DURATION 90`, and — Diet Plan slice 1, mezo-xwgb — `DIET_SPLIT_PRESETS` {balanced 0.275/low_fat 0.20/low_carb 0.40/high_carb 0.22}, drift-guarded against backend `mezo.goal.diet` by `dietSplitDriftGuard.test.ts`; see [`goal-engine.md`](goal-engine.md) §4). Planner core `features/fuel/logic/buildDayPlan.ts` — `blockKcal`/`activityKcal` (MET burn), `deriveDailyBudget` gains the third `EnergyInputs {bmr,neat,weightKg,blocks}` arg (dynamic path: `BMR×neat` maintenance + MET activity + `segment.dailyEnergyBalanceKcal` balance, BMR-floored; protein fixed / fat from BASE / carbs absorb the bonus; static fallback when no BMR/neat — mezo-eujg reframed the arg from `{bmr,tdee}` to `{bmr,neat}` + read the explicit wire balance), `placeWindows` adds the peri-workout-snack window + a single collision-free training-envelope snap, `buildDayPlan` step 6 is the fixed-plan `missed/now/pending` classifier (now-reflow deleted) and step 7 the merge+sort — both run on the unwrapped wake→bed axis (`daySpan`/`unwrapDayMinute`, mezo-9rtw), so a midnight-wrapped slot from a meal-slot template (mezo-7102, e.g. a 01:00 "Late snack" on a wake-07:00/bed-03:00 day) classifies/sorts correctly instead of landing hours-early/first on the raw HH:mm axis, and `FuelPlanToday.energy {base,activity,balance,target}` is populated. Timeline `data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts` threads `bmr`/`neat` off `goalResponse.tdeeBootstrap` + `weightKg` off `goal.currentWeight`/`startWeightKg` + `blocks[]` into `deriveDailyBudget`, and **returns that `budget` (`DayBudget`) alongside `plan`** so the page can render one dynamic target across the whole gauge card (fix-wave). **Fix-wave (final whole-branch review):** `FuelMaiPage` fed the (since-retired) `KcalGauge`/macro-bars off ONE dynamic `budget`; that single-target rule passed through the guided-redesign's `DayBudgetCard.tsx`, then `KeretBelt.tsx` (both since retired), and now lives in `KeretHero.tsx` (`buildKeretHero`'s `budget` param — the remaining-kcal hero, the day-bar and the 5 rings all read the SAME `budget`; nothing gates off extra — `windowIslands`/`keretHero` render whatever `useFuelTimeline` composed, static-fallback included). The `isSuggestion = !isDone && !isMissed && !!slot.suggestedRecipeId` guard (originally `SlotCard`'s, carried into `ZoneSlotRow`, both since retired) has no direct successor in `WindowIsland` — a window's `state` (`done|now|missed|future`) already encodes the same distinction one level up, in `windowIslands.ts`'s `mapState`. Types `data/types.ts` (`FuelSlot.state` +`'missed'`; `FuelPlanToday.energy`). Mock seed realigned to the fixed-plan state (Reggeli+Ebéd logged, one `now` at 13:30). Tests: `logic/buildDayPlan.test.ts` (MET/budget/peri-snack/fixed-state), `data/fuel/timelineHooks.test.tsx` (one now-slot + dynamic energy `base 2064`). Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-fuel-dynamic-day-plan-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-fuel-dynamic-day-plan-design.md)** + plan **[`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-26-fuel-dynamic-day-plan.md`](../superpowers/plans/2026-07-26-fuel-dynamic-day-plan.md)**.
- **Energy-breakdown explanation + Profile Alap-TDEE restyle (FE-only, mezo-hobb):** the shared presentational `features/fuel/sheets/EnergyBreakdownSheet.tsx` (owns the `EnergyBreakdown`/`EnergySection`/`EnergyBlock` types) — an equation bar (`Alap + Mozgás [− Deficit] = cél`) over per-section structured **tile** rows: base = `BMR × NEAT = Alaphő` (exact), movement = per-activity pills (`🏋️ Gym · 60 perc · 430 kcal …`) summed, deficit = a **derivation** `Cél ütem → Napi deficit` (a "→" arrow, NOT `×7700÷7=`, because the engine's `projectedRateKgPerWk` tracks the real weight trend and need not reconcile with `dailyEnergyBalanceKcal` via kcalPerKg — 7700 stays a rough prose guide). The tapped section is highlighted (`.seg.hl`). Two pure adapters build the prop: `features/fuel/logic/buildEnergyBreakdown.ts` (Fuel — `plan.energy` + today's `blocks` via `blockKcal` + current segment; null on the static path) and `features/me/logic/buildTdeeBreakdown.ts` (Profile — `tdeeBootstrap`, **no deficit**, movement = weekly-avg `weeklyEatKcalPerDay`). `data/fuel/timelineHooks.ts` now also returns `blocks`/`weightKg`/`energyBreakdown` (built via `buildEnergyBreakdown`, reading the NEAT band off `useBiometricProfile().profile.activityLevel` + `ACTIVITY_SHORT`). Wiring history (three hops, Fuel-side opening path went dark then came back): the `"honnan a {target} kcal"` `.ebchip` row (originally `FuelMaiPage.tsx`'s inline `"Mai cél"` chips, mezo-rrtj; then `DayBudgetCard.tsx`'s section-opening chips) was retired with `DayBudgetCard` (`mezo-jgh9`) — `KeretBelt`'s kibontott view printed the SAME energy-breakdown rows inline (`.kbelt-bd`) instead of drill-in chips, so for the ADR-0023 window-river era `EnergyBreakdownSheet` was reachable ONLY from `features/me/components/BiometricCard.tsx`. **The keret-hero iteration (mezo-c9t5, ADR 0024) restored a Fuel-side opening path** — `KeretHero`'s three energy chips (`Alap`/`Mozgás`/`Cél`, §2) each call the page's `onChip(section)` → `setEnergyOpen(section)` → `EnergyBreakdownSheet` at that section, the exact `DayBudgetCard`-era wiring pattern, just off a new caller. So today `EnergyBreakdownSheet` has TWO real openers again: `KeretHero`'s chips (Fuel) and `BiometricCard.tsx`'s Alap-TDEE block (Me) — the Alap-TDEE block is restyled to Variant B (stacked `.tdee-split` rows + emphasized total, kept both `tdee tdee-split` classes) and is a `<button>` opening the same sheet with `initial='base'` (cross-feature import — the domain sheet lives in Fuel, `me` reuses it, keeping `shared/ui` domain-free). CSS: `.eqbar`/`.seg`/`.btiles`/`.btile`/`.tdee-split`/`.chip-tap` in `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css`. Tests: `EnergyBreakdownSheet.test.tsx`, `buildEnergyBreakdown.test.ts`, `buildTdeeBreakdown.test.ts`, `BiometricCard`'s tap-opens-sheet case, + `FuelMaiPage.test.tsx`'s own three-chips case (retired with `DayBudgetCard` mezo-jgh9, restored with `KeretHero` mezo-c9t5).
