- **URL-scrape import (backend-backed, mezo-8vum · the first LLM-backed endpoint):** FE `data/fuel/pantryApi.ts` (`scrape` + `fromScrapeResult`), `data/fuel/pantryHooks.ts` (`usePantryActions().scrapeItem` dual-mode — real `POST /api/pantry-import/scrape`, mock `MOCK_SCRAPE_DRAFT`), `data/fuel/pantry.ts` `MOCK_SCRAPE_DRAFT` seed, `data/types.ts` `PantryScrapeDraft` (+ `sourceUrl`/`confidence`/price on `PantryImportInput`), `data/pantrySources.ts` (`gymbeam.hu`/`web` + the 13-value lockstep), sheet `features/fuel/sheets/ImportItemSheet.tsx` (the Link segment of what is now a 3-segment wizard — see the photo-import bullet below). Backend `feature/pantry/`: `service/{WebPageClient,HtmlNutritionStripper,ScrapeExtractionService,ScrapeDraftValidator,PantryScrapeService,ScrapeLlm}`, `controller/PantryScrapeController` (`PantryScrapeApi`, `@ConditionalOnProperty`), `config/PantryScrapeProperties` (`mezo.pantry-scrape.*`); the companion-side adapter `feature/companion/llm/PantryScrapeLlmAdapter` (delegates to `CompanionLlm`, gated on the companion switch — **ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)**); `PantryImportService` extended (source-from-`sourceUrl`, price passthrough, `manual-review` on low confidence). Contract: the `PantryScrape` tag + shared `PantrySource` schema in `api/feature/pantry/pantry.yml`; migration `202607181100_mezo-8vum_pantry_scrape.sql`; jsoup is a new main-scope dependency. ITs `PantryScrapeApiIT` (WireMock product pages + `FakeCompanionLlm` `[fake-scrape:{json}]` sentinels) + `PantryScrapeDisabledApiIT` (switch-off 404) + `PantryScrapeLlmUnavailableApiIT` (companion-off 503) + `WebPageClientIT` + units `HtmlNutritionStripperTest`/`ScrapeDraftValidatorTest`; `ArchitectureTest` stays 11/11. Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-url-scrape-import-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-url-scrape-import-design.md)**.
- **Photo import (backend-backed, mezo-d8tr):** FE `data/fuel/pantryApi.ts` (`photoExtract`), `data/fuel/pantryHooks.ts` (`usePantryActions().photoExtract` dual-mode — real `POST /api/pantry-import/photo` multipart, mock `MOCK_PHOTO_DRAFT` after a 600ms demo delay), `data/fuel/pantry.ts` `MOCK_PHOTO_DRAFT` seed, `data/types.ts` `PantryImportInput.origin?: 'photo' | null`, `data/pantrySources.ts` (`photo` source, label „Fotó"), sheet `features/fuel/sheets/ImportItemSheet.tsx` (the `Keresés (OFF) | Link | Fotó` 3-segment wizard — Fotó reuses the Link-mode preview verbatim; `pickPhoto`/`extractPhotos` + a 5MB client pre-check; a re-extract-with-both-images affordance on low-confidence/nameless drafts). Backend `feature/pantry/`: `service/PhotoExtractLlm` port + `service/PantryPhotoService` + `controller/PantryPhotoController` (`PantryPhotoApi`, `@ConditionalOnProperty` on `mezo.feature.pantry-photo.enabled`), `config/PantryPhotoProperties` (`mezo.pantry-photo.*` — max-photo-bytes 5MB, allowed mime types, confidence-threshold); the companion-side adapter `feature/companion/llm/PantryPhotoLlmAdapter` delegates to a new **multi-image `CompanionLlm` overload** (ADR 0012 idiom); `PantryImportService` maps `origin=photo` → `source='photo'` on both `pantry_item` and the `pantry_import` feed row (Task 7). Contract: the `PantryPhoto` tag (`POST /api/pantry-import/photo`, multipart `photo` + optional `photo2`) + widened `PantrySource`/`PantryImportRequest.origin` in `api/feature/pantry/pantry.yml`; migration `202607231400_mezo-d8tr_pantry_photo_source.sql` (widens both `source` CHECKs with `'photo'`). ITs `PantryPhotoApiIT` + `PantryPhotoDisabledApiIT` (switch-off 404) + `PantryPhotoLlmUnavailableApiIT` (companion-off 503). Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import-design.md)**, plan **[`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import.md`](../superpowers/plans/2026-07-23-pantry-photo-import.md)**.
- **AI meal logging (backend-backed, mezo-78rn · the second LLM-backed endpoint):** FE surface **`features/fuel/pages/LogFlowPage.tsx`'s ✨ AI panel** since `mezo-d20.4.2` (`AiLogSheet.tsx`'s input→drafting→review wizard is deleted), `shared/lib/resizeImage.ts` (canvas downscale + pure `fitWithin`), entry points: the hub lane's per-window `✨ AI` action and its trailing `✨ AI napló` tile (`features/fuel/components/WindowLane.tsx` → `FuelMaiPage`'s `aiPanelOpenOnMount`). Data layer: `data/fuel/mealApi.ts` (`aiDraft` multipart + `fromAiDraftResponse`, discriminated `toRequest` estimate arm + `provenance`), `data/fuel/fuelHooks.ts` (`useMealActions().draftMealFromAi` — ephemeral, no cache), `data/_client/api.ts` (FormData-aware `apiFetch` — omits the JSON Content-Type for multipart), `data/fuel/pantry.ts` `MOCK_AI_MEAL_DRAFT`, types in `data/types.ts` (`MealItemInput` union, `MealProvenanceInput`, `MealAiDraft`/`MealAiDraftLine`, `MealItemSource` +`estimate`). Backend `feature/meal/`: `service/{MealAiDraftService,MealDraftLlm,MealAiDraftValidator,PantryNameIndex}` (the deterministic name+unit match arm, mezo-qrks), `controller/MealAiDraftController` (`MealAiLogApi`, `@ConditionalOnProperty`), `config/MealAiLogProperties` (`mezo.meal-ai-log.*` — max-photo-bytes 5MB, allowed-mime jpeg/png/webp, confidence-threshold 0.6, max-items 12), `entity/{MealProvenanceJson, MealEntity#provenance, MealItemEntity#source}`, `MealService` (estimate confirm arm + provenance persist + package-private `perServing`); the companion-side adapter `feature/companion/llm/MealDraftLlmAdapter` + the `CompanionLlm` multimodal overload (`GeminiCompanionLlm` `Media` / `FakeCompanionLlm` `[fake-meal:…]`); techcore `GlobalExceptionHandler.handleMethodNotAllowed` (405). Contract: the `MealAiLog` tag + widened `MealItemRequest`/`MealRequest`/`MealProvenance` in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml`; migrations `202607181500_mezo-78rn_meal_item_estimate.sql` + `202607181501_mezo-78rn_meal_provenance.sql`; messages `MEAL_AI_INPUT_REQUIRED`/`MEAL_AI_EXTRACT_FAILED`/`MEAL_AI_LLM_UNAVAILABLE`/`METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED`. ITs `MealAiDraftServiceIT`/`MealAiDraftApiIT`/`MealAiLlmUnavailableApiIT`/`MealAiDraftSwitchOffApiIT` + unit `MealAiDraftValidatorTest`/`PantryNameIndexTest`; multipart helpers `postMultipartForResponse`/`photoPart` on `ApiIntegrationTest`. Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-ai-meal-log-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-fuel-ai-meal-log-design.md)**, ADR **[0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)**.
- **Recipe ingredient overrides at log time (backend-backed, mezo-ormb):** FE `frontend/src/features/fuel/components/RecipeOverrideRow.tsx` (the presentational per-ingredient row — stepper + tap-to-type amount + `MÓD` chip + revert; `stepFor`/`parseAmount`/`formatAmount` helpers), wired — since `mezo-d20.9.1` — into `features/fuel/pages/LogFlowPage.tsx` instead of the retired `LogMealSheet` (the `HOZZÁVALÓK · N` disclosure, `DraftLine.overrides`, the delta-only `setOverride`, the stale-index drop on save, the `Alaphelyzet` reset — §2), `data/fuel/recipeMacros.ts` (`computeRecipeMacrosWithOverrides` — the FE half of the round-per-line-then-sum parity contract, §9), `data/fuel/mealApi.ts` (`toRequest` carries `ingredientOverrides` on the recipe arm; the response side round-trips through the contract but has no FE reader yet — no UI edits an already-logged meal, §7 of the design spec), `data/types.ts` (`MealIngredientOverrideInput`, `MealRefInputItem.ingredientOverrides`). Backend `feature/meal/`: `entity/MealItemRecipeOverrideJson.java` (the typed jsonb envelope record — `lineOrder`, `pantryItemId`, `name`, `unit`, `originalAmount`, `amount`), `entity/MealItemEntity#recipeOverrides`, `service/MealService` (`resolveOverrides`/`toOverrideEnvelope`/`dominantNova`, and the `rollupNutrientsWithOverrides` call that freezes the nutrient snapshot from the same map — §9; the `overrideMap`/`recipeFacts` scoring-time re-derivation was deleted by mezo-m6uv), `mapper/MealMapper` (`ingredientOverrides` response mapping). `feature/recipe/mapper/RecipeMapper` gains **`contributionWithAmount`** (the per-line formula generalized to take an explicit amount instead of the line's own) and **`rollupWithOverrides`** (the whole-recipe sum over a `lineOrder → amount` substitution map) — both reused by the meal-log path so the recipe rollup formula exists exactly once (§9). Contract: `MealIngredientOverrideRequest`/`MealIngredientOverrideResponse` + widened `MealItemRequest`/`MealItemResponse` in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml`; migration `202607301200_mezo-ormb_meal_item_recipe_overrides.sql` (additive, nullable `meal_item.recipe_overrides jsonb`, no constraint — validated in Java). ITs `MealItemRecipeOverridesIT`/`MealOverridesIT`/`MealOverridesServiceIT`/`MealOverridesScoringIT`, unit `RecipeMapperOverrideRollupTest`; FE `RecipeOverrideRow.test.tsx` + `pages/LogFlowPage.overrides.test.tsx` (re-homed from `LogMealSheet.overrides.test.tsx`). Known residual gap: **mezo-10by** (§9 — an overridden line's live preview can drift from the frozen backend value if the referenced pantry item's macros changed since the recipe was last saved). Design spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-recipe-ingredient-overrides-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-recipe-ingredient-overrides-design.md)**.
- **Nutrition-quality-fact freeze + display (mezo-m6uv — [ADR 0026](../decisions/0026-freeze-nutrient-facts-per-line.md), spec [`2026-08-11-recipe-meal-nutrient-freeze-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-11-recipe-meal-nutrient-freeze-design.md)):** FE `frontend/src/features/fuel/components/NutrientCells.tsx` (**NEW** — the `MacroCells` sibling for telített/cukor/rost/só, fixed order, `size='sm'|'md'`, `perLabel?`, `empty='hide'|'dashes'`; `frontend/src/shared/lib/grams.ts`'s `formatGram` for the HU-comma one-decimal display, `—` for null, `<0,1` for a positive value that would round to a misleading `0`), wired into `pages/RecipeDetailPage.tsx` (macro-hero row following the `ServingToggle` basis + a per-line `dashes` row in the Hozzávalók tab), `pages/LogFlowPage.tsx` (per-item row + the „Ez az étkezés" total row, override-aware — re-homed from the retired `LogMealSheet`), `sheets/ImportItemSheet.tsx` (one row in the shared preview card, covering all **three** modes — Keresés/OFF, Link, Fotó — since Link/Fotó already share one preview block), and `components/RecipeCard.tsx` (switched its whole-recipe `MacroCells` strip to the `/adag` basis, matching the detail hero and `MealPickerSheet` — no tápérték strip on the card itself). `data/types.ts` gained `Nutrients` (`fiberG`/`sugarG`/`saltG`/`saturatedFatG`, all `number | null`) on `Recipe`/`RecipeIngredientLine`/`FuelMeal`/`MealItem`; `data/fuel/recipeMacros.ts` gained `NO_NUTRIENTS`/`sumNutrients`/`lineNutrients`/`scaleNutrients` alongside the existing macro helpers, and `mealApi.ts`'s `fromResponse` maps `nutrients.fiberG` onto `FuelMeal.fiberG` (closing the Rost-ring gap ADR 0024 flagged — §2's `KeretHero` bullet). Backend: `feature/recipe/mapper/RecipeMapper` gained `lineFactor`/`nutrientsWithAmount`/`nutrients`/`rollupNutrientsWithOverrides`/`rollupNutrients` (three-decimal `scaledGram`, §4/§9 above), `feature/recipe/service/RecipeService.fitLines` reads the frozen snapshot instead of a live `pantryById` lookup (the separate live `factFactor` is gone), `feature/meal/service/MealService.toScoredLine` reads `meal_item`'s own snapshot (the deleted `Facts` record + `pantryFacts`/`recipeFacts`/`addFact` trio + the scoring-time `overrideMap` replay it needed are gone with it — §9). Two owned migrations, both nullable `ADD COLUMN` + best-effort backfill from today's live pantry values (honest approximation, not a historical reconstruction — the ADR's Consequences section has the full caveat): `backend/src/main/resources/db/changelog/1.0.0/script/202608111200_mezo-m6uv_recipe_ingredient_nutrient_snapshot.sql` and `…/202608111210_mezo-m6uv_meal_item_nutrient_snapshot.sql`. Contract: the shared `Nutrients` schema in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml`, cross-fragment `$ref`'d onto `RecipeResponse`/`RecipeIngredientResponse` in `api/feature/recipe/recipe.yml` (§4 above). Known follow-up: **mezo-ax1m** (§9 above — the FE `movesRecipeScores` gate over-invalidates); NOVA freezing stays out of scope (**mezo-4tzf**).
- **Backend (Meal scoring / nutrition core, present, mezo-yta):** `backend/.../feature/nutrition/{service,config,entity}` — `MealScoringService` (pure 8-dim engine since mezo-7797 over `ScoredLine` carriers — now +`category`/`amountG` for plant-diversity/energy-density — + the read-time `recipeFit`), `MealBreakdownJson` (the full typed jsonb envelope), `MealScoringProperties` (`mezo.fuel.scoring.*` — the 8-dim weights + NOVA-scores, fiber ref, WHO sugar/salt limits, fat-quality satFat refs, plant-diversity categories, energy-density band, portion default-share, slot-windows; **since mezo-8ms6 the macro deviation is ASYMMETRIC on protein**: a protein *surplus* is discounted by `macro-protein-surplus-penalty` — default `0.0` = overshoot fully forgiven, fitness-app policy; protein *deficit* and carb/fat deviations always count in full, and `1.0` restores the old symmetric total-variation penalty. The recipe-breakdown prose prompt mirrors this so the LLM never scolds extra protein) + `NutritionTargetsProperties` (moved here from feature/meal). **Training-aware since mezo-ta8p:** `MealRole` (STANDARD/PRE_WORKOUT/POST_WORKOUT — `MealRole.java`) + the pure static `MealScoringService.classifyRole` + the role-rubric overlay in `scoreMeal(…,MealRole)`; `MealScoringProperties` gains the `Roles`/`RoleRubric` records binding `mezo.fuel.scoring.pre-lead-min`/`post-trail-min`/`roles.pre`/`roles.post`; the day's workout windows come from the **train slice's `WorkoutWindowQueryService`** (`feature/train/service/WorkoutWindowQueryService.java` — schedule-slot start/derived-end/kind/`done`), mapped in by `MealService.applyScore` (§5/§9). Wire-through: `MealService.applyScore` (write — since mezo-ta8p also derives the `MealRole` from the train windows), `RecipeService.withFit` (read), `MealMapper.toScore` → the nutrition-owned **`BreakdownDtoMapper`** (relocated from MealMapper in mezo-bw3y so recipe reuses it without a recipe→meal edge; `recipeTemplateBreakdown` emits the full 8-dim template envelope — portion replaces the meal-side context, so the old weight-0 degraded-context card is gone (mezo-7797), `recipeFit` is its delegate); migration `202607051000_mezo-yta_meal_score_column.sql`; unit test `MealScoringServiceTest` (+ role classification & rubric overlay, mezo-ta8p) + `WorkoutWindowQueryServiceIT` (train, mezo-ta8p) + flipped `MealApiIT`/`MealServiceIT`/`RecipeApiIT` assertions. FE: `mealApi.fromBreakdown` (union + colors; degraded dims kept unconditionally since mezo-jcpt.1 — the old `keepDegraded` opt is gone). Spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-05-fuel-p7-meal-scoring-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-07-05-fuel-p7-meal-scoring-design.md)**, driving ADR **[0006](../decisions/0006-meal-score-jsonb-envelope.md)**.
- **Backend (Fuel day/week/timeline — all present):** `/api/fuel/day/{date}` + `/api/fuel/week/{start}` live in `api/feature/meal/meal.yml` (`FuelDayService`, mezo-arb + mezo-kpo); the timeline stayed a client-side composition (P5 decision — no endpoint). **Since mezo-gst9 `FuelDayResponse.targets`/`FuelDayRollup.targets` are goal-engine-driven, not pure config:** `FuelDayService.targetSet(userId, date)` looks up the owner's `active`-status goal (`GoalRepository.findByCreatedByAndStatusAndDeletedFalse`), resolves the ISO-week-from-`startDate` and reads that week's segment via `GoalPrescriptionJson.currentSegment` — a hit overrides `kcal`/`p` with the segment's `kcal`/`proteinG` (the goal engine already bakes in TDEE − deficit, weekly-stepped); since Diet Plan slice 1 (mezo-xwgb) a hit ALSO overrides `c`/`f` with the segment's prescribed `carbsG`/`fatG` when present (a pre-split segment carries null, falling back to `NutritionTargetsProperties` config); `water` is never goal-prescribed — it always comes from `DietPreferencesResolver` (the saved diet-preference row's `waterMl`, or its config ghost). No active goal, or no segment covering the date's week, ⇒ full config fallback for kcal/p/c/f (unchanged pre-mezo-gst9 behavior). This closes the gap with the FE's own independent goal-aware `deriveDailyBudget` (§5, `useFuelTimeline`, Fuel P5/mezo-9ys) — that client-side computation is unchanged and still the source for the Mai timeline budget; this backend change only affects the day/week MacroHero `targets` field. Master spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md)** (Slice C).
- **Receptműhely (backend-backed, mezo-92pb · the fifth LLM-backed endpoint, and the first stateless one):** FE page `frontend/src/features/fuel/pages/RecipeWorkshopPage.tsx` (§2 entry points, save gate, goal chips), components `frontend/src/features/fuel/components/workshop/{WorkshopChatDock,WorkshopIngredientRow,WorkshopMacroCard}.tsx`, pure state logic `frontend/src/data/fuel/workshopState.ts` (`draftTotals`/`lineMacros`/`scaleServings`/`diffLineKeys`/`lineKey`/`recipeToDraft`/`draftToInput`/`goalRole` — unit-tested network-free, `workshopState.test.ts`), wire boundary `frontend/src/data/fuel/workshopApi.ts` (domain↔`WorkshopTurnRequest`/`Response` mapping), hook `frontend/src/data/fuel/workshopHooks.ts` (`useWorkshop` — ephemeral, no cache, mirrors `draftMealFromAi`), mock `frontend/src/data/fuel/workshopMock.ts` (scripted pure rounds after a 600 ms demo delay). Router entry `frontend/src/app/router.tsx` (`fuel/recipes/muhely`); callers `FuelRecipesPage.tsx` (`✨ Műhely`), `RecipeDetailPage.tsx` (`✨ Iterálás a Műhelyben`, `?recipeId=`). Backend `feature/recipe/`: `controller/RecipeWorkshopController` (`RecipeWorkshopApi`, `@ConditionalOnProperty`), `service/{RecipeWorkshopService,RecipeWorkshopValidator,RecipeWorkshopLlm}`, `config/RecipeWorkshopProperties` (`mezo.recipe-workshop.*`); the companion-side adapter `feature/companion/llm/RecipeWorkshopLlmAdapter` (ADR [0012](../decisions/0012-consumer-owned-llm-ports.md)). Contract: the `RecipeWorkshop` tag (`POST /api/recipe/workshop/turn`) + `WorkshopChatMessage`/`WorkshopDraft`/`WorkshopDraftLine`/`WorkshopTurnRequest`/`WorkshopTurnResponse` schemas in `api/feature/recipe/recipe.yml`; no migration (nothing persisted by this endpoint). Messages `RECIPE_WORKSHOP_EXTRACT_FAILED` (502)/`RECIPE_WORKSHOP_LLM_UNAVAILABLE` (503); switch `FeaturesConfiguration.RECIPE_WORKSHOP_SWITCH` = `mezo.feature.recipe-workshop.enabled`. ITs `RecipeWorkshopApiIT`/`RecipeWorkshopSwitchOffApiIT`/`RecipeWorkshopLlmUnavailableApiIT` + unit `RecipeWorkshopValidatorTest`; FE `RecipeWorkshopPage.test.tsx`/`workshopHooks.test.tsx`/`workshopState.test.ts`. Design lineage: approved prototype `docs/design_2.0/prototypes/receptmuhely.html`, spec **[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-receptmuhely-design.md)**.
